mātur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā mātṛbāndhavāḥ ||
sabrahmacāriṇi trirātram | ekācāryopanītaḥ sabrahmacārī | svadeśarājamaraṇe tu dinamaraṇe dināntaram āśaucam | rātrau maraṇe rātryantam āśaucam | grāmamadhye śave sthite grāmasya tāvad āśaucam | kṣatriyādīnāṃ bhūrakṣārtham abhiṣiktānām āśaucaṃ nāsti | bhūrakṣārtham anyasādhye vyāpāre ‘bhicārādau purohitāder nāśaucam | svavihiteṣu bhūpāder anadhikāra eva | strīviprārthaṃ gavārthaṃ ca saṃgrāme ca vidyutā ca hatānāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ nāśaucam | yuddhakṣatena kālāntaramaraṇe grāmeśvare kulapatau mṛte caikāham | bhaginyāṃ saṃskṛtāyāṃ bhrātari saṃskṛte mitre jāmātari dauhitre bhāgineye śālake tatsute caiteṣv asavarṇeṣu maraṇe sadyaḥ snānena śuddhiḥ | svalpasaṃbandhe snānaṃ sacailam ityādi sarvavarṇasamam | svavarṇottamavarṇābhyāṃ naṣṭabhāryāmaraṇe bhartur ekāham āśaucam | jārasya trirātram | pratilomajānāṃ kṛtaprāyaścittānām āśaucādikaṃ kāryam eva | akṛtaprāyaścittānām āśaucaṃ nāsty eva | svairiṇyādyāśritasya trirātram | saṃnidhau pitus trirātram | jātāśaucamadhe tatsame tato nyūne vā jātāśauce sati tato nyūne vā mṛtāśauce saty api pūrvaśeṣeṇaiva śuddhiḥ | jātāśaucamadhye mṛtāśauce tena jātāśaucena tatsamaṃ na samāpyate | mṛtāśaucenaiva tatra śuddhir na jātāśaucena | mṛtāśaucamadhye tu tatsame tato nyūne vā jātāśauce mṛtāśauce vā sati pūrveṇaiva śuddhiḥ | janmamaraṇanimittasvalpakālāśaucamadhye dīrghakālāśauce dīrghakālā[93]śaucenaiva śuddhir na svalpakālāśaucena | mātṛmṛtāśaucamadhye pitṛmṛtāśauce sati pitrāśaucenaiva śuddhir na mātrāśaucena | pitṛmṛtāśaucamadhye mātṛmṛtāśauce sati pitrāśaucaṃ samāpyādhikaṃ pakṣiṇy āśaucaṃ kuryān na pitrāśaucenaiva śuddhiḥ | mṛtāśauce rātrimātrāvaśiṣṭe mṛtāśaucāntare sati pūrvāśaucaṃ samāpyānantaraṃ dvābhyāṃ rātribhyāṃ śuddhiḥ | tasyā rātreś caturthe yāme mṛtāśaucāntare sati paścāt trirātreṇaiva śuddhir na pūrvaśeṣeṇaiva śuddhiḥ | jātāśaucamadhye mṛtāśauce sati pretam uddiśya dahanodakapiṇḍadānādikaṃ kāryam eva | mṛtāśauce gate kāryam ity eke | mṛtāśaucayoḥ saṃnipāte pretakṛtyaṃ kāryam eva | jātāśaucayoḥ saṃnipāte jātakarmādikaṃ kāryam eva | tatra kartus tātkālikī śuddhir asty eva | strīpuruṣamaraṇa ekasmin dahanakāle prāpte savarṇānāṃ samānadharmāṇāṃ patnyā saha dahanaṃ kṛtvodakapiṇḍādikaṃ patipūrvaṃ pṛthak kāryam | sapiṇḍanaṃ tu patyuḥ paścān maraṇe ca patyuḥ pūrvaṃ kṛtvā patnyāḥ sāpiṇḍyaṃ kāryam | asavarṇānāṃ samānadharmāṇāṃ patnīnāṃ dahanādikaṃ sarvaṃ pṛthag eva kāryam | savarṇānāṃ samānadharmāṇāṃ patnīnāṃ ca saha dāhaḥ | udakapiṇḍādikaṃ jyeṣṭhapūrvaṃ pṛthag eva | asavarṇānām asamānadharmāṇāṃ sapatnīnāṃ dahanādikaṃ pṛthag eva | asamānadharmatāyāṃ sarvaṃ pṛthag eva | pitṛputrayoḥ samānadharmayoḥ kapālāgninā dāhyayor dāhaḥ sahaiva | udakadānādikaṃ pitṛpūrvaṃ pṛthag eva | bhrātṝṇāṃ samānadharmāṇāṃ kapālāgninā dāhyānāṃ saha dāhaḥ | udakādikaṃ jyeṣṭhapūrvaṃ pṛthag eva | asamānānāṃ sarvaṃ pṛthag eva | puṃbālānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ strībālānāṃ dahane khanane caivam | anvārohaṇaṃ sarvajātistrīṇāṃ pativratānāṃ praśastam ātmano bhartuś ca sarvapāpakṣayadaṃ narakottāraṇam anekasvargaphaladaṃ svargānta āyurārogyaiśvaryaputrādisarvasaṃpatpradaṃ duḥkhadāridryavaidhavyādināśakaram ityādi purāṇasmṛtiṣu śrūyate | tatrānvārohaṇaṃ garbhiṇyā niṣiddham | brāhmaṇyā garbhiṇyā brahmahatyāsamam | kṣatriyādijātigarbhiṇyāḥ kṣatriyādijātivadhasamam | savarṇayā patyā sahaikacityārohaṇaṃ kāryam | brāhmaṇyaikacityārohaṇam eva na pṛthakcityārohaṇaṃ kāryam | kṣatriyādistriyā bhartari deśāntaramṛte pṛthakcityārohaṇam eva | asavarṇayā pṛthakcityārohaṇaṃ kāryam | tac cānvārohaṇam evaṃ kāryam | anvārohaṇaṃ kariṣyāmīti saṃkalpya patiṃ namaskṛtya citim āruhya sarvaṃ prayogaṃ kārayet | yad vā saṃkalpya prayoge kṛte dahyamāne bhartari taṃ natvāgniṃ praviśet | citi[94]bhraṣṭā nārī mohād vicalitā ca prājāpatyaṃ caret | saha dahane tu śrāddhādau piṇḍapākaikyaṃ kālaikyaṃ kartraikyaṃ ca bhavati | sarvatra piṇḍadānaṃ yogyaḥ putro dadyāt | putrābhāve mitho dampatī sapatnyaś ca | tadabhāve bhrātṛputro bhrātā snuṣā duhitā dauhitro ‘nyaḥ sagotraḥ sapiṇḍaḥ sabrahmacāry api mitraṃ śiṣyo gurur bāndhavo dhanasaṃbandhī vā dadyāt |
na putrasya pitā dadyān nānujasya tathāgrajaḥ |
api snehavaśād dadyāt sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā ||
adattānāṃ kanyakānāṃ tu pitarau dattaḥ | anyābhāve dattakanyām api pitarau dattaḥ | dauhitramātāmahau parasparaṃ dattaḥ | mātāmahyāś ca dauhitraḥ | jāmātṛśvaśurau mithaḥ | śvaśrvādeḥ snuṣā | bhrātarau mitho dattaḥ | guruśiṣyau parasparam | uktakartrabhāve yaḥ kaścit | kurvataḥ sarvasaṃpatprāptir iti vijñāyate | ekādaśāhādau putro jyeṣṭhādir yogyaḥ kuryāt | navaśrāddhāni ṣoḍaśaśrāddhāni ca vibhakto vāvibhakto vaika eva kuryāt | sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tu vibhaktāḥ putrāḥ sadhanāś caikasminn eva dine svavṛddhikramāt pṛthak pṛthak kuryur nānyathā | ata ūrdhvaṃ pṛthag eva kuryuḥ | śūdrasyāmantrakaṃ sarvatra dvijavat kāryam | sarvatra gotrājñāne nāmnaiva kāryam | vrīhyādipiṇḍadravyābhāve phalena mūlena payasā śākena guḍena vā tilamiśreṇa kāryam | daśāhāntaṃ kṛṣṇāyasapāṇiḥ syāt | anāhitāgniṃ yajamānaṃ laukikāgninā dahet | patnyāḥ patiparokṣe kapālāgner apy anujñāsti | dahanakāle ‘gnināśe dagdhakāṣṭhe ‘gniṃ mathitvā dahet | laukikāgnau bhūr bhuvaḥ svāhety ājyāhutīr hutvā vā dahet | taddehaṃ mahyāṃ jale vā kṣipet | daśāhapretapiṇḍaṃ datvāsnātvā bhuktāv asapiṇḍasya trirātraṃ sapiṇḍasyopavāsaḥ | matyā dviguṇaṃ pretakṛtyaṃ kurvataḥ | saṃcayanād arvāk strīṇāṃ saṃgame cāndram ūrdhvaṃ kāryam | anyeṣāṃ pretāśaucināṃ pūrvaṃ trirātraṃ paścād upavāsaḥ | pretakāryam akṛtvā tad dravyaṃ haraṃs tadvarṇavadhaprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | deśāntaramaraṇe parākadvayam aṣṭau kṛcchrān kṛtvā dahet | dūradeśāntaragate jīvadvārtāṃ punaḥ paryālocya jīvadvārtāyām aśrūyamāṇāyāṃ pūrvavayaske viṃśatyabdād ūrdhvaṃ madhyamavayaske pañcadaśābdād ūrdhvaṃ cāndrāyaṇatrayaṃ triṃśatkṛcchrāṇi vā kṛtvā pālāśavṛntaiḥ kuśair vā pratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā [95] dāhāśaucādikaṃ piṇḍaśrāddhāni ca kāryāṇi | evaṃ kṛte ca punar āgataś ced ghṛtakuṇḍe nimajjya tasya jātakarmādisaṃskāraṃ kṛtvā pūrvapatnyā vivāhaḥ kāryaḥ |
atha śrāddhakramaḥ —
tatrādau navaśrāddhaṃ prathame ‘hni tṛtīye pañcame saptame navama ekādaśe ‘hni vihitam | tatra prathamam ekādaśe ‘hni utkṛṣyate na vā | utkarṣapakṣe tṛtīyāhādiṣu pañcāhargaṇasaṃjñaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ bhavati | anutkarṣapakṣe caikādaśāhikasya vikalpitatvāt prathamādiṣu pañcāhargaṇasaṃjñaṃ bhavati | yad vā prathamāhe tṛtīye pañcame saptamāhe caivaṃ tryahargaṇasaṃjñakaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ kāryam | aharyugmatvaṃ na karyam | etan navaśrāddham ekādaśe ‘hni vā kāryam | tathā ṣoḍaśaśrāddhāni navamiśrasaṃjñāni kāryāṇi | tāni pratimāsaṃ mṛtāha āsaṃvatsaraṃ vihitāni dvādaśadvādaśāhe tripakṣe nyūnaṣaṇmāse nyūnābde catvāri | evaṃ ṣoḍaśa | tatrādyam ekādaśe ‘hni utkṛṣya vidhīyate | tatra kramaḥ – ekādaśāhe dvādaśāhe dvitīyamāse tripakṣe tṛtīye caturthe pañcame ṣaṣṭhe māse nyūnaṣaṣṭhe saptame ‘ṣṭame navame daśama ekādaśe dvādaśe māse nyūnābde ceti | tato ‘paredyuḥ saṃvatsarānte sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | tataḥ paredyur dvitīyasaṃvatsarādāv ābdikam | tṛtīyasaṃvatsarādau pratyābdikam iti kramaḥ | yad vā piṇḍapitṛyajñapārvaṇaśrāddhārtham agnimān daśāhād ūrdhvaṃ dvādaśāhe tripakṣe vā vṛddhiśrāddha upasthite vāyuṣyasyāniyatatvāc cārvāk saṃvatsarāt sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt | tac ca ṣoḍaśaikoddiṣṭāni kṛtvaiva kāryam | tatraikādaśe ‘hni catvāri śrāddhāni saṃbhavanti | navaśrāddhādyaṃ navaśrāddhāntyam ādyamāsikaṃ svatantraikoddiṣṭaṃ ceti | svatantraikoddiṣṭe kriyamāṇe ‘nyeṣāṃ tantrānuṣṭhānāt siddhir icchatām asti ata ādyaikoddiṣṭaṃ navasu miśram ity ucyate | navaśrāddhadharmakaṃ navamiśradharmakam ubhayadoṣakaṃ bhavati | tato ‘vaśiṣṭaṃ navaśrāddhaṃ tato ‘vaśiṣṭaṃ māsikaṃ ca | pṛthaganuṣṭhānapakṣe cādau svatantraikoddiṣṭaṃ kāryam | tac cobhayadoṣam eva | tato navaśrāddhaṃ tato māsikam ekādaśe ‘hany ekādaśabrāhmaṇabhojanaṃ pretoddeśenaikādaśarudroddeśena vā | ekādaśarudroddeśād rudragaṇam ity āhuḥ | vṛṣotsargaś ca praśasyate | dvādaśāhasthāne nyūnamāsikaṃ kṣātrā[96]diṣu bhavati | saṃcayane kṛte manuṣyalokāt pretalokaṃ gacchata āmena pātheyaśrāddham ekoddiṣṭavidhinā kāryam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇe kṛte pretalokāt pitṛlokaṃ gacchata āmena pātheyaśrāddhaṃ pārvaṇavidhinā kāryam | tataḥ puṇyāhavācanaṃ kāryam | saṃvatsarād arvāk sapiṇḍīkaraṇe ‘numāsikāni pṛthak kuryād ekoddiṣṭavat pārvaṇavad vā mṛtāhaśrāddhavat | anumāsikānte saṃvatsaravimokāntaṃ śrāddhaṃ puṇyāhavācanaṃ ca kāryam it kramaḥ |
atha saṃnyāsavidhiḥ —
saṃnyāsasya vidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ pravakṣyāmy anupūrvaśaḥ |
govindarājasya mataṃ baudhāyanamataṃ yathā ||
ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kuryāc chrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa |
agnimān pārvaṇenaiva vidhinā nirvapet savyam ||
kṛcchrāṃs tu caturaḥ kṛtvā pāvanārtham anāśṛamī |
āśramī cet taptakṛcchraṃ tenāsau yogyatāṃ vrajet ||
daivikaṃ cārṣikaṃ divyaṃ pitryaṃ mātṛkamānuṣe |
bhautikaṃ cātmanaś cānte tv aṣṭau śrāddhāni nirvapet ||
keśaśmaśrulomanakhaṃ vāpayitvopakalpayet |
yaṣṭiṃ jalapavitraṃ kuryān nityena vidhinā tataḥ ||
anagnimān dvijaḥ kuryān nityena vidhinā tataḥ |
svāgnāv evāgnimān kuryād apavargāntam ādṛtaḥ ||
grāmānte grāmasīmānte agnyāgāre surālaye |
ājyaṃ payo dadhīty etat trivṛt prāśyopaveśayet ||
abhāve apo vā | oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi oṃ tat savitur vareṇyaṃ oṃ bhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi oṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi oṃ svaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi oṃ dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt | iti paccho ‘rdharcaśaḥ samastayā vā | oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi oṃ tat savitur vareṇyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi | dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt | ity ātmānam ātmanāśramād āśramam anunīya brahmībhūto bhavatīti vijñāyate | tat tathāgnim upasamādhāya samitpūrvām oṃ svāheti pūrṇāhutiṃ juhuyāt | tena brahmānvādhānam iti vijñāyate | anāhitāgner iṣṭisthāne vaiśvānaraś caruḥ
[97]        anagneś cāgnivarjaṃ tu brahmacāriṇa iṣyate |
tathāgnīn samāropya gurave sarvasvaṃ datvā oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ saṃnyastaṃ mayā iti trir upāṃśu trir uccais triṃśatyā hi devā iti vijñāyate | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
pañcāyudhadharaṃ saumyaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam |
dhyātvā hṛdi tv anujñāto guruṇā praiṣam īrayet ||
tato ‘manta iti vāpāṃ pūrṇam añjalim ninayati | sakhe māṃ gopāyeti daṇḍaṃ gṛhṇāti | padasya pāre rajasa iti śikyam | yena devāḥ pavitreṇeti jalapavitram | sāvitryā kamaṇḍalum | sarvābhir vyāhṛtibhir brahmabhājanam | tata ud u tyaṃ citraṃ tac cakṣur haṃsaḥ śuciṣan namo mitrasyeti |
namaḥ savitre jagadekacakṣuṣe
jagatprasūtisthitināśahetave |
trayīmayāya triguṇātmadhāriṇe
virañcinārāyaṇaśaṃkarātmane ||
ity ādityam upatiṣṭhate | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
kaṭiprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā praṇavena mṛdā bahiḥ |
tataḥ snānaṃ prakurvīta mantravat tu jalāśaye ||
tisṛṣv api ca saṃdhyāsv añjalīn pradāya oṃ bhūs tarpayāmi, oṃ bhuvas tarpayāmi, oṃ svas tarpayāmi, oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svas tarpayāmi, iti tarpaṇaṃ kuryāt | athodite bhānau oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity añjaliṃ pradāya ud u tyaṃ citraṃ tac cakṣur haṃsaḥ śuciṣad idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrame tredhā tridivo brahma jajñānam ity ādityam upatiṣṭhate | sarvabhūtebhyo nama ity ātmānaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskuryāt | oṃ ādityāya vidmahe sahasrākṣāya dhīmahi | tan naḥ sūryaḥ pracodayāt, iti trir japet trikālam | atha bhikṣiṣyann ud vayaṃ tamasas parīti catasṛbhir ādityam upasthāya maunī bhūtvā pañcāgāram saptāgāraṃ vā praviśet | tataḥ pūrṇam asi pūrṇaṃ me bhūyād iti japitvā punar āvartate | śucau deśe śucir bhūtvā caturaḥ piṇḍān nirvapet | oṃ bhūḥ svadhā namaḥ, oṃ bhuvaḥ svadhā namaḥ, oṃ svaḥ svadhā namaḥ, iti sārvabhautikaṃ caturtham | śeṣaṃ bhuktvā prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret | brahmabhūyāya kalpate, brahmabhūyāya kalpata ity āha bhagavān baudhāyanaḥ | [cf. BDh 2.17.13–41]
iti smṛtyarthasāre saṃnyāsavidhiḥ |
[98]
atha parivrājakasya saṃskāravidhiḥ —
grāmāt prācīm udīcīṃ vā gatvā brahmavṛkṣasyādhastān nadītīre vā śucau deśe vā daṇḍapramāṇaṃ devayajanaṃ vyāhṛtibhiḥ khātvā trisaptavyāhṛtibhiḥ prokṣya darbhān saṃstīryālaṃkṛtya śavaṃ nidadhāti | śvabhre viṣṇo havyaṃ rakṣasveti | idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrama iti dakṣiṇahaste daṇḍaṃ nidadhāti | yad asya pāre rajasa iti savyahaste śikyam | yena devāḥ pavitreṇeti mukhe jalapavitram | sāvitryā udare brahmabhājanam | bhūmir bhūmneti guhye kamaṇḍalum | cittiḥ srug iti daśahotribhir anumantrayate | nātra śeṣasaṃskārāḥ pūrvam anuṣṭhitatvāt |
sarvasaṅganivṛttasya dhyānayogaratasya ca |
na tasya dahanaṃ kāryaṃ nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā ||
atha saṃnyāsināṃ dahanavidhiḥ —
bhūmibhāge khāte –
saṃnikṛṣṭais tu saṃnyaste pitary uparate sutaiḥ |
dahanaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ yac cānyac cheṣasaṃjñitam ||
tasya samīpe ‘gniṃ prajvālya yater dakṣiṇahaste upāvarohety avarohya tasyāgniṃ putrāḥ pitṛvidhānataḥ, agnihotravidhānena sāvitryā praṇavena vā dahet | yatīn dahan spṛśan vāpi jalāvagāhanād eva sadyaḥ śudhyet | prāpnoty aśvamedhaphalam |
ahany ekādaśe prāpte pārvaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ na kurvīta tridaṇḍānāṃ kadācana ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ na kartavyaṃ sutena tu |
tridaṇḍadhāraṇāt praityaṃ naṣṭam āhośanā muniḥ ||
kuṭīcaraṇ saṃpradahet pūrayec ca bahūdakam |
haṃso jale vinikṣepyaḥ parahaṃsaṃ prapūrayet ||
ity āha bhagavān baudhāyanaḥ |
iti smṛtyarthasāre ‘śaucaprakaraṇam
kāmadhenau pradīpte ‘bdhau kalpavṛkṣalatādiṣu |
śaṃbhudraviḍakedāralolaṭādyaiś ca bhāṣitam ||
[99]        manvatriyājñavalkyādivyākhyātṛpratipāditam |
smṛtyarthasāraṃ vakṣyāmi sukhānuṣṭhānasiddhaye ||
atha prāyaścittāny ucyante —
tatra pāpānām acīrṇaprāyaścittānāṃ narakabhogānte karmavipākā ucyante | ajñānād brahmahā kramāt sṛgālaśvasūkaroṣṭrāṇāṃ yoniṃ yāti | surāpaḥ kharapulkasavenānāṃ nāgapakṣicāṇḍālapulkasānāṃ yonim, suvarṇasteyī viḍvarāhakṛmikīṭapataṅgatvam | gurutalpagas tṛṇagulmalatātvaṃ kramād yāti | jñānād brahmahā śvasūkarakharoṣṭrago’jāvimṛgapakṣicaṇḍāla-pulkasānāṃ yoniṃ yāti, surāpo viṭkrimipataṅgapakṣihiṃsrasattvānām | suvarṇasteyī ūrṇanābhi-kṛkalāsatiraścām ambucāriṇāṃ hiṃsrāṇāṃ piśācānāṃ ca sahasraśo yoniṃ yāti | gurutalpagas tṛṇagulmalatānāṃ kravyādānāṃ krūrakarmaṇāṃ ca śataśo yoniṃ yāti | tatsaṃsargī ca tadvat | tato manuṣyajanmani brahmahā kṣayarogī, surāpo nijakṛṣṇadantaḥ, suvarṇasteyī kunakhī, gurutalpagaḥ kuṣṭhī | tatsaṃyogī ca tadvat | parastriyaṃ dravyaṃ ca hṛtvāraṇye nirjale deśe brahmarākṣasaḥ syāt | anadhyāye ‘dhyāyī sṛgālo bahupratigrāhī ca | annaṃ ratnaṃ vā hṛtvājīrṇavyādhir atyantadaridro vā | jñānavedavācakapustakahārī mūkaḥ, patraśākaṃ mayūraḥ, ratnāni hemakārī pakṣy atyantadaridro vā, nyāsam anapatyo daridro vā, śubhagandhāñ chuchundarī, chuchundarī rājamūṣikā, dhānayaṃ mūṣakaḥ, tailaṃ tailapāyī pakṣī | parasvaṃ hṛtvā nānātiryagjātiḥ syāt | hutaṃ havir aśnañ chvātiduḥkhī ca | yānaṃ harann uṣṭraḥ, phalaṃ kapiḥ, jalaṃ jalaplavaḥ pataṅgo vā pakṣī matsyo vā, kṣīraṃ kāko bako vā, gṛhopaskaraṃ gṛhahārī kīṭaḥ | madhu haran daṃśaḥ kīṭaḥ, māṃsaṃ gṛdhraḥ, gāṃ godhā sarpo vā, agniṃ bakaḥ, vastraṃ kuṣṭhī pataṅgo vā, rasāṃś ca lavaṇacārī kīṭaḥ, taijasaṃ maṇḍalī, godevabrāhmaṇasvaṃ pāṇḍurogī, nyāsaṃ kāṇo ‘napatyo vā, śayyāṃ kṣapaṇakaḥ | śaṅkhaṃ śuktiṃ haran kapālī, dīpaṃ kauśikaḥ, snehaṃ kṣayī, parasvaṃ parapreṣyaḥ | yad yad apaharanti tattatprakārāḥ prāṇino jāyante tatsādhyavikalā vā | brāhmaṇīṃ gacchan nirbījaḥ | mātaraṃ snuṣāṃ vā gacchan vātavṛṣaṇaḥ, caṇḍālīṃ [100] pulkasīṃ vājagaraḥ, pravrajitāṃ naraḥ piśācaḥ, śūdrīṃ dīrghakīṭaḥ, savarṇāṃ daridraḥ, rājastriyaṃ napuṃsakaḥ | gāṃ gacchan maṇḍūkaḥ | dhānyamiśrako ‘tiriktāṅgaḥ, piśuno durgandhi(ndha)nāsikaḥ, sūcako durgandhāsyaḥ, devabrāhmaṇakrośakaḥ skhaladvāk, garadāgnidāv unmattau, gurupratikūlo ‘pasmārī, goghnaś cāndhaḥ | dharmapatnīm utsṛjyānyatra pravṛttaḥ śabdavedhī prāṇī gulmī vā | madhukuṇḍāśī madhubhakṣyaḥ, strīpaṇyajīvī ṣaṇḍhaḥ, kaumāradāratyāgī durbhagaḥ, mṛṣṭaikāśī vānaro vātagulmī vā, abhakṣyabhakṣako gaṇḍamālī, krūrakarmā vāmanaḥ, mitradhruk kṣayī, mātāpitror ākrośī ṣaṇḍhākāraḥ, anṛtavāk saṃcalitavāk, kūṭasākṣy ucchinnajaṅghācaraṇaḥ, vivāhavighnakartā chinnoṣṭhaḥ, avagoraṇe chinnahastaḥ, catuṣpathe viṇmūtravisarge mūtrakṛcchrī, kanyādūṣakaḥ ṣaṇḍhaḥ, īrṣyālur maśakaḥ, pitrā vivadamāno ‘pasmārī, vidyāvikrayī puruṣamṛgaḥ, vedavikrayī dvīpī, bahuyācako jalaplavaḥ, ayājyayājako varāhaḥ, animantritabhojī vāyasaḥ, yatastato ‘śnan mārjāraḥ, kakṣavanadāhī khadyotaḥ, adattādāyī balīvardaḥ, dārakācāryo mukhavigandhaḥ, paryuṣitāśī kṛmiḥ, matsarī bhramaraḥ, agnyutsādī maṇḍalakuṣṭhī, śūdrācāryaḥ śvapākaḥ, vārdhuṣiko ‘ṅgahīnaḥ, avikrayavikrayī gṛdhraḥ, rājākrośako gardabhaḥ, anadhyāye ‘dhyāyī śṛgālaḥ, matsyavadhe garbhavāsī | ityādīny anūrdhvagasamāni striyo ‘py eteṣu nimitteṣu tajjātiṣu striyo jāyante | idānīṃtanapāpināṃ prāyaścittonmukhatvārthaṃ pūrvajanmakarmavipākā darśitāḥ |
atha mahāpātakinaḥ —
brahmahā surāpo brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyī gurutalpaga ete mahāpātakinaḥ | abdaṃ tatsaṃsargī pañcamaś ca tadvat sākṣāt kartur anugrāhaka ājñāpayitopadeṣṭā ca | ājñāpayitābhyarthayitā | upadeṣṭā prayojayitā svārthaṃ parārthaṃ ca |
(*ākruṣṭas tāḍito vāpi dhanair vā viprayojitaḥ |
yam uddiśya tyajet prāṇān brahmahā sa nigadyate || )
[101] anumantā sanimittaṃ bhartsanatāḍanārthaharaṇādinā kopaṃ janayan nimittakartā | tatkartṛṣu sākṣāt kartur anugrāhakasyālpadoṣaḥ | tasmāt prayojakasya | tasmāc copadeṣṭuḥ | tasmāt prayojakānumantuḥ | tasmān nimittakartur alpadoṣaḥ prāyaścittaṃ ca tadvat |
athānupātakāni —
tatra brahmahatyāsamāni yāgasthanṛpavaiśyayor vadhaḥ, śaraṇāgatasya ca | rajasvalāyā garbhiṇyāś cānatrigotrāyāś cāvijñātagarbhasya suhṛnmātrasya ca vadhaḥ | guruvipraviṣaye ‘jñānān mithyābhiśaṃsanaṃ krodhotpādanam | adhikṣepo ‘sakṛn mithyānirbandhanaś ca | rājagāmi pauśunyam | gurau mahādveṣaḥ | nāstikyād vedanindāśāstrābhyāsād vedanāśaś ca |
atha surāpānasamāni —
laśunaviḍvarāhacchatrākagrāmakukkuṭapalāṇḍugṛñjanādīnāṃ matyā bhakṣaṇam | guruviṣaye mithyābhiśaṃsanenāyathāvāditvam anyathākartṛtvaṃ ca | jaihmyaṃ kauṭilyaṃ ca tadvat | ātmotkarṣārthaṃ rājakulādāv anṛtoktir udakyāvaktrāsvādo mitravadhaḥ kūṭasākṣyam | vedatyāgo vedanindā | tayor annasya bhuktiḥ |
atha suvarṇasteysamāni —
brāhmaṇasaṃbandhyaśvaratnamanuṣyastrībhūdhenuharaṇam | nikṣepasya cārvāk suvarṇamānāt |
atha gurutalpagasamāni —
snuṣābhaginīcaṇḍālīsakhibhāryottamajātikanyāsu retaḥsekaḥ | tathā pitṛṣvasāraṃ mātṛṣvasāraṃ mātulānīṃ mātuḥ sapatnīṃ snuṣābhaginyau sakāme ācāryapatnīṃ tanayāṃ svasṛpitṛvyamātāmahamātulaśrotriyartviggurūpādhyāyaśiṣyastriyam uttamām udakyāṃ nikṣiptāṃ pravratijāṃ vratasthām uttamāṃ brāhmaṇīṃ sagotrāṃ śaraṇāgatāṃ mātuḥ sakhīṃ rājapatnīṃ stanyadhātrīm anyamātaraṃ mātṛṣvasuḥ sakhīṃ mātulānīsakhīṃ gacchan gurutalpagavat sadyaḥ patati | tathā pitṛmātṛyonisaṃbandhāṃ gacchan | stenanāstikāninditakarmatyāgininditakarmātyāgipatitātyāgyapatitatyāginaḥ patitāḥ pātakasaṃyojakāś cety anupātakāni |
athopapātakāni —
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
govadho vrātyatā steyam ṛṇānāṃ cānapakriyā |
anāhitāgnitāpaṇyavikrayaḥ parivedanam ||
[102]        bhṛtād adhyayanādānaṃ bhṛtakādhyāpanaṃ tathā |
pāradāryaṃ pārivittyaṃ vārdhuṣyaṃ lavaṇakriyā ||
strīśūdraviṭkṣatravadho ninditārthopajīvanam |
nāstikyaṃ vratalopaś ca sutānāṃ caiva vikrayaḥ ||
dhānyakupyapaśusteyam ayājyānāṃ ca yājanam |
pitṛmātṛsutatyāgas taḍāgārāmavikrayaḥ ||
kanyāsaṃdūṣaṇaṃ caiva [parivedakayājanam |
kanyāpradānaṃ tasyaiva][1] kauṭilyaṃ vratalopanam ||
ātmano ‘rthe kriyārambho madyapastrīniṣevaṇam |
svādhyāyāgnisutatyāgo bāndhavatyāga eva ca ||
indhanārthaṃ drumacchedaḥ strīhiṃsauṣadhijīvanam |
hiṃsrayantravidhānaṃ ca vyasanāny ātmavikrayaḥ ||
śūdrapraiṣyaṃ hīnasakhyaṃ hīnayoniniṣevaṇam |
tathaivānāśrame vāsaḥ parānnaparipuṣṭatā ||
asacchrāstrābhigamanam ākareṣv adhikāritā |
bhāryāyā vikrayaś caiṣām ekaikam upapātakam || [YDh 3.235–42]
govadho gopiṇḍavyāpādanam | kāle ‘nupanītatvaṃ vrātyatā | brāhmaṇasuvarṇaṃ tatsamād arvāk parasvahāraṇaṃ steyam | gṛhītasya suvarṇāder apradānam ṛṇānapākaraṇaṃ devarṣipitṝṇām ṛṇānapākaraṇaṃ ca | saty apy adhikāre ‘nāhitāgnitvam | apaṇyasya lavaṇāder vikrayaḥ | sodare jyeṣṭhe bhrātari sthite kaniṣṭhasya dārāgnihotragrahaṇaṃ parivedanam | paṇapūrvādhyayanaṃ bhṛtādhyayanam | gurutalpavyatiriktatatsamastrīgamanaṃ pāradāryam | kaniṣṭhasya vivāhe jyeṣṭhasya vivāharāhityaṃ pārivittyam | niṣiddhavṛddhyupajīvitvaṃ vārdhuṣyam | lavaṇasyotpādanaṃ lavaṇakriyā | anātreyyā agarbhiṇyā api striyā vadhaḥ | śūdravadhaḥ | adīkṣitakṣatriyavadho vaiśyavadhaś ca | arājasthāpitārthopajīvanam | paralokābhāvabuddhir nāstikyam | brahmacāriṇaḥ strīsaṃyogo vratalope devatārādhanārthaṃ gṛhītavratalopaś ca | apatyānāṃ vikrayo dāsībhāvārthaṃ taḍāgārāmopavanādeś ca vikrayo dravyagrahaṇena vrīhyādidravyāṇām āharaṇaṃ steyam asāratrapusīsādikupyasya ca | gavādeḥ paśoś ca | jātiduṣṭaśūdrā[103]dīnāṃ yājanaṃ karmaduṣṭavrātyādīnāṃ ca yājanam | bhojanaṃ cobhayatra | apatitapitṛmātṛsutānāṃ gṛhanirgamas tyāgaḥ | aṅgulyādinā yonividāraṇaṃ kanyādūṣaṇam | parivedakayājanaṃ tasmai kanyāpradānaṃ ca | agurau kauṭilyam | ātmārthaṃ pākādikriyārambho madyapāyāḥ striyāś copabhogaḥ | kuṭumbarakṣaṇārtham asacchāstrādhigamārthaṃ cādhītasvādhyāyatyāgaḥ | śrautasmārtāgnīnāṃ ca saṃskārādyakaraṇena sutasya ca śaktau bāndhavānāṃ ca tyāgaḥ | pākādidṛṣṭakāryārtham ārdradrumāṇāṃ ca cchedaḥ | bhāryāṃ paṇyabhāvena prayojya tallabdhopajīvanaṃ strījīvanaṃ strīdhanopajīvanaṃ vā | prāṇivadhena jīvanam | vaśyādyarthauṣadhijīvanam | tilekṣvādipiṇḍārthaṃ hiṃsrayantrapravartanaṃ mahāyantrapravartanaṃ vā | vyasanāni mṛgayādīny aṣṭādaśa | ātmavikraya ātmano bhāryāyāś ca vikrayaḥ | śūdrapraiṣyaṃ śūdrasevā | hīneṣu maitraṃ hīnasakhyam | akṛtasavarṇavivāhaś ca hīnavarṇavivāho hīnayoniniṣevaṇaṃ sādhāraṇastrīsaṃbhogaś ca | agṛhītāśramitvam anāśramitvam | parapākaratitvaṃ parānnapuṣṭatā | cārvākādigranthābhyāso ‘sacchāstrādhigamaḥ | suvarṇādyutpattisthāneṣu rājājñayādhikāritvam ākareṣv adhikāritvam abhicāraś ca | laśunāder matyā bhakṣaṇam ityādīny upapātakāni |
atha jātibhraṃśakarasaṃjñakāni —
brāhmaṇasya rujaḥ kṛtyaṃ ghrātir aghreyamadyayoḥ |
jaihmyaṃ puṃsi ca maithunyaṃ jātibhraṃśakaraṃ hi tat || [MDh 11.68]
atha saṃkarīkaraṇāni —
kharoṣṭramṛgahastyajāvimīnāhimahiṣāṇām anyeṣāṃ grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ vadhaḥ |
athāpātrīkaraṇāni —
ninditebhyo dhanādānaṃ vāṇijyaṃ śūdrasevanam | kusīdajīvanam asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ ca |
[MDh 11.70]
atha malinīkaraṇāni —
bahukṛmikīṭajalasthajalajavayohatyā | madyānugatabhojanam | bahuphalendhanapuṣpasteyam adhairyaṃ ca |
ato ‘nyāni pāpāni prakīrṇasaṃjñāni |
atha mahāpātakaprāyaścittam —
ajñānād brāhmaṇaṃ hatvā vālavalkalacīrādivāsā jaṭī vāpito vā svena hatabrāhmaṇasya śiraḥkapālaṃ cihnārthaṃ tadabhāve ‘nyasya vā tadanyad eva śiraḥkapālaṃ dhvajadaṇḍau dhārayan vanyair annādyair ekavāraṃ pātramātrādiṣu mitāśī saṃyatātmā trikālasnāyī saṃdhyām [104] upāsanaṃ ca vidhivan mantrapūrvaṃ ca kurvann adhyayanādhyāpanavyākhyāpravacana-yājanadānapratigrahādi varjayan brahmacārī strīmadhumāṃsagandhamālyādi divānidrāñjanābhyañjanopānacchatrakāmakrodhamohaharṣaśokaviṣādādi nṛtyagītaparivādādi varjayan sarvabhūtahita āryabhuvaṃ yathopakramasthānāsanābhyāṃ viharaṃs tīrthāny anuvasan vane vā puṇyāśrame vā kuṭīṃ kṛtvā nivasan grāmānte govraje vā vṛkṣamūle vā dvādaśābdam evaṃ vrataṃ caritvā śuddhaḥ | yadi vanyair na jīvati tadā bhikṣārthaṃ grāmaṃ praviśet | tatra lohitakena mṛnmayakhaṇḍena śarāveṇa vā gṛhadvāri sthitvā brahmahāsmīti svapāpaṃ prakhyāpya saptāgārāṇi mṛṣṭāmṛṣṭāvivekena cāturvarṇyaṃ vā bhakṣyaṃ yācayitvaikakālam eva mitāśī dvādaśābdaṃ vrataṃ caritvā śuddhaḥ | yad vā dvādaśābdaṃ niyataḥ prājāpatyaṃ kṛcchraṃ nirantaram āvartya ṣaṣṭyadhikaṃ triśataṃ caritvā śudhyati | dvitīye brahmavadhe dviguṇam | tṛtīye triguṇam | caturthe na niṣkṛtiḥ | yad vā caturthādiṣu pratipāpeṣu gosahasram | nirdhano ‘gniṃ praviśet | yad vā pūrvoktaṃ bhikṣācaryākṛcchrācaraṇādi vrataṃ yāvan maraṇaṃ kuryāt tena śudhyati | (* tatra buddhipūrvaṃ sākṣād akartur buddhipūrvakam anugrāhakaprayojakānumantṛnimittakartṛprotsāhakādīnāṃ brahmahatyāsamānam ardham ardhaṃ tasyāpy ardham ityādy ūhyam | yad vā buddhipūrvam abuddhipūrvaṃ vā sākṣāt kartur anugrāhakaḥ pādonaṃ caritvā śudhyati | prayojakasya tadardham anumantā sārdhaṃ pādaṃ caret | nimittakartuḥ pādam | protsāhakādīnāṃ tāratamyavibhāgāt kalpyam | yadi nirnimittaṃ bhartsako guṇavān bharstyo nirguṇo mṛtas tadā tv ekābdaṃ vrataṃ caret | tadāpi mukhyakartṝṇāṃ ye tv anugrāhakādayas teṣāṃ tāratamyāt kalpyam | tathā daṇḍo ‘pi protsāhakāśrayadānumodakādīnāṃ yathāyogaṃ kalpyaḥ | ūnaṣoḍaśābdabālānām aśītyadhikavṛddhānāṃ ca strīṇāṃ rogiṇāṃ ca sarvaprāyaścittam | yad vā dvādaśābdād arvāg aśīter ūrdhvaṃ tu pādam eva | ūnaikādaśābdāntānāṃ puṃsām ardham | strīṇāṃ pādamātram eva | anupanītānāṃ bālānāṃ tu pādam eva prāyaścittaṃ pitā bhrātā tatsamo ‘nyo vā suhṛt sarvaṃ kalpoktaprāyaścittaṃ caret | tataḥ param ā ṣoḍaśābdād anye vā careyuḥ *) | tataḥ param aśītivarṣāntaṃ svayam evācareyuḥ | guruprāyaścitte prāyaścittāntarābhāve prasaṅgād anuṣṭhānasiddhiḥ | somayāgasthabrāhmaṇavadhe dviguṇaṃ vratam | tadaśaktau gavāṃ sahasradvayaṃ vidhivat [105] pātrebhyo dadyāt | ācāryavadhe dvādaśābdaṃ vrataṃ gosahasraṃ vā | upādhyāye ‘ṅgavaktari cānūcānaṛtvigvadhe dvādaśābdavratād ūrdhvaṃ gosahasraṃ pādonam ardhapādaṃ cared ityādy ūhyaṃ tāratamyakalpanayā | tathā ṛtvigguruvadhe yāvajjīvaṃ vrataṃ caret | dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caturguṇaṃ vā | tadaśaktau vrataṃ dvādaśābdaṃ kṛtvā gosahasraṃ ca kuryāt | atha yadi dvādaśābdaṃ caran madhye coravyādhair hanyamānam ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ godvādaśakaṃ vātmānam antarā kṛtvā rakṣet | asāv asaṃpūrṇadvādaśābdo ‘pi tadaiva śudhyati | yadi tatra pravṛttas tad akṛtvaiva mriyeta tathāpi tadaiva śudhyet | tathā parāśvamedhādimahāyajñāvabhṛthe yajamānasaṃnidhau vā svapāpam ṛtvijāṃ prakhyāpya tair anujñātaḥ snātvā tadaiva śudhyati | yad vā vratamadhye kuṣṭhādidīrghatīvravyādhigrastaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ gāṃ cauṣadhaiḥ saṃrakṣya tadaiva śudhyet | yad vā vratamadhye viprasya bhūdhenvādisarvasvaṃ caurair hṛtaṃ sarvaṃ datvā śudhyet | tatra pravṛttas tair hato bahukṣato mṛtakalpo jīvan vāpi tadaiva śudhyet | jñātvā brāhmaṇavadhe caturvarṇā bhṛgupatanam agnipatanaṃ vā kuryuḥ | tatra ye traivarṇikā iṣṭaprathamayajñās te ‘gnipatanaṃ svarjitaṃ gosavam abhijidviśvajitau trivṛdagniṣṭutaṃ vā krameṇālpadakṣiṇaṃ kuryuḥ | kṣatriyaḥ sārvabhaumaś ced aśvamedhaṃ kuryāt | yady ajñānāt traivarṇikāḥ sāgnikā mahāpāpaṃ kuryus tarhi tatputrādayo hy ā doṣakṣayāt tadagnīn rakṣayeyuḥ | jñānāc ced gṛhyāgnīn vaitānikān apsu prakṣipet tato ‘gnipatanādi prāyaścittaṃ kurvatāṃ maraṇe caivaṃ saparicchadānām | vidvāṃś cen nirdhano guṇī hantā hato nirguṇo ‘raṇye niyatas trivāraṃ vedaṃ japet | śaktaś ced vedaṃ japan yojanaśataṃ vrajet | avidvān nirdhano guṇī plākṣaprasravaṇādau paścimodadheḥ pratisrotaḥ sarasvatīṃ haviṣyamitāśī gacchet | nirguṇo dhanī hantā cet pātre gṛhabhūmyādi jīvanasamarthaṃ dadyāt | gṛhaṃ vā sopaskaraṃ jīvanasamartham anapatyaś cet sarvasvam | etattraye ‘nyatamaṃ gṛhītvāhitāgnir vaiśvānareṣṭim anāhitāgnir vaiśvānarasthālīpākaṃ kuryāt | hantā mūrkho nirdhano vipramātravadhe ‘dhaḥśāyī triṣavaṇī svakarma vedayan bhaikṣāśī mahānadīsaṃgamādipuṇyāśramagoṣṭhaparvataprasravaṇatapovanavihārī sthāne vīrāsanī vatsaraṃ kṛtsnaṃ caritvā ratnahemagodhānyatiladadhisarpīṃṣi viprebhyo dadyāt | manasā brahmavadhyavyavasāyaṃ kṛtvā svayam evoparato dvādaśāhaṃ jalāśī dvādaśāham upavased ityādi | anyatra sameṣu vikalpo viṣameṣu vyavasthā kalpyā | uktāni dvādaśābdādīni brāhmaṇasyaiva | dviguṇaṃ kṣatriyasya [106] triguṇaṃ vaiśyasya | śūdrādeś caturguṇam | tatrāpi hantṛhanyamānaguṇaviśeṣeṇa prāyaścittaviśeṣo jñeyaḥ | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyādāv api | hīnotkṛṣṭavadhe prāyaścittatāratamyaṃ daṇḍagauravāt kalpyam | anulomapratilomajānāṃ tu daṇḍavat prāyaścitam | gṛhasthād dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇo vānaprasthasya triguṇaṃ yateś caturguṇam | brahmacāriṇo dvaiguṇyaṃ ṣoḍaśābdād ūrdhvam eva | sarvatra prakrāntaprāyaścittasya madhye maraṇe tadaiva śuddhiḥ |
atha nideśaḥ —
somayāgasthakṣatravaiśyavadhe brahmahatyāvrataṃ dvādaśābdaṃ caret | jñānād dvaiguṇyam eva na prāṇāntikam | tatra ca jātiśaktiguṇādyapekṣayā vyavasthā | sarvatra strīnapuṃsakatvenāvijñātagarbhasya vadhe, ṛtumaty ātreyy atrigotrā cātreyī tasyāś ca vadhe puruṣahatyāvad vyavahāre sākṣāt satyoktau varṇānāṃ vadhaprāptau sākṣitvaṃ gurau krodhāveśī ca brāhmaṇanikṣepaṃ hṛtvāhitāgnibrāhmaṇabhāryāṃ saguṇāṃ somayāgasya nighātako brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | ātideśikeṣu pādonaṃ prāyaścittam | prāyaścittasyātideśo na pātityasya ca | brāhmaṇādivadhārthaṃ śastraprahāre kenacit pratibandhenāsau na mṛtas tathāpi brahmahatyādivrataṃ pādonaṃ cared āgamane ‘rdham | prayatne pādaḥ | brahmahatyāsamānārthaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sarvatra sākṣāt kartur anugrāhakasya pādonaṃ prayojakasyārdham anumantuḥ sārdhaṃ pādaṃ nimittakartuḥ pādaṃ protsāhakādīnāṃ ca tāratamyaṃ vidyāt | sarvatra bālavṛddhastriyo rogiṇaś ca sākṣāt kartṛtve ‘pi prāyaścittārdham arhanti | aśītivarṣād ūrdhvaṃ vṛddhaḥ ṣoḍaśābdāt prāg bālaḥ | anupanītānāṃ bālānāṃ kanyānāṃ ca pādaṃ prāyaścittam | tac ca pitā bhrātānyo vā suhṛc caret | tato dvādaśābdānta evaṃ cārayeyuḥ | ūrdhvaṃ svayam eva careyuḥ | sarvatra jātiśaktiguṇādyapekṣayā vyavasthā |
iti smṛtyarthasāre brahmahatyāprāyaścittam |
atha surāpānaprāyaścittam —
tatra mukhyasurā paiṣṭhī gauḍī mādhvī ca | tanmadhye surāṃ traivarṇikas tathā śūdrā strī ca cāturvarṇyavivāha utpattyādi na pibet | yadi surāṃ jñānāt sakṛt pibet surāmbugomūtrakṣīra-ghṛtānām anyatamam agnisaṃnibhaṃ kṛtvārdravāsā lauhena pātreṇāyasena tāmreṇa vā pītvā maraṇāc chudhyet | ajñānāj jalabuddhyā surāpāne tālumātrasaṃyoge jaṭī vālacīrādivāsā brahmahatyāvrataṃ tacchihnavarjaṃ dvādaśābdaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ kuryān na [107] pādonam | sarvatra strībālavṛddhānām ardhaṃ ṣaḍabdam | anupanītānāṃ kanyānāṃ ca pādaṃ tryabdam eva kuryāt | jñānāc ced dviguṇam | yadi pītvā charded vā tryabdaṃ rātrau sakṛt piṇyākam evādyān nāśnīyāt | surāpāne tāvanmātrasaṃyoge varṣaṃ vā vālavāsā jaṭī dhvajī tathā kuryāt | suraikauṣadharogārthe pāne tv ajñāne kṛcchrābdapādaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā kṛtvā punar upanayanaṃ ca kāryam | bālye tu tac ca mātā bhrātā pitā vā kuryāt | jñāne tu taptakṛcchraṃ parākaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ca kramāt kuryāt | surāsaṃspṛṣṭaśuṣkarasānnasya mūtraviḍretaḥśavādīnāṃ vā jñānād bhakṣaṇe kāryaṃ punarupanayanam | śuṣkasurābhāṇḍodakapāne chardanam upavāso ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca | taj jalaṃ paryuṣitaṃ pītvā śaṅkhapuṣpīvipakvakṣīraṃ tryahaṃ pibet | ajñānābhyāse pañcarātraṃ pibet | jñānāt tajjalapāne saptarātraṃ pibet | jñānābhyāse tu dvādaśarātraṃ kṣīreṇa brāhmīsuvarcalāṃ pibet | surāpasya mukhagandhasyāmatyā ghrāṇe somapaś ced apsu trīn prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśnīyāt | matyā ced dviguṇaṃ sarvatra | asomapaś ced anapsu prāṇāyāmān kṛtvoktaṃ prāśnīyāt kuśodakaṃ cāhuḥ | sākṣāt surāgandhaghrātau tu jātibhraṃśakareṣūktaṃ sāntapanam | matyā cet kāryaṃ jñeyam ityādi |
iti smṛtyarthasāre surāpānaprāyaścittam |
atha madyapānaprāyaścittam —
tatra gauḍī mādhvī ca surā madyam | tālaṃ pānasaṃ drākṣaṃ mādhukaṃ khārjūraṃ sairam āriṣṭaṃ maireyaṃ nālikerajam etāny ekādaśamadyāni vidyāt | etāni madyāny utpattyādi brāhmaṇānāṃ tatstrīṇāṃ cānupanītānāṃ kanyāyāś cāpeyāni | kṣatravaiśyayor na duṣyati madyam | ajñānāj jalādibuddhyā tālādimadyapāne taptakṛcchraṃ punar upanayanaṃ ca | mūtraviḍretaḥsrāvādibhakṣye dvijānāṃ caivam | ajñānād gauḍīmādhvyor jñānāt tālādimadyapāne ca kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇāni kṛtvā punarupanayanaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca | gauḍīmādhvyor jñānāt pāne tu piṇyākena kaṇair vā traivārṣikam | abhyāse ṣaḍabdam | atyantābhyāse nirantarābhyāse ca dvādaśābdam | bahukālābhyāse cāgnivarṇaṃ madyaṃ pītvā mṛtaḥ śudhyet | tālādiṣu tadardhaṃ kalpyam | sarvatra strībālavṛddhādīnām ardham | anupanītasya kanyāyāś ca pādaṃ mātā [108] pitā bhrātā vā kuryāt | tālādimadyāpekṣayā lehyābhakṣyamūtraviḍretaḥ-srāvādīnāṃ mukhamātrapraveśe kṣīraudumbarabilvapālāśakuśodakaṃ ṣaḍrātraṃ pibet | gauḍīmādhvyos tu dviguṇam | ajñānād gauḍīmādhvīmadyavāsitaśuṣkabhāṇḍāmbupāne kuśamūlavipakvaṃ kṣīraṃ tryahaṃ pibet | jñānato ‘bhyāse tu kṣīraudumbarabilvapālāśais tryaham | ajñānābhyāse śaṅkhapuṣpīghṛtaṃ kṣīraṃ ca pañcarātraṃ pibet | jñānato ‘bhyāse tu gomūtrayāvakaṃ saptarātraṃ pibet | atyantābhyāse tu brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ payasā dvādaśarātram | tālādimadyabhāṇḍodakapāne tv ardhaṃ kalpyam | sarvatra strībālādīnāṃ yogyaṃ kalpyam |
iti smṛtyarthasāre madyapānaprāyaścittam |
atha suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittam ucyate —
steyaśabdena samakṣaṃ parokṣaṃ vā balāc cauryeṇa vā krayādisvatvahetutvaṃ vinā grahaṇam ucyate | tatrādau suvarṇapramāṇaṃ nirūpyate | gavākṣagatasūryaraśmimadhye dṛśyamānaṃ trasareṇupramāṇaṃ raja ity ācakṣate | trasareṇvaṣṭakaṃ tu likṣā | likṣātrayaṃ rājasarṣapaḥ | rājasarṣapatrayaṃ gaurasarṣapaḥ | gaurasarṣapaṣaṭkaṃ yavaḥ | yavatrayaṃ kṛṣṇalaḥ | kṛṣṇalapañcakaṃ māṣaḥ | ṣoḍaśamāṣakaṃ suvarṇam | amatyā brāhmaṇasya suvarṇaṃ hṛtvā dvādaśa varṣāṇi brahmahatyāvrataṃ śavaśirodhvajatatkapāladhāraṇarahitaṃ caret | yasya kasyāpi hiraṇyaṃ yaḥ kaścid dharati sa tasyaikādaśaguṇaṃ datvā prāyaścittaṃ samācaret | gurūṇāṃ yajñakartṝṇāṃ vā śrotriyāṇāṃ vā dhārmikabrāhmaṇānāṃ vā suvarṇaṃ hṛtvātmano muṇḍanaṃ kṛtvātmaśarīraṃ ghṛtenābhyajya kārīṣācchādito dagdhaḥ śudhyet | nirguṇaviprasuvarṇaharaṇe navābdam īdṛśam eva caret | kṣāmabahukuṭumbarakṣaṇārthaṃ haraṇe kṛcchraṃ ṣaḍabdaṃ viśvajidādikaṃ kratuṃ vā kuryāt | matyā yaḥ kaścid brāhmaṇasuvarṇahāry ekasuvarṇam ārabhya svapāpaṃ bhūpāya nivedyāyasaṃ daṇḍaṃ khādiraṃ vā pradāya muktakeśo bhṛśaṃ rājñā daṇḍena tāḍito mṛto vā jīvan vā śudhyet | svaśaktyātāḍane rājñas tu doṣaḥ syāt | suvarṇasteyī brāhmaṇo vratam eva caren na daṇḍenātmānaṃ ghātayet | na ca vipraṃ rājā hanyāt | hanane brahmahatyā bhavaty eva | agnihotrī dhārmikaś ca vidvān kṣatriyo brāhmaṇasuvarṇahāry aśvamedhena śudhyati | gosavaṃ yajñaṃ vā kṛtvā śudhyet | ātmasaṃmite hema vā datvā śudhyet | tāvaddhanābhāve tapasy aśaktau ca viprasya sakuṭumbasya [109] rakṣaṇaparyāptaṃ dhanaṃ dadyāt | abrāhmaṇaś ced brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyī daṇḍatāḍanenātmamaraṇam anicchan surāpānavrataṃ kṛtvā śudhyet | yadā tv apahārānantaraṃ jātānutāpaḥ pratyarpayati tyajati vā tatrāpastamboktaṃ caturthakālamitāśanaṃ tryabdam | manasā suvarṇasteye pravṛttaḥ svayam evoparataś ced dvādaśāhaṃ vāyubhakṣaḥ śudhyet | suvarṇasteyasameṣv aśvaratnamanuṣyastrībhūdhenu-haraṇādiṣu suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittārdhaṃ kāryam | trasareṇupramāṇasteya ekaprāṇāyāmaḥ | likṣāpramāṇahemasteye trayaḥ | rājasarṣapapramāṇahemasteye catvāraḥ ṣaḍ vā prāṇāyāmāḥ | gaurasarṣapapramāṇasteye ‘ṣṭasahasragāyatrījapaḥ | yavapramāṇahemasteye prātar ārabhya sāyaṃkālāntaṃ gāyatrījapaḥ | kṛṣṇalapramāṇahemasteye sāntapanam | māṣapramāṇahemasteye gomūtrapakvayavabhuṅ māsatrayeṇa śudhyet | suvarṇapramāṇāt kiṃcinnyūnahemasteye gomūtrapakvayavabhug abdena śudhyet | saṃpūrṇapramāṇabrāhmaṇahemasteye dvādaśābdavratādikaṃ kāryam ity uktam | suvarṇapramāṇanyūnarajatasteye sāntapanam | niṣkacatuṣkapramāṇād ūrdhvaṃ daśaniṣkāntarajatasteye cāndrāyaṇaṃ kāryam | daśaniṣkapramāṇād ūrdhvaṃ śataniṣkāntarajatasteye cāndradvayam | śataniṣkapramāṇād ūrdhvaṃ sahasraniṣkāntarajatasteye cāndratrayam | sahasraniṣkapramāṇād adhikarajatasteye pādonaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | nāgarāṅgāyasatāmrapittalakāṃsyānāṃ sahasraniṣkapramāṇasteye parākam | eṣāṃ niṣkasahasrādimūlyānāṃ steye brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ dvādaśābdavratādikam | caturniṣkamūlyaratnasteye sāntapanam | caturniṣkamūlyād ūrdhvaṃ daśaniṣkamūlyāntaratnasteye cāndram | daśaniṣkād ūrdhvaṃ śataniṣkamūlyāntaratnasteye cāndradvayam | śataniṣkamūlyād ūrdhvaṃ sahasraniṣkamūlyāntaratnasteye cāndratrayam | ratnasahasraniṣkamūlyād adhikaratnasteye brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ pādonaṃ navābdādikam | abrāhmaṇasya nikṣepaṃ suvarṇaṃ hṛtvā ṣaḍabdaṃ vrataṃ caret | narabhūmituraṅgaharaṇe ca ṣaḍabdakam |
iti smṛtyarthasāre suvarṇasteyaprāyaścittam |
atha gurutalpaprāyaścittam —
janako gurus tattalpas tasya bhāryocyate | ajñānena mātaraṃ gatvā mātṛsapatnīṃ savarṇām uttamavarṇāṃ vā tayā protsāhito jñānād gatvā lohastriyaṃ sutaptām āliṅgya mṛtaḥ śuddhaḥ | tapte ‘yaḥśayane suptvā vā mṛtaḥ śuddhaḥ | ubhayoḥ protsāhanāt tu [110] taptāyaḥśayane suptasyāliṅgane śuddhiḥ | svayaṃ protsāhitaś cet svasya guhyatrikaṃ svahastacchinnaṃ svāñjalinā dhṛtvā nairṛtīṃ diśaṃ gacchann agrataḥ sthitam avicārayan pṛṣṭhato ‘paśyan prāṇāntaṃ śudhyet | amatyā savarṇottamastrīgamane dvādaśābdaṃ brahmahatyāvratam | amatyābhyāse ghṛtākto muṇḍitaḥ karīṣarāśimadhyasthaḥ pādādisarvāṅgadāhān mṛtaḥ sudhyet | jananyāṃ matyā caivam | jananyāṃ matyā pravṛttau retaḥsekāt prāṅ nivṛttasya dvādaśābdam | amatyā ṣaḍabdam | matyā mātuḥ sapatnīṃ sarvarṇāṃ vyabhicāriṇīṃ gatvā vedajapasahitaṃ cāndratrayaṃ kuryāt | amatyābdaṃ kṛcchram | kṣatriyām api pitṛbhāryāṃ matyā vipraḥ sakṛd gatvā traivārṣikam aṣṭakālāśanam | matyābhyāse liṅgaṃ vinā vṛṣaṇacchedāc chuddhiḥ | kṣatriyāyāṃ matyā pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt prāṅ nivṛttau tayā protsāhitasya traimāsikaṃ kāryam | ubhayaprotsāhitasyātikṛcchraṃ traimāsikam | svena protsāhitāyāṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ traimāsikam | tatraivāmatyā pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt prāk pravṛttasya [nivṛttasya?] yoṣidubhayātmaprotsāhe tu kramāt kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇāni | vaiśyāyāṃ tu gurupatnyāṃ matyā sakṛdgamane ṣaḍabdaṃ caret | matyābhyāse liṅgārdhacchedane śuddhiḥ | amatyā sakṛdgamane tryabdam | atyantābhyāse yāvajjīvaṃ vratī | vaiśyāyāṃ matyā pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt pūrvaṃ nivṛttasyobhayātmayoṣitprotsāheṣu kramāt taptakṛcchraparākasāntapanāni | amatyā pravṛttasya yoṣidubhyātmaprotsāheṣu kramāt pañcarātrasaptarātrāṣṭarātrāṇi | śūdrāṃ gurubhāryāṃ matyā vipraḥ sakṛd gatvā tryabdaṃ caret | matyābhyāse dvādaśābdaṃ kāryam | amatyābhyāse nirantaraṃ cāndrāyaṇam | śudrāyāṃ matyā pravṛttasya retaḥsekāt prāṅ nivṛttasya yoṣidubhayātmaprotsāheṣu kramād atikṛcchraparākāḥ | tatraivāmatyā pravṛttasya kāyasāntapanasaptarātropavāsāḥ | evaṃ kṣatriyāputrasya vaiśyāyāṃ mātari matyā sakṛdgamanādiṣu navābdādīni yojyāni | śūdrāyāṃ ca navābdādīni yojyāni | vaiśyāputrasya ca śūdrāyāṃ navābdādīni yojyāni | sarvatra gamanaṃ caramadhātuvisargāntam ucyate | sarvatra retaḥsekāt prāṅ nivṛttau maraṇānteṣu dvādaśābdam | anyatroktārdhaṃ kāryam | puruṣavat strīṇāṃ mahāpātakāni santy eva | atas tāsāṃ matyā [111] pravṛttau maraṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittam | amatyā pravṛttau dvādaśābdādiṣv eva sarvatrārdhakḷptyā yogyaṃ puruṣoktavat kāryam |
jātyuktaṃ pāradāryaṃ ca kanyādūṣaṇam eva ca |
sādhāraṇaṃ striyāṃ nāsti gurutalpatvam eva ca ||
athātipātakānupapātakeṣūcyante —
matyā samānagotrāṃ samānapravarām ūḍhāṃ gatvā gurutalpavratāc chudhyet | tajjo garbhaś cāṇḍālaḥ syāt | sā patnī saṃbhoge tyājyā rakṣyaiva | amatyoḍhāṃ gatvā tribhiś cāndraiḥ śudhyet | garbhaḥ kaśyapaḥ syāt | savarṇakanyāṃ mitrastriyaṃ bhaginīṃ sagotrāṃ cāṇḍālīṃ māgaṅgīṃ vātmajastriyaṃ sakṛd ajñānād gatvā cāndradvayāc chudhyet | jñānāt sakṛdgamane kṛcchrābdāc chuddhiḥ | āsām anyatamām ekasyāṃ rātrau bahuvāraṃ gatvā brahmahatyāvrataṃ traivārṣikaṃ caret | āsām anyatamām anekadineṣu gatvā brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | āsām ekāṃ buddhipūrvaṃ bahudineṣu gatvāgniṃ praviśya mṛtaḥ śudhyet | mātṛṣvasāraṃ pitṛṣvasāram ācaryaputrīm ācāryabhāryām anujapatnīṃ cātmasutāṃ mātulānīm ajñānāt sakṛd gatvābdakṛcchreṇa śudhyet | āsām ekām ekasmin dine bahuvāraṃ gatvā traivārṣikaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | āsām ekām bahudineṣv anubandhena gatvā brahmahatyāprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | āsām ekām ekasmin dine ‘sakṛj jñānād gatvā ṣaḍabdaṃ brahmahatyāvrataṃ caret | āsām ekāṃ bahudineṣu buddhipūrvaṃ gatvāgniṃ praviśya mṛtaḥ śudhyet | cāṇḍālīṃ pulkasīṃ vyādhastriyaṃ bhaginīṃ snuṣāṃ mātuḥ svasāraṃ ca sakhīṃ viśvastāṃ pravrājitāṃ mātulānīṃ śaraṇāgatām antaḥpurastriyaṃ ca gatvā śiṣyapatnīṃ patitāṃ sagotrāṃ ca gatvā retoniḥsaraṇāt pūrvaṃ nivṛttasya māsopavāsena śuddhiś cāndreṇa vā | etāsu vyabhicāriṇīṃ yadi sakṛd gacchec cāndreṇa śudhyet | ajñātāṃ striyaṃ sakṛd gatvā taptakṛcchreṇa śudhyet |
iti smṛtyarthasāre gurutalpagaprāyaścittm |
atha mahāpātakisaṃsargiprāyaścittam —
etaiś caturbhir mahāpātakibhir adhyayanādhyāpanavivāhayajñayājanasahavāsasahabhojanāni kṛtvā sadyaḥ pātitym āpnoti | etair ekāsanasthitim ekapaṅktibhojanam ekaśayane svāpam ekavāhanagatiṃ caikavatsaraṃ kṛtvā pātityam āpnoti | yena mahāpātakinā yaḥ saṃsargī patitaḥ sa tasyoktavratāc chudhet | asya maraṇāpādakaprāyaścittasthāne brahmahatyāvrataṃ dvādaśābdikaṃ syāt | ajñānād yasya yena saṃsargo jātas tasya taduktavratārdhāc chuddhiḥ | ajñānāc caturbhir mahāpātakibhiḥ pañcarātraṃ saṃsarge trirā[112]traṃ daśarātrasaṃsarge kāyakṛcchrādi | dvādaśarātraṃ saṃsarge sāntapanam | pakṣasaṃsarge daśopavāsāḥ | māsasaṃsarge parākāc chuddhiḥ | trimāsasaṃsarge cāndram | ṣaṇmāsasaṃsarge cāndradvayam | kiṃcinnyūnābdaṃ nairantaryasaṃsarge ṣaṇmāsaṃ kṛcchrād vā | kiṃcinnyūnaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ saṃsarge cāndrāyaṇābdam | etaiś caturbhiḥ saṃsarge strībālavṛddhāturās tattaduktavratārdhaṃ kuryuḥ | mahāpātakisaṃsargisaṃsarge tu tatsaṃsargivratasyārdhaṃ kuryāt |
iti smṛtyarthasāre mahāpātakisaṃsargiprāyaścittaṃ samāptam |
athopapātakaprāyaścittāny ucyante —
ajñānād brāhmaṇamātrasya gomātraṃ brāhmaṇo hatvā trirātram upoṣya vṛṣabhaikādaśagā datvā śudhyati dvādaśaprajāpatyair vā | pratyāmnāyadvādaśadhenūr vā datvā śudhyati | jñānāc ced gohito gor gamanasthānāsanaśayaneṣu svayaṃ ca tathā kuryāt | bhayavyādhikṣutpipāsāśītātapādibhyo rakṣann ātmīyaparakīyabhakṣyapāneṣv avārayann avedayan rātrau gāṃ natvā vīrāsano vanaśākādyāhāro māsatrayānte vṛṣabhaikādaśa gā dadyāt | aśaktaḥ sarvasvaṃ datvā śudhyati | prājāpatyapratyāmnāyapakṣe tv ajñānapakṣād dviguṇam | ajñānāt kṣatriyasya gomātraṃ hatvā gohito go’nugāmī goṣṭhaśāyī triṣavaṇasnāyī saṃyataḥ pañcagavyam eva piban māsānte dhenuṃ datvā śudhyati ṣaṭprājāpatyair vā | pratyāmnāye ṣaḍdhenavo deyāḥ | jñānāc cec caturthakāle haviṣyam akṣāralavaṇaṃ mitam aśnan gomūtrasnāyī dvau māsau nītvā śudhyet | prājāpatyaṃ pratyāmnāye dviguṇam | ajñānād vaiśyasya gomātraṃ hatvā gohitādikṛd atikṛcchraṃ nirantaraṃ caran māsānte dhenuṃ datvā śudhyati pañcaprājāpatyair vā pratyāmnāye dhenuṃ dadyāt | jñānāc ced vaiśyasya gomātraṃ hatvā taddviguṇaṃ sarvaṃ caret | ajñānāc chūdrasya gomātraṃ hatvā gohitādikṛt prājāpatyaṃ nirantaraṃ caran māsānte dhenuṃ datvā śudhyati | pratyāmnāye dhenucatuṣkam | jñānāc cet kṛtavān gocarmaṇārdreṇa saṃvṛto goṣṭhe vasan ṣaṇmāsaṃ yavāgūṃ pibañ śudhyati | prājāpatyapratyāmnāyau ced dviguṇau | akāmāt somayāgasthaśrotriyasya gomātraṃ hatvā gohitādikṛc chākādibhug abdānte vṛṣabhaikādaśagā dadyāt | gogrāsabhuk ṣaṇmāsair vā śuddhaḥ | kāmāc ced gohitādikṛn mauṇḍī śākabhuk tryabdaṃ caret | dhanī vakṣyamāṇavrataṃ gośataṃ caret | jñānāt somayāgasthaśrotriyātidurgatakuṭumbibrāhmaṇasya kapilāṃ [113] homārthāṃ garbhiṇīṃ bahukṣīrāṃ suvṛttataruṇādiguṇāṃ nirguṇo dhanavān pratijñayā khaḍgādinā hatvā bhrūṇahā bhavati | sa brahmahatyādviguṇaṃ vrataṃ caret | yad vā gohitādikṛd daṇḍamauñjībhṛd akṣāralavaṇaṃ rūkṣaṃ ṣaṣṭhe kāle ‘śnan gomatīṃ praṇavaṃ vedaṃ vā japan dvimāsānte gosahasraṃ dadyāt | ajñānāc ced brahmahatyāvrataṃ traivārṣikam | vṛṣabhaikaśatagodānam agarbhiṇyāḥ kāmataś ced vadhaḥ | akāmataś ced gohitādikṛn mauñjī śākādibhuk tryabdaṃ caret | yadi tāṃ kāṣṭhena hanyāt sāntapanaśataṃ gosahasraśatadānaṃ tryabdādiṣu | loṣṭhena kāyam | pāṣāṇena taptakṛcchrapūrvaṃ vratānte viprān saṃbhojya tebhyas triṃśadgā vṛṣabhaṃ ca dadyāt | bālām ativṛddhām atikṛśāṃ rogiṇīṃ ca hatvā pūrvaproktārdhavrataṃ kṛtvā viprān saṃbhojya śaktyā hematilān dadyāt | ekābde gavi hate pādaṃ dvyabde ‘rdhaṃ tryabde pādonaṃ tataḥ kāyaṃ caret | garbhinyā vadhe tūcyate | utpanne garbhe pādaṃ dārḍhye ‘rdham āpūrṇe pādonaṃ caitanye govrataṃ dviguṇam | ajñānād ekāṃ bahavo hatvoktavratasya pādaṃ pratyekaṃ kuryuḥ | dve hatvārdhaṃ bahvīḥ pādonam | jñānāt pratyekaṃ kṛcchram eva | ekena rodhādinā bahūnāṃ vadhe dviguṇaṃ vratam | ajñānād vaidyena mithyauṣadhenaikasyā vadhe dviguṇam | ajñānād avaidyena hitabuddhyā tv auṣadhadāne pādavratam | rodhādau vyavahitakartṛṣu rodhane pādabandhane ‘rdhaṃ yojane pādonaṃ nipātane sarvam | ajñānād rodhādi kṛtvā tajjanyapramādaparihārārtham avekṣamāṇe mṛte kāryaṃ kṛtvā viprān saṃbhojya gomithunaṃ dadyāt | rodhanādipramādarakṣaṇākaraṇe kramāt pādārdhaṃ tripādaṃ sarvāṇi traimāsikādīni kāryāṇi | cihnavyādher anyatra dāhe ‘tivāhe nāsāpucchādicchede nadīparvatādirodhe ‘tidāhe ‘tidamane saṃghāte yojane śṛṅkhalānālikeraśaṇavālamauñjādidṛḍhapāśabaddhe mṛte pādonam | sarvatra ghaṇṭākṣibhūṣaṇanimitte ‘rdhakṛcchram | mṛtakalpavadhe tu saktuyāvakabhaikṣa-payodadhighṛtāni kramān māsārdhaṃ bhuktvā viprān saṃbhojya gāṃ dadyāt | maraṇahetuvyādhim utpādya dvādaśarātraṃ pañcagavyaṃ pibet | śṛṅgāsthibhaṅge carmavimocanādau ca gavi jīvati māsāntaṃ yavāgūṃ pibet | aśaktaḥ payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ vā daśarātraṃ pibet | anugrāhakaprayojakādīnāṃ pūrvavad doṣatāratamayam | arakṣopekṣānimittaṃ jalapaṅkāc ca nīcādiviṣamasthāne durge ca śūnyagṛhabandhane vidyutsarpamṛgavyāghra-śvāpadādyair aśītavātādyair udbandhanādyaiś ca mṛte gosvāmī kāyaṃ [114] kuryāt | kāryāntaravyagratve tv ardham | gūḍhagarbhanirgamanārthaṃ sadaṃśadaṇḍāṅkuśādipraveśana-nimitte vadhe rakṣārthaṃ rodhabandhane ca na doṣaḥ | auṣadhasnehāhāre dīyamāne hitārthadāhacchedaśirobhedeṣu ca grāmaghāte śaraughe gṛhabhaṅge ‘tivṛṣṭau kuḍyādipāte cānyagṛhagrāmādidāhe doṣo nāsti | bandhe mṛte pādaṃ caret | gavi strītvam avavakṣitam | rājadaṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam | etat sarvaṃ hantur brāhmaṇasyaiva | kṣatriyasya sarvatra pādonam | vaiśyasyārdhaṃ śūdrādeḥ pādaṃ vidyāt | sarvatra strībālavṛddhādīnāṃ tv ardham | anupanītādīnāṃ pādaṃ tatpitrādiś caret | strīṇāṃ tu vapanam anugamanaṃ goṣṭhaśayanaṃ carmaprāvaraṇaṃ ca nāsti tatsarvakeśān samuddhṛtya dvyaṅgulaṃ chedayet | sarvatraivaṃ tāsām | puṃsāṃ tu pāpavrata ā kaṇṭhāl lomnāṃ vapanam | ardhe śmaśrūṇāṃ ca | tripāde śikhāvarjam | samaste saśikhaṃ vapanam | jñātvā cet sarvatra dviguṇam |
iti smṛtyarthasāre govadhaprāyaścittam |
atha vrātyādau prāyaścittam —
tatra sarvopapātakārthaṃ cāndraṃ pañcagavyaṃ māsaṃ payo māsaṃ parākaṃ traimāsikaṃ kṛcchraṃ vā pratipadoktābhāve kuryāt | vrātyatve tūpanetrādyabhāvenopanayanakālātikrame cāndrādivratacatuṣke ‘nyatamaṃ śaktyā kārayitvopanayanaṃ kāryam | anāpadi kālātikrame traimāsikaṃ kārayitvopanayet | tatraiva pañcadaśavarṣād ūrdhvam api kiyatkālātikrama auddhālakaṃ vrataṃ caret | tatraivam – dvau māsau yāvakena vartayet | māsaṃ payasā | pakṣam āmikṣayā | aṣṭarātraṃ ghṛtena | ṣaḍrātram ayācitena | trirātram abbhakṣo ‘horātram upavased iti | vrātyastomena vā yajet | yasya pitā pitāmaho vānupanītau syātāṃ tasyābdaṃ traividyakaṃ brahmacaryam | yasya prapitāmahāder nānusmaryata upanayanaṃ tasya dvādaśābdatraividyakaṃ brahmacaryam ity āpastamboktaṃ kṛtvopanayanam |
atha steye prāyaścittam —
tatra vipro viprasya daśakumbhaṃ dhānyaṃ vā tatparimitaṃ taṇḍulādi vā tāmrarajatādikam amatyā hṛtvā traimāsikaṃ kuryāt | matyābhyāse kṛcchrābdam | kṣatriyāder hartuḥ pādapādahrāsaḥ | kṣatriyasvaṃ vipro hṛtvā ṣāṇmāsikam | vaiśyasve traimāsikaṃ śūdrasve cāndram | evam uttaratrāpy ūhyam | kumbhaḥ pañcasahasrapalaparimāṇaḥ | viprasya gṛhakṣetrabhūmi[115]nikṣeparajatavajramaṇīnāṃ narāśvastrīṇāṃ ca sārdhaśatadvayapaṇalabhyapāṇīyarasavāpīkūpajalānāṃ ca hartur viprasya suvarṇasteyasamavat | kṣatrādisve cāndratadardhapādādi | trapusīsādidravyāṇām alpaprayojanānāṃ sārdhaśatadvayaṃ paṇapañcadaśārdhānāṃ hartuḥ sāntapanam | bhakṣyabhojyāhārapānādīnām ekavārabhojanaparyāptānāṃ pānaśayyāsanānāṃ puṣpamūlaphalānāṃ ca hartuḥ pañcagavyenāhorātram | trivārabhojanaparyāpte trirātram | tṛṇakāṣṭhadrumāṇāṃ trivārabhojanaparyāptamūlyārghyāṇāṃ śuṣkānnaguḍatailacarmamāṃsānāṃ ca hartus trirātram | maṇimuktāpravālatāmrarajatāyaḥkāṃsyopalānāṃ ca dvādaśāhāraparyāptamūlyānāṃ hartur dvādaśāhakaṇānnatvam | kārpāsakīṭajorṇānāṃ dvikhuraikakhurarajjupakṣigandhauṣadhīnāṃ trivārabhojanaparyāptamūlyārghyāṇāṃ hartuḥ payas tryaham | sarvatra hriyamāṇadravyālpatvabahutvābhyāṃ prāyaścittālpatvamahattve kalpye | sarvasteyaprāyaścittadravyaṃ svāmine sati datvaiva kāryam |
iti steyaprāyaścittam |
atha ṛṇānapākaraṇe —
tatra ṛṇam ātmapitṛpitāmahādikṛtam | tapobrahmacaryādyakaraṇe ṛṣīṇām ṛṇam | yajñākaraṇe devānām ṛṇam | prajotpattyakaraṇe pitṝṇām ṛṇam | eṣām ṛṇānām anapākaraṇe cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ śaktyā kāryam | sarvathā tadasaṃbhave ‘bdānte vaiśvānareṣṭiḥ kāryā | anāhitāgnitve tu saty adhikāritve ‘gnyādhāne ‘bdād arvāk pratimāsaṃ trirātram | tataḥ pratyabdaṃ cāndrādiṣv ekam anāpadi traimāsikam | pitary anāhitāgnāv ādhātary ayaṣṭari yaṣṭuś ca sutasya vrātyapaśuḥ kāryaḥ | aupāsanāgnyasaṃnidhau dvyabdaṃ pratimāsam upavāso ‘bde cāndrādiṣv ekam | yad vādyābde kṛcchro dvitīye ‘tikṛcchras tṛtīye kṛcchrātikṛcchrau tataś cāndram |
apaṇyānāṃ vikraye —
guḍatilapuṣpamūlaphalapakvānnavikraye saumyakṛcchram | lākṣālavaṇamadhumāṃsatailakṣīra-dadhighṛtatakragandhacarmavāsasāṃ vikraye cāndram | ūrṇākeśakesaribhūdehnuveśmaśastra-vikraye cāndram | abhakṣyamāṃsasnāyvasthiśṛṅganakhavikraye taptakṛcchraḥ | hiṅguguggula-haritālamanaḥśilāñjanagairikākṣāralavaṇamaṇimuktāpravālavaiṇavaveṇumṛnmayeṣu taptakṛcchraḥ | ārāmataḍāgodapānapuṣkariṇīsukṛtavikraye triṣavaṇasnāyy adhaḥśāyī caturtha-kālāhāro daśasahasraṃ japann abdena śudhyet | hīnamānonmānasaṃkīrṇavikraye caivam | evam anyaiḥ śaṅkhalikhitādyair uktaṃ [116] jñeyam | yatra prāyaścittaṃ noktaṃ tatrāpi cāndrādikam | anāpadi traimāsikam |
ity apaṇyavikrayaprāyaścittam [?] |
atha parivedanam —
tatrāmatyā parivettā cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ kṛtvā jyeṣṭhāya svoḍhāṃ striyaṃ datvā tenānujñātāṃ tām evodvahet | matyā cet tatrāpi kanyāpitrādyajñāne ‘bdaṃ kāryam | brāhmaṇagṛhabhaikṣāśanaṃ ca kṛtvaivaṃ kuryāt | kanyāpitrādidattodvāhe traimāsikam | kṛcchrātikṛcchrau vā kṛtvaiva kuryāt | parivedyasya tu dve kṛcchre kanyāyāḥ kṛcchro dātur atikṛcchro hotuś cāndram | evaṃ parivedyavat paryāhitaparījyaparivittānāṃ kāyadvayam | parivettṛparivittaparyādhātṛ-pariyaṣṭragredidhiṣūparividhiṣūpatīnāṃ cābdaṃ gautamokataṃ brahmacaryaṃ kṛtvā paścād vivāhaḥ | agredidhiṣūpatiḥ kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā tām evodvahet | didhiṣūpatis tu kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kṛtvā tasmai datvānujñātaḥ punas tām evodvahet | yad vāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ kāyaṃ kṛtvā tām eva jyeṣṭhāṃ paścād anyenoḍhām udvahet | didhiṣūpatis tu kṛcchrātikṛcchrau kṛtvā svoḍhāṃ jyeṣṭhāṃ kanīyasyāḥ pūrvavoḍhre datvānyām udvahet | jyeṣṭhe sthite kaniṣṭhaḥ kṛtavivāhaḥ parivettā parivividānaś ca sa jyeṣṭhaḥ parivittiḥ parivedyāś ca saḥ | parivibhāgavettṛparivibhāgavittyādeś caivam | jyeṣṭhāyāṃ kanyāyām anūḍhāyām anujoḍhā cet sāgredidhiṣūḥ pūrvā didhiṣūḥ | bhṛtakādhyāpako bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca payasā brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ trīn pakṣān niyataḥ pibet | cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ vā kuryāt | anuyogapradāne caivam | utkarṣahetor adhīyānasya kiṃ paṭhasi nāśitaṃ tvayety evaṃ paryanuyogo ‘nuyogapradānam | anuyogapradānābhyāse pātityam āhuḥ |
atha pāradārye —
tatra gurutalpatatsamādiṣu prāyaścittaviśeṣa uktaḥ | ṛtukāle jātimātrabrāhmaṇīgamane vārṣikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam | ṛtāv eva dharmakarma[?]sādhanatvādiguṇavatyāṃ brāhmaṇyāṃ gamane dvivārṣikam | tādṛśyām eva śrotriyapatnyāṃ traivārṣikam | tādṛśyām eva kṣatriyāyāṃ dvivārṣikam, tādṛśavaiśyāyāṃ vārṣikam, śūdrāyāṃ ṣāṇmāsikam | evaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi kṣatriyādiṣu dvivārṣikavārṣikaṣāṇmāsikāni | vaiśyasya ca vaiśyāśūdryor vārṣika[117]ṣāṇmāsike | śūdrasya ca śūdryāṃ parapatnyāṃ ṣāṇmāsikam eva | ananyapūrvikāsu cābhyāse dvādaśābdam | ekasyām eva gamanābhyāse pādapādanyūnaṃ syāt | amatyā sarvatrārdhaṃ yojyam | anṛtau jātimātrabrāhmaṇyāṃ traimāsikam | kṣatriyādistrīṣu dvaimāsikacāndramāsikāni | kṣatriyādīnāṃ kṣatriyādiṣu dvaimāsikāny eva | amatyā brāhmaṇādyā vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānaṃ māsaṃ pañcagavyaṃ māsaṃ kāyaṃ ca kramāt kuryuḥ | śūdrādamane tu māsaṃ kāyārdham | brāhmaṇaś ced aprekṣāpūrvakaṃ brāhmaṇabhāryāṃ śūdrām adhigacchen nivṛttadharmakarmaṇaḥ kṛcchram | anivṛttadharmakarmaṇo ‘tikṛcchraḥ | dvijātistrīviproḍhāsu dvis trir vyabhicaritāsv amatyā gamane caivam | matyā kṛcchradvayam | varṇastriyo ‘pi caturthe vyabhicāre svairiṇyaḥ pañcame bandhakyaḥ svairiṇīgamaneṣu śūdryāṃ sacailasnāta udakumbhaṃ viprāya dadyāt | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro viprān bhojayet | kṣatriyāyāṃ trirātropoṣito yavāḍhakaṃ dadyāt | bandhakīṣu tu brāhmaṇyāṃ kiṃcid dadyāt | kṣatriyāyāṃ ced dhanum, vaiśyāyāṃ cailakam, śūdrāyām udakumbhaṃ dadyāt vipraḥ | idaṃ ca prāyaścittaṃ garbhānutpattiviṣayam | garbhotpattau yad viśeṣeṇa prāyaścittam uktaṃ tad eva tatra dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | prātilomyagamane puṃso vadhaḥ | striyāḥ karṇādikartanam | svastrībhrāntyā prātilomyagamane brāhmaṇyāṃ śūdrasya dvādaśābdam | vaiśyādeḥ pādapādahrāsaḥ | atyantavyabhicāritabrāhmaṇyāṃ tu śūdrasya gomūtrayāvakam | vaiśyasya kṣatriyasya ca kṛcchraṃ sāntapanam | rajakacarmakārabhilla-vyādhaśailūṣanaṭaburuḍakaivartamedamlecchādyā ete rajakādyāḥ kāpālikāś ca tadvat | rajakādyantyajāgamane tv amatyā brāhmaṇasya parākaś cāndraṃ vā | matyā cāndradvayam | kṣatriyādīnāṃ pādapādahrāsaḥ | abhyāse kṛcchrābdam | retaḥsekāt prāṅ nivṛttau kāyam | cāṇḍālādyantyāvasāyistrīsaṃgamane tu gurutaraṃ prāyaścittaṃ gurutalpaprakaraṇe darśitam | cāṇḍālādiṣu garbhotpattau dvādaśābdārdhagurutalpavratam | strīṇām api savarṇānulomagamane yat puruṣoktaṃ traivārṣikādikaṃ tad eva kāryam | pratilomagamane tu śūdro brāhmaṇīṃ gacchec ced vīraṇair veṣṭayitvāgnau taṃ kṣipet | brāhmaṇyāṃ śiro vāpayitvā sarpiṣābhyajya nagnāṃ karam āropya mahāpathaṃ gamayet | sā pūtā bhavati | vaiśyaś cel lohitadarbhair veṣṭayitvāgnau kṣipet | brāhmaṇīṃ pūrvavad gamayet | [118] kṣatriyaś cec charapatrair veṣṭayitvāgnau nikṣipet | brāhmaṇīṃ pūrvavad gamayet | evaṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyaśūdrayor vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrasya caivaṃ strīpuṃsayoḥ prāyaścittam | yad vā brāhmaṇyāḥ kṣatriyagamane ‘tikṛcchro vaiśye kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | kṣatriyāyāṃ viprakṣatriyavaiśyagamaneṣv ardhakāyakāyātikṛcchrāṇi | śūdrāyāḥ śūdre kāyam | viprakṣatriyavaiśyeṣu tv ahorātratrirātrārdhakṛcchrāṇi | brāhmaṇyāḥ pratilomagamane garbhotpattau viśeṣaḥ | brāhmaṇyāḥ śūdrasaṃgame kṛcchraṃ cāndratrayaṃ ca | vaiśye kṛcchraṃ cāndradvayaṃ ca | kṣatriye kṛcchraṃ cāndraṃ ca | kṣatriyāyāḥ śūdrasaṃgame krcchraṃ cāndradvayaṃ ca | vaiśye kṛcchraṃ cāndraṃ ca | vaiśyāyāḥ śūdrasaṃgame kṛcchraṃ cāndraṃ ca | vaiśye kṛcchram | garbhe prabhūte tu brāhmaṇyā vipragarbhe tu parākaḥ | kṣatragarbhe cāndram | vaiśagarbhe cāndraṃ parākaṃ ca | sūdragarbhe tu cāṇḍālatvāt tyāgaḥ | ṛtukāladoṣair garbhasrāve cāndratrayam | matyā gamane tu parākādi dviguṇam | dvijabhāryāḥ śūdreṇa saṃgatā anisṛtagarbhāḥ prasūyante cet prāyaścittābhāvaḥ | garbhadhāraṇakāle śūdrasaṃgame tu prasavottarakālam eva prāyaścittaṃ kāryam anyathā garbhabādhā syāt | tac ca māsaṃ yāvakam | sa garbho doṣābhāvāt saṃskāryaḥ | auddhatyāt prāyaścittākaraṇe striyāḥ karṇādikartanaṃ kāryam | antyajagamane ‘pi strīṇām | tatra brāhmaṇyā amatyā rajakādyantyajagamane cāndratrayam | cāṇḍālādyantyāvasāyisaṃgame tv amatyā brāhmaṇyāś cāndracatuṣkam | matyā dviguṇam | garbhiṇyāḥ paścād antyasaṃame ‘pi prasūtāyā evaṃ prāyaścittam | sā gṛhe na pracaret | na bhartrā saha śayīta | na bāndhavaiḥ saha bhuñjīta | na dharmakarmasādhanam | sā kṛcchrābdaṃ caret | hiraṇyaṃ dhenur vā dakṣiṇā | matyāntyasaṃparke tu sā pradīpte ‘gnau praviśya mṛtā śudhyet | prāyaścittam akurvāṇāḥ puṃliṅgenāṅkanīyā vadhyā vā syuḥ |
iti smṛtyarthasāre pāradāryaprāyaścittam |
parivitter api parivettṛprāyaścittavat | tatra kṛcchrātikṛcchrasthāne ‘tra kāyam | vārdhuṣye cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ jātiśaktiguṇādyapekṣayā yojyam | lavaṇakriyāyāṃ caivam |
atha strīśudraviṭkṣatravadheṣu —
atrāmatyā jātimātrastrīkṣatravaiśyaśūdravadheṣu krameṇa brahmahatyāvrataṃ traivārṣikavārṣikaṣāṇmāsikāni kuryāt | yad vā vṛṣa[119]bhaikasahasrā gā vṛṣabhaikaśatā gā vṛṣabhaikādaś gāḥ savatsā dhenur dadyāt | īṣadvṛttasthakṣatrādivadheṣu brahmahatyāvratacaturthāṃśāṣṭāṃśaṣoḍaśāṃśavratāni | samyagvṛttasthavadheṣv ekāṃśādhikānīmāni | sadācāragurupūjāghṛṇāśaucendriyanigrahabhūtahitādiguṇayuktatvaṃ vṛttasthatvam | matyā ṣaḍvārṣikatraivārṣikavārṣikāṇi | śrotriyakṣatrādivadheṣu navaṣaṭtrivarṣāṇi vratāni | vratasthaśrotriyakṣatrādivadheṣu daśavārṣikam | prārabdhasomayāgastha-śrotriyakṣatrādivadhe dvādaśābdam | somayāgasthaśrotriyakṣatrādivadhe ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ brahmacaryam ṛṣabhaikādaśagodānasahitaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ ca gautamoktam | durvṛttakṣatrādivadhe matyā traimāsikadvaimāsikacāndrāṇi | amatyā trirātropavāsasahitam ṛṣabhaikādaśasahitagodānaṃ pañcagavyamāsaṃ payomāsaṃ ca kuryāt | idaṃ prāyaścittajātaṃ brāhmaṇasya kartur jñeyam | kṣatriyādeḥ kartuḥ pādapādanyūnaṃ jñeyam | mūrdhāvasiktādīnāṃ vadhe caivaṃ prāyaścittaṃ tatra tatrohyam | sarvatra daṇḍavṛddhihrāsābhyāṃ prāyaścittavṛddhihrāsau vijñeyau |
atha strīvadhe —
tatra brāhmaṇyādistrīṇāṃ prātilomyenāntyajātiprasūtānāṃ brāhmaṇādibhāryāṇāṃ svairiṇīnām amatyā vadhe tu jalādhāracarmakośaṃ dṛtiṃ dhanuś chāgameṣān kramād dadyāt | matyā ṣaṭcaturdvyekamāsāḥ kāryāḥ | sutādīnāṃ vadhe caivam | veśyākarmaṇā jīvantīnāṃ vadhe kiṃcid eva jalaṃ dadyāt | prātilomyena vyabhicāritabrāhmaṇyādistrīvadhakartṝṇāṃ kṣatriyādīnāṃ govadhoktaprāyaścittam yathārhaṃ yojyam | brāhmaṇīnāṃ vadhe tv amatyā ṣāṇmāsikaṃ daśadhenudānaṃ vā | kṣatriyādivadhe traimāsikatattadardhāni | matyā brāhmaṇyādiṣu dviguṇam | yad vā vaiśyāyāṃ dhenudānam | śūdrāyāṃ cāndrādiṣv ekam | dharmakarmasādhanabrāhmaṇy-ādivadhe tu brāhmaṇyāṃ ṣaḍvarṣaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryam, kṣatriyāyāṃ traivāriṣikam, vaiśyāyāṃ sārdhaṃ varṣam, śūdrāyāṃ navamāsam | amatyā sarvatrārdham | ātreyyāṃ prāyaścittam uktam |
athānyahiṃsāyām —
tatrāsthimatāṃ kṛkalāsādīnām anuktaniṣkṛtīnāṃ sahasravadhe vānasthimatāṃ kṣodiṣṭhānāṃ yūkāmaśakamatkuṇadaṃśādīnāṃ śakaṭapūrṇavadhe ca ṣāṇmāsikaṃ prākṛtaṃ brahmacaryaṃ daśadhenudānaṃ vā | tatpramāṇādhike ‘dhikaṃ kalpyam | tato vā kṛśāsthiprāṇivadhe kiṃcid deyam | aṣṭamu[120]ṣṭimitaṃ dhānyaṃ kiṃcit | kiṃcid aṣṭau tu puṣkalam | puṣkalāni tu catvāri pūrṇapātram āḍhakaṃ ca | caturāḍhakaṃ droṇaṃ droṇātmikā khārikā | hiraṇye tu paṇaṃ kiṃcit | anasthike kṣodiṣṭhe prāṇāyāmaḥ | sthaviṣṭhānasthighuṇādiprāṇivadhe tu taptayāvakaṃ tryaham | phalapuṣpānnarasajātaprāṇivadhe ghṛtāhāro ‘horātram | mārjāragodhānakulamaṇḍūkacāṣa-kākolūkādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ payas trirātraṃ pādakṛcchraṃ yojanagamanaṃ vā | sravantyāṃ samudraganadyāṃ snānaṃ vā | abdaivatasūktajapo vā | etāni pratyekaṃ trirātraṃ kuryat | ekavarṣavatso vā deyaḥ | matyā dviguṇam | abhyāse kāyam | eṣāṃ samuditānāṃ vadhe kāyaṃ ṣāṇmāsikam | gaje pañcanīlavṛṣā deyāḥ | haye varavastrayugmam | uṣṭre guñjābhāraḥ suvarṇaguñjā vā | khare vṛṣa ekavarṣavatso vā deyaḥ | aje ‘naḍvān vṛṣo vā | meṣe caivam | haṃsasārasapārāvatamayūracakravākabakabalākāviṣajñakaṅkaśvādiṭkāraṇḍavaśyenabhāsarāja-pakṣādiṣu gaur deyā | gṛdhrakākolūkakapotakukkuṭabṛhatpakṣādiṣu trivarṣo vatsaḥ śukacāṣa-khañjarīṭalāvakasārikādiṣu dvivarṣo vatsaḥ | ṭiṭṭibhatittiramaṅgvāhikādikṣudrapakṣiṣv ekavarṣo vatsaḥ | kravyādavyāghraśṛgālādimṛgavadheṣu vānare ca haṃsaśyenakaṅkagṛdhrādiṣu jalacarabalākādipakṣiṣu sthalacarakākādiṣu bhāse mayūre caiteṣāṃ pratyekavadhe gaur deyā | akravyādahariṇādimṛgeṣu khañjarīṭādipakṣiṣu ca vatsatarī deyā sarīsṛpeṣv ayodaṇḍas tīkṣṇobhayāgraḥ | yad vā śuke dvivarṣo vatsaḥ | krauñce trivarṣaḥ | mṛgapakṣiṣu napuṃsakavadhe trapusīsakaṃ māṣamānaṃ deyam palālabhāro vā sūkare ghṛtakumbhaḥ | tittirau tiladroṇaḥ | eṣu dānāśaktau dravyakalpanayā tatsamasya kṛcchrādi kāryam | yad vā dānāśaktau gajagaṇḍabhārasāśvoṣṭrakharagauragavayaṛṣyamahiṣameṣādiṣv amatyā pratyekavadhe sārdhakṛcchraḥ | samastavadhe cāndram | matyā pratyekavadhe cāndram | samastavadhe tv āvṛttiḥ | hariṇasāraṅgaruruvarāhasiṃhagaṇḍakesarivyāghramakaramahāmatsyagrāha-śiṃśumārādīnām amatyā pratyekaṃ kṛcchraḥ | samastavadhe kṛcchradvayam | matyā pratyekavadhe caivam | samastavadhe cāndram | śvavṛkavānarajambūkaviḍvarāhādiṣv amatyā pratyekaṃ trirātram | samastavadhe kṛcchraḥ | matyā prayekavadhe caivam | samastavadhe dviguṇam | mārjārasarpājagaradundubhanakulamaṇḍūkamūṣakakarkaṭaśalabhasedhāgodhā-śālyakakūrmaśaśādiṣv amatyā pratyekavadhe pādakṛcchraḥ | samastavadhe kṛcchraḥ | matyā pratyekaṃ caivam | [121] haṃsādiṣv amatyā pratyekavadhe kṛcchraḥ | gṛdhrādiṣu pādanyūnakṛcchraḥ | śukādiṣv ardhakṛcchraḥ | matyā pratyekavadhe caivam | ṭiṭṭibhādiṣu pādakṛcchraḥ | samastavadhādau tac ca dviguṇaṃ sarvatra | ekahantṛviṣaya evam | hantṛdvitve tu pratyekaṃ tatra taroktārdhaṃ syāt | hantṛbahutve tu gajādiṣu pratyekaṃ kṛcchraḥ | hariṇādiṣu trirātram | śvādiṣu dvyaham | mārjārādiṣūpavāsaḥ | ṭiṭṭibhādiṣu naktam | mṛtaprāyeṣv amatyā pratyekavadhe tu gajādiṣu trirātram | hariṇādiṣu dvyaham | kharādiṣu caturthakālaḥ | mārjārādiṣūpavāsaḥ | gṛdhrādiṣu naktam | śukādiṣv ekabhaktam | ṭiṭṭibhādiṣu jale prāṇāyāmaḥ | avijñātasarvamṛgapakṣiṣu trirātram – ityādideśakālajātiśaktiguṇādyapekṣayā guruviṣaye laghuviṣaye ca yojyam |
iti hiṃsāprāyaścittam |
atha vṛkṣacchedanaprāyaścittam —
vṛkṣagulmalatāvīrudhāṃ phalapuṣpādibhir upayogināṃ chedane gāyatryādīnām ṛcāṃ śataṃ japitavyam | tāvadgāyatrījapo vā | oṣadhīnāṃ grāmyāraṇyānāṃ vṛthaiva chedane divā goparicaryāṃ kṛtvā rātrau kṣīraṃ pītvaiva svapet | pañcamahāyajñādyadṛṣṭārthatvacchedane na doṣaḥ | kṛṣyādyupakaraṇādidṛṣṭārthatve ca na doṣaḥ | caityaśmaśānasīmāpuṇyasthāna-devālayasthe vṛkṣe ‘nyasmin prakhyāte vā vṛkṣe chedana ṛkśatajapo dviguṇaṃ daṇḍānusāreṇa prayaścittaṃ kāryam | sarvatra śūdrasya japasthāne daṇḍānusāreṇa dvirātrādi kalpyam | vṛkṣacchedābhyāse cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ kāryam |
iti vṛkṣacchedaprāyaścittam |
atha śvamārjārādidaṣṭeṣu —
śvavṛkaśṛgālakharoṣṭraviḍvarāhakākakukkuṭavānarapuṃścalīkravyādāntyajātibhir daṣṭaḥ śaktaś cen nābher adhaḥ payasā dvirātram | aganihotry ekarātram | brahmacārī trirātram | nābher ūrdhvaṃ dviguṇam | vaktre triguṇam | mastake caturguṇam | yad vottamāṅge samudraganadīsnānaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ ca kṛtvā ghṛtenaikarātram | aśaktasya jale prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā ghṛtenaikarātram | atyaśaktau gatvā brāhmaṇān praṇipatya tair nirīkṣitaḥ śudhyet | atyantāśaktau hiraṇyodakamiśraṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya śudhyet | īṣaddaṣṭe tu nābher adhaś ced āpohiṣṭhīyādibhiḥ snānaṃ prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ ca | nābher ūrdhvaṃ dviguṇādi | mārjāramūṣakaśvādinakulāśvājamahiṣīhīnajātyāraṇyamṛgair daṣṭe jale daśa prāṇāyāmāḥ | īṣaddaṣṭasya pañca | kṣatriyādeḥ pādapādanyūnam | śūdrasya tūpavāsena godānena gomithunadānena vāmantrakaprāṇāyāmena [122] vā śuddhiḥ | brāhmaṇī daṣṭā snātvoditaṃ grahanakṣatraṃ dṛṣṭvā śudhyet | kṛcchrādivratasthā daṣṭā trirātram upoṣya saghṛtaṃ yāvakaṃ bhuktvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet | brāhmaṇyanusāreṇa kṣatriyādistrīṇāṃ caivam | rajasvalā śvādidaṣṭā pañcadaśarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyati | nābher ūrdhvaṃ mastake ca dvitricaturguṇādi yojyam | śvādighrātasya cāvalīḍhasya ca nakhair vilikhitasya cādbhiḥ prakṣālanam agninā copatāpanam | śvādidaṃśaśastraghātādijanitavraṇe kṛmijanane gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa trisaṃdhyaṃ snānam ācaret | nābher ūrdhvaṃ cet trirātraṃ pañcagavyāśī syāt | tata ā kaṇṭhāt ṣaḍrātram | śirovraṇe tu kāyam | tatra śvādidaṃśavraṇe taddaṃśaprāyaścittā-nantaram idaṃ kṛmijanananimittaṃ prāyaścittaṃ kāryam | śastrādivraṇe tv evam eva tryahaṃ pañcagavyāśanādikam | kṣatriyādiṣu pādapādahrāsaḥ | anyavraṇe kṛmidoṣe tūpavāso hiraṇyaṃ ca datvā kṛcchreṇa śuddhiḥ | ninditārthopajīvane cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā | nāstikyaṃ vedoktakarmanindanam | tena jīvanaṃ nāstikyavṛttitvam | nāstikye sakṛtkṛte kṛcchraḥ | nāstikyāvṛttāv atikṛcchraḥ | abhyāse cāndrādiṣv ekam | atyantābhiniveśena bahukālābhyāse pañcābdaṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣacaryā |
atha brahmalope prāyaścittam —
tatropakurvāṇo naiṣṭhikaś ca traivarṇikabrahmacārī striyaṃ gatvāvakīrṇī bhavati tatra gurutalpe tatsame ca guruprāyaścittam uktam | tato ‘nyastriyaṃ gatvā sa nairṛtadaivatena rakṣodaivatena caikākṣagardabhena paśunāraṇye catuṣpathe rātrau laukike ‘gnau pākayajña-dharmeṇāśvalāyanādipaśukalpatantreṇeṣṭvā tasyājinam ūrdhvavālaṃ paridhāya triṣavaṇasnāyī lohitapātraḥ svakarmācakṣāṇaḥ saptagṛhabhaikṣāṇy ekavārāśī vatsareṇa śubhyet | evam aśaktau tu nairṛtaṃ caruṃ nirupya paktvā kāmāya svāhā nirṛtyai svāhā rakṣodevatābhyaḥ svāheti hutvā homaśeṣaṃ samāpayet | ambuvrataṃ caritvā śudhyet | idaṃ vārṣikavratam aśrotriyapatnyāṃ vaiśyāyāṃ śrotriyapatnyāṃ vā kāryam | guṇavatyāṃ ca kṣatriyāyāṃ śrotriyapatnyāṃ vaiśyāyāṃ śrotriyapatyāṃ vā kāryam | gunavatyāṃ ca kṣatriyāyāṃ śrotriyapatyāṃ dvivārṣikam | guṇavatyāṃ brāhmaṇyāṃ trivārṣikam | amatyāvakīrṇī vārṣikasthāne brahmahatyāvrataṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ cīravāsāś caret | īṣadvyabhicāriṇyāṃ caivam | dvivārṣikādiṣv amatyārdham | atyantavyabhicāritāsu strīṣu śūdryāṃ sacailaṃ snātvodakumbhaṃ dadyāt | vaiśyāyāṃ caturthakālāhāro brāhmaṇān bhojayed yavasabhāraṃ ca [123] gobhyo dadyāt | kṣatriyāyāṃ tu trirātropoṣito ghṛtapātraṃ dadyāt | brāhmaṇyāṃ ṣaḍrātropoṣito gāṃ dadyāt | goṣv avakīrṇaḥ kāyam | ṣaṇḍhāyāṃ palalabhāraṃ sīsaṃ māṣaṃ ca dadyāt | strīsaṃbhogaṃ vinā matyā reto visṛjya nairṛtayāgamātram | divā svapne caivam | kṛcchracāndrādivrateṣv atidiṣtabrahmacaryeṣu retaḥskandane caitad eva yāgamātram | svapne retaḥskandane brahmacārī snātvārkam arcayitvā punar mām aitv indriyam ity ṛcaṃ trir japet | vānaprastho yatiś ca matyā brahmacaryalope parākatrayayuktam avakīrṇivrataṃ caret | amatyā kṛcchratrayam uktam | saṃnyastasya gārhapatyaparigrahe ṣāṇmāsikaṃ kṛcchraṃ kṛtvā punar jātakarmādisarvasaṃskāraiḥ śuddhiḥ punaḥsaṃnyāsena | kṣatriyasya cāndradvayam | vaiśyasya kṛcchratrayam | yad vaitāni brāhmaṇasyaiva śaktisakṛdabhyāsāpekṣayā yojyāni | anāśakanivṛttānāṃ caivam | maraṇasaṃnyāsināṃ tu jalāgnyudbandhanabhraṣṭānāṃ pravrajyānāśakacyutānāṃ viṣaprapa(ta)naprāptaśastrāghātacyutānāṃ ca cāndraṃ taptakṛcchradvayaṃ ca śaktyādyapekṣayā jñeyam | ātmatyāgādyaśāstrīyamaraṇādhyavasitasya tāvanmātre trirātram | śastrādikṣate kṛcchraḥ | dṛḍhakṣate cāndram | śastrādimṛtasya putrādayaś cāndraṃ taptakṛcchradvayaṃ ca kuryuḥ |
ity [atha?] avakīrṇiprāyaścittam |
brahmacārī tv anāturo guruśuśrūṣādigurutarakāryavyagratayā saptarātraṃ bhaikṣyasyāgnikāryasya vā lope kāmāc cāvakīrṇo ‘smy avakīrṇo ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāhā kāmam avapanno ‘smy avapanno ‘smi kāmakāmāya svāhety ābhyām ājyāhutīr hutvā,
saṃ mā siñcantu marutaḥ sam indraḥ saṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃ māyam agniḥ siñcatv āyuṣā ca balena ca ||
ity anenopatiṣṭheta | avyagratayā lope ‘vakīrṇivratam | upanayanānantaraṃ vratamadhye yajñopavītādīnāṃ nāśe vastraṃ sūtrāntaraṃ vā dhṛtvā manojyotir ityādibhir manoliṅgābhis tvam agne vratapā asītyādivrataliṅgābhiś catasra ājyāhutīr hutvā vidhinā dhārayet | asadbhaikṣyabojane ‘bhyudite ‘stamite vānte divāsvapne nagnastrīdarśane nagnasvāpe śmaśānam ākramya hayādīṃś cāruhya svapūjyān atikramyāgnikāryalope caitair juhuyāt | maṇivāsogavādīnāṃ prati[124]grahe sāvitryaṣṭasahasraṃ japet | sthāvarajaṅgamavṛkṣa-valmīkapaśusarīsṛpādiprāṇivadhe kūṣmāṇḍībhir homaḥ | yajñopavītaṃ vinā yajñopavitam anyathā kṛtvā vā bhojane viṇmūtrotsarge vā gāyatryaṣṭasahasreṇa saprāṇāyāmena śuddhiḥ | pane triḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | nagnabhakṣaṇe ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | bhojane mehane caivam | yajñopavīta-mekhalājinadaṇḍānāṃ lope vyāhṛtihomaṃ ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā punar dhārayet | saṃdhyāgnikāryalope snātvāṣṭasahasrajapaḥ | bhikṣāṭanam akṛtvā svasthasyaikānnāśane ‘ṣṭaśatajapaḥ | bhikṣāṃ yācitvaikānnāśane na doṣaḥ | guruśuśrūṣādilope ‘ṣṭaśatajapaḥ | madhubhakṣaṇe medhyamāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca kṛcchraḥ | matyā parākaḥ | abhyāse dviguṇaṃ punaḥsaṃskāras tato vratasamāpanam | tad eva vaidyakārthe gurūcchiṣṭaṃ deyam | tato nīrujo bhūtvā haṃsaḥ śuciṣad ity ādityam upatiṣṭheta | ājñāvighātādi gurupratikūlaṃ karmācaran praṇipātādinā prasādyaiva śudhyet | coravyāghrādibhayākulapradeśe mahāndhakāre rātrau guruṇā svakāryārthaṃ preṣitaḥ śiṣyo daivān mṛtaś cet sa guruḥ kṛcchratrayaṃ kṛtvā śudhyet | sarvatrārogyārtham auṣadhapathyānnapradāne tadarthayatnena mṛte na kaścid doṣaḥ |
atha mithyābhiśaṃsane —
yas tu brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ mahāpātakādyair mithyābhiśaṃsati sa māsam ambubhakṣo niyatendriyaḥ śuddhavatīmantrajapaśīlaḥ śudhyet | kṣatriyādyabhiśaṃsane ‘rdhārdhahānena kuryāt | kṣatriyādyā brāhmaṇābhiśaṃsane dvitricaturguṇaṃ kuryuḥ | śūdrasya japasthāne ‘mantrāḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | bhūtābhiśaṃsane tattadardham | atipātakābhiśaṃsināṃ pādonam | anupātakābhiśaṃsināṃ tadardham | upapātakābhiśaṃsināṃ tato nyūnam | mithyābhiśastaḥ kṛcchraṃ cāgneyaṃ puroḍhāśaṃ vāyavyaṃ paśuṃ vā kuryāt | atipātakādiṣu pādapādahrāsaḥ | jyeṣṭhabhrātuḥ kaniṣṭabhrātur vā bhāryām aniyukto ‘matyā gatvā cāndram | matyā saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇagṛhe bhaikṣyaṃ caret | rajasvalāṃ svabhāryām amatyā sakṛd gatvā trirātraṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyet | abhyāse saptarātram | matyā sakṛc cet saptarātram | abhyāse kṛcchraḥ | antyantāvicchinnābhyāse traivārṣikam |
atha rajasvalānāṃ parasparasparśane vakṣyate —
rajasvale dve savarṇaikabhartṛke amatyā vā matyā parasparaṃ sparśe sadyaḥ snāte śudhyataḥ | asapatnyos tu sarvarṇayor [125] yonigotrasaṃbandhayor amatyā sparśe snānamātram | matyā tv ekarātraṃ nirāhāratvaṃ pañcagavyāśanatvaṃ ca | asaṃbandhayoḥ savarṇayoḥ sparśane tv amatyā snātvā dinānte rātryante vā śuddhiḥ | matyā sparśe tv ā śuddher nāśnīyāt | bhuktau pratidinam upavāsas tāvaddānaṃ vā | asavarṇayos tu sparśe brāhmaṇīśūdrayor matyā sparśe brāhmaṇyāḥ kṛcchraḥ | śūdrāyāḥ pādakṛcchras tāvaddānaṃ vā | brāhmaṇīkṣatriyayoḥ sparśe brāhmaṇyāḥ kṛcchrārdham | kṣatriyāyāḥ pādakṛcchraḥ | kṣatriyāśūdrayoḥ sparśe kṣatriyāyās trirātram | vaiśyāyā ahorātram | vaiśyāśudrayoḥ sparśe ca vaiśyāyās trirātram | śūdrāyāś caturthakāla āhāraḥ | eṣāṃ varṇastrīṇāṃ matyā sparśe śuddhiḥ | amatyā sparśe tu savarṇām adhikavarṇāṃ vā spṛṣṭvā snāyād eva | hīnavarṇāṃ spṛṣṭvā snātvāśuddher nāśnīyāt | yadi bhuṅkte paścāt pratibhojanam upavāsas tāvaddānaṃ vā | viprabhojanaṃ vā pratyāmnāyaṃ vā kuryāt | cāṇḍālādyantāvasāyipatitaśavādīnāṃ matyā sparśane tadādyahāny abhuñjānātikramya prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | tac ca prathame ‘hni sparśe trirātram | dvitīye dvyaham | tṛtīye tv ahorātram | parato naktam | prathamādidine yadi bhuṅkte paścāc cāndraṃ pādapādanyūnaṃ dinānusāreṇa kuryāt | amatyā cāṇḍālādisparśe tu tadādyanāhārā kālenaiva śudhyet | tatra prathamādidine bhuktau pratyāmnāyaḥ | matyā rajakādisparśe tu cāṇḍālādisparśavat kiṃ tu prathamādidine bhuktau kṛcchradvayam ardhārdhanyūnaṃ kuryāt | amatyā rajakādisparśe ca tadādye bhuñjānā kālenaiva śuddhā | bhuktau pratyāmnāyaḥ | śvavṛkaśṛgālavānarakharoṣṭraviḍvarāhakākakukkuṭa-pañcanakhocchiṣṭaśūdrāṇāṃ matyā sparśane tadādyahāny abhuñjānā nītvā prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | prathame ‘hni sparśe dvyaham | dvitīye ‘horātram | tṛtīye naktam | tata ekabhaktam | tatra prathamādidine bhuktau kāyaṃ pādapādanyūnaṃ kuryāt | amatyā śvādisparśe tadādyabhuñjānaikakālena śudhyet | bhuktau pratyāmnāyaḥ | cāṇḍālarajakarajasvalādi-saṃspṛṣṭānāṃ matyā sparśane hīnāmedhyādimalocchiṣṭasparśane ca tadādyahāny abhuñjānātikramya prathame ‘hni sparśe tūpavāsam | dvitīye naktam | tṛtīye tv ekabhaktam | prathamādidine bhuktau tridvyekarātropavāsanaktaikabhaktāni | amatyā tatspṛ[126]ṣṭasparśane ‘bhuktvā kālena śudhyet | bhuktau pratyāmnāyaḥ | aśaktāv amatyā caṇḍālarajakaśvādisparśane sadyaḥ snātvā śucir ā candradarśanāt | atyaśaktau snātvā divaivādyāt | ucchiṣṭayo rajasvalayor amatyā sparśane brāhmaṇī kṛcchreṇa trirātreṇa śudhyet | śūdrī dānair upoṣitā | anyavarṇastrīṇām anucchiṣṭānāṃ matyā parasparasparśane yat prāyaścittaṃ tad evocchiṣṭānām amatyā sparśane ‘pi matyocchiṣṭayoḥ sparśane tattaddviguṇam | ucchiṣṭān dvijān rajasvalā spṛṣṭā ced adhastanocchiṣṭeṣūpavāsaḥ | ūrdhvocchiṣṭasparśane trirātram | rajasvalā bhuñjānocchiṣṭā ca caṇḍālādisparśane tadādyahāny abhuñjānā nītvā prathame ‘hni sparśe gomūtrayāvakaṃ ṣaḍrātraṃ dvitīye catūrātraṃ tṛtīye dvirātraṃ caturthe tūpavāsam kuryāt | (* atra prathamādidine bhuktau kāyottaraṃ cāndram | ucchiṣṭā rajasvalā rajakādīn spṛṣṭvā tadādy abhuñjānaiva sthitvā prathamādidine sparśe pañcarātrādyekaikadinahrāsaṃ kuryāt |*) atra prathamādidine bhuktau trirātrottaraṃ kāryam | śvādisparśe (*tadādy abhuktvā catūrātrādi | prathamādidine bhuktvopavāsottaraṃ trirātram | caṇḍālarajakādisaṃspṛṣṭasparśane*) tadādy abhuktaiva trirātrādi | prathamādidine bhuktau pratyāmnāyaḥ | sarvatrāśaktau kāñcanaṃ dadyāt | viprebhyo bhojanaṃ cānyad vā tatsamaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ śuddhā bhavatv iti vācayet | prāyaścitte samutpanne rajodṛṣṭau tv evam | sapatnīṃ rajasvalāṃ spṛṣṭvā rajodṛṣṭau sadyaḥ snātvā śudhyet | sapatnīṃ savarṇām adhikavarṇāṃ vā spṛṣṭvā rajodṛṣṭāv ekarātraṃ nirāhāratvam | hīnavarṇāṃ spṛṣṭvā rajodṛṣṭāv ā śuddher abhuktvādṛṣṭau pañcagavyāśanaṃ ca | caṇḍālādisūtikāśavatatparicārādisparśe rajodṛṣṭāv ā śuddher abhuktvā paścāc cāndram | rakajādisparśe rajodrṣṭāv ā śuddher abhuktvā paścāt kāyam | śvādisparśe rajodṛṣṭau trirātram | anyāspṛśyopahatau rajo drṣṭvopavāsaḥ | aprāyatye samutpanne rajodṛṣṭau sarvatra bālāpatyāyā abhiṣeke kṛte bhuktiḥ syāt | paścād aśanapratyāmnāyo vā | kṛcchracāndrādivratasthāyāḥ snāne naimittike prāpte rajodṛṣṭau pātrāntaritatoyena snānaṃ tato 'dbhiḥ siktagātrā vastraniṣpīḍanam anyavastradhāraṇaṃ vā kṛtvā vrataṃ samācaret | śāvāśauce prasavāśauce vā madhya [127] ṛtudarśane tv asnātvā bhojanaṃ śuddhāv upavāsaḥ | ārtavamadhye jātamṛtāśauce tu snātvā bhojanam | rajasvalāyā vratasthāyā rajasvalābhibhāṣaṇe tūpavāsaḥ | rajasvalāṃ spṛṣṭvā bhuktvā rajasvalāsnānakālaṃ nāśnīyāt | vratasthāyāḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | rajasvalā śvādidaṣṭā pañcarātraṃ nirāhārā pañcagavyena śudhyet | prārabdhe dīrghavrate rajodṛṣṭau vratalopo nāsti | haviṣyabhojanādi kuryāt | divārcanādikaṃ ca kārayet | śuddhikāle tv asnātvā bhuktau kāyaṃ matyaḥ cāndram | śeṣaṃ bhakṣyābhakṣyaprāyaścittaṃ vakṣyate | rajasvalā tv añjanābhyañjanasnānapracāradantadhāvananakhanikṛntana-rajjusparśavyāpāratāmbūlamadhumāṃsagandhapuṣpadivāsvāpagrahanakṣatranirīkṣaṇādi varjayet | pāṇau mṛnmaye vā kharpare vā bhuñjītetyādiniyamasthā ca | atra sarvatra snānaṃ sacailam |
iti rajasvalāvidhiḥ |
atha sutānāṃ vikraye —
tatra kanyāvikraye devagṛhapratiśrayodyānārāmapuṣkariṇīsabhāprapāpuṇyasetuvikraye ca cāndrādiṣv ekam | atha vā traimasikam | āpadi taptakṛcchraḥ | atyantāpadi sāntapanam | putravikraye sarvaṃ dviguṇam | ekāpatyaputravikraye triṣavaṇasnāyy adhaḥśāyī caturthakālāśī vatsareṇa śudhyet | kanyāvikraye tadardhena śuddhiḥ |
athāyājyayājane —
tatra vrātyayājakaś ca vrātyopanetādhyāpakaś ca hīnayājakaḥ śūdrāntyeṣṭikarmayājakaś ca vedavikrayī taskaravyatiriktaśaraṇāgataghātī taskarātyāgī cābhicārī vā matyā kāyādiṣu yogyaṃ kṛcchrāditrayaṃ kuryāt | matyā vrātyasyopanayanādhyāpanayājana uddālakavrataṃ kuryāt | tac coktaṃ vrātyaprāyaścitteṣu | atikrāntopanayano vrātyaḥ | dvyahādir dvādaśāhānto ‘hargaṇo yajño ‘hīnaḥ | āvratāvyabhicāre(?) doṣam āhuḥ | brāhmaṇādyantyeṣṭikarmasu lobhād yājane kāyātikṛcchrataptakṛcchrāḥ | abhyāse dviguṇam | atyantābhyāse triguṇam | śūdrādyayājyayājane tv amatyā cāndrādiṣv ekam | aśaktau kṛcchraḥ | matyā traimāsikam | abhyāse dviguṇādi | parivedakādiyājane caivam | parvacāṇḍālādiśrotrāvakāśādyanadhyāyeṣv adhyayanaṃ vedavipalvaḥ | utkarṣahetor adhīyānasya kiṃ paṭhasi nāśitaṃ tvayety evaṃ paryanuyogadānaṃ ca vedaviplavaḥ | cāṇḍālādipatitaśravaṇe vedapāṭha [128] upavāsaḥ | matyā trirātram abhyāse kṛcchraḥ | atyantābhyāse kṛcchratrayam | smṛtidharmaśāstravyākhyāne tu tadardham | nityānadhyāyeṣv adhyayane yāvad adhītaṃ tāvaj jale ‘ghamarṣaṇavat svādhyāyaṃ japet | aśaktau japed anaghamarṣaṇavat | atyaśaktau sthale japet | tāvad anyamantraṃ vā japet | matyā pratyanadhyāyam upavāsaḥ | aśuddhyadhyayane caivam | naimittikānadhyāye ‘dhyayane caivam | durbodhasya tadardham | grāmakukkuṭamūṣakamaṇḍūkādyantarāgamane naktam | gavāśvamahiṣādipaśustrīśūdrādyantarāgamana upavāsaḥ | mārjārasarpanakulapañcanakha-jātyantrāgamane trirātraṃ trikālasnānaṃ ca | śvavṛkaśṛgālavānararajakādīnām antarāgamane ‘dhyayane kāyam | kharavarāhoṣṭrādicaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavādyantarāgamane kāyatrayam | gauragavayājādibrahmodgatanāstikādīnāṃ gamane traimāsikam | śaśameṣaśvapākādigamane ṣāṇmāsikam | gajagaṇḍasārasavyāghramahāpātakikṛtaghnādigamane ‘dhyayane kṛcchrābdaṃ tāvatkālam anadhyāyaś ca | sarvatra matyārdham | antarāgamane tadānīm eva svādhyāyaviramaṇe na prāyaścittam ity eka ityādi ca jñeyam | pitṛmātṛgurūṇām akāraṇatyāge cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā | adhikaṃ māsaṃ ṣaṣṭhānnakālatvaṃ saṃhitājapo vādhikaḥ | sutatyāge cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā bāndhavatyāge caivam | kanyādūṣaṇe savarṇānāṃ cāndraṃ traimāsikam | asavarṇānām ānulomye payomāsaḥ kāyaṃ vā | prātilomye kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ kṛcchrābdam | śūdrasya vadha evam | ātyantābhyāse savarṇasyāpi kṛcchrābdam | somavikrayī vṛṣalīpatiḥ kaumāradāraparityāgī śūdrayājako guroḥ pratihantā surāmadyapo brāhmaṇavṛttighnaḥ kūṭavyavahārī patitavyavahārī mitradhruk pratirūpakavṛttiś cāmatyā traimāsikavrataṃ matyā ṣāṇmāsikam abhyāse kṛcchrābdaṃ kuryāt | devatādyārādhanārthaṃ gṛhītavratalope ‘nyasmin vratalope vātmārthaṃ pāke ca madyapastrīniṣevaṇe ca cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikam | adhītādhyayanatyāge vyasanāsaktyā kṛte brahmahatyāsamānavat | sacchrāstrādyabhyāsāsaktyā bahukuṭumbarakṣātyāge cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā | atyantāpadi brahmojjhasya kāyam |
athāgnityāge —
nāstikyād agnityāge māsadvaye kāyaṃ kṛtvā punaḥsaṃdhānam | māsacatuṣṭaye ‘tikṛcchraḥ | ṣāṇmāske parākaḥ | ṣaṇmāsād ūrdhvaṃ cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ traimāsikaṃ dvaimāsikaṃ vā | ālasyādinā [129] tyāge tu dvādaśāhātikrame tryaham upavāsaḥ | māsātikrame dvādaśopavāsāḥ payobhakṣaṇaṃ vā | pramādādinā tyāge tv ātrirātraṃ dvādaśa laghuprāṇāyāmāḥ | ā ṣaḍrātrād viṃśatiḥ | ā dvādaśarātrāt pañcāśat | ā viṃśatirātrāc chataṃ prāṇāyāmāḥ | ā triṃśadrātrād upavāsaḥ | ā ṣaṣṭirātrāt trirātram | ā saṃvatsarāt kāyam | ataḥ paraṃ pratyabdaṃ ca kāyam | sarvatra yāvat kālam ahomī ca tāvad dravyaṃ dadyād ity āhuḥ | saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam agnihotratyāge cāndraṃ kṛtvā punar ādhānam | dvivarṣatyāge cāndraṃ somāyanaṃ cāndradvayaṃ vā | trivarṣatyāge kṛcchrābdaṃ godānaṃ punarādhānaṃ ca | strījīvane hiṃsājīvane vaśyādyarthauṣadhijīvane vā hiṃsārthayantravidhāne ca cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ vā | dyūtamṛgayādivyasaneṣu caivam | anṛtavāk taskaro rājasevako vṛkṣāropakavṛttir garado ‘gnido ‘śvagajārohaṇavṛttī raṅgopajīvī śvāgaṇikaḥ śūdropādhyāyo vṛṣalīpatir bhāṇḍako nakṣatropajīvī śvavṛttir brahmajīvī cikitsako devalaḥ purohitaḥ kitavo madyapaḥ kūṭakārako ‘patyanarapaśuvikretā cetyādyā abhojyānnās teṣām annāśane brāhmaṇyam icchanto dravyaṃ tyaktvā traimāsikaṃ kuryuḥ | bahukāle dravyaṃ tyaktvā caturthakālāhārās triṣavaṇasnāyino ‘bdaṃ caritvānte devapitṛtarpaṇaṃ gavāhnikaṃ ca datvā śudhyeyuḥ | śvāgaṇikaḥ śvagaṇajīvī | bhāṇḍakas tūryādijīvī bandī vā | śvavṛttiḥ sevakaḥ | brahmajīvī maulyena dvijakarmakartā | ātmavikraye śūdrasevāyāṃ ca cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā | bahukālaṃ śūdrasevāyāṃ caturthakāle mitabhojinas trikālasnāyinaḥ sthānāsanābhyāṃ viharantas tribhir varṣaiḥ śudhyeyuḥ | samudrayāne brāhmaṇanyāsaharaṇe sarvapaṇyair vyavaharaṇe bhūmyanṛte caivam | hīnasakhye cāndreṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā | ahīnasakhyabheda upoṣya payaḥpāraṇam | hīnayoniniṣevaṇe cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vānuktau | tatra tv amatyā brāhmaṇo rājanyāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā kāyam | vaiśyāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā kṛcchram | śūdrāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā tv atikṛcchram | kṣatriyo vaiśyāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā kāyam |śūdrāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā kṛcchram | vaiśyaḥ śūdrāṃ pūrvoḍhāṃ gatvā kāyam | matyā cāndrādiṣv ekam | brāhmaṇādiṣu yogyaṃ yojyam | sādhāraṇastrīgamane paśuveśyābhigamane tv amatyā kāyam abhyāse cāndrādiṣv ekam | matyā kuśataptam udakaṃ saptarātraṃ sakṛt pibet | abhyāse traimāsikam | atra pratinimittaṃ naimittikaprāyaścittasyāvṛttir nāsti kiṃ tu [130] matyā gamanābhyāse ‘har guṇavṛddhir māsād arvāk | tato māsaguṇavṛddhir yāvad abdam | tato ‘bdaguṇavṛddhir yāvat pāpam evam ācaret | amatyā gamane ‘bhyāse tu sakṛt kṛte yat proktaṃ tribhir dinais tattriguṇaṃ māsāt pañcaguṇam | ṣaṇmāsāt tu daśaguṇam | abdāt pañcadaśaguṇam | tryabdād viṃśatiguṇam | tato ‘py evaṃ prakalpyam | evam upapātakābhyāse | mahāpātakābhyāse tu sakṛt kṛte yat proktaṃ taddvitīye dviguṇaṃ tṛtīye triguṇaṃ caturthe niṣkṛtir nāstīty evamādikam | sādhāraṇagamane tajjanmaprabhṛty anubandhato ‘navacchinnābhyāse gurutalpavratam | anāśramī tv āśramāsaṃbhave saṃvatsare kāyaṃ kṛtvāśramam upeyāt | dvitīye ‘bde ‘tikṛcchraḥ | tṛtīye ‘bde kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ | ata ūrdhvaṃ cāndram | saṃbhave ‘nāśramī prathame ‘bde cāndrādiṣv ekam | yad vā traimāsikam | tato ‘bdāc caturguṇaṃ yojyam | parapākarucitve ‘sacchāstrādhigamana ākarādhikāritve bhāryāvikraye ca cāndrādiṣv ekaṃ traimāsikaṃ vā |
iti smṛtyarthasāra upapātakaprāyaścittam |
athāsatpratigrahe —
tatra pratigrahasyāsattvaṃ jātiduṣṭacāṇḍālādeḥ karmaduṣṭapatitādeś ca bhavati | kurukṣetrādideśe grahaṇādikāle ca | svarūpato ‘pi surāmadyamahiṣīkṛṣṇājinakālapuruṣamṛtaśayyobhayamukhyāder asattvam | tatra patitāder meṣyādipratigrahe brahmacaryavān goṣṭhe vasan pratyahaṃ sāvitrītrisahasraṃ japan payovṛttir māsena śudhyet | nyāyavṛttibrāhmaṇādeḥ sakāśān niṣiddhameṣyādipratigrahe patitāder aniṣiddhabhūmyādipratigrahe ca cāndram | pavitreṣṭir vā mitravindeṣṭir vā | gāyatrīlakṣajapo vā | abhyāse māsam apsu vasan ṣaṣṭhe kale payobhakṣyo māsānte viprān saṃtarpya śudhyet | patitādeḥ kurukṣetragrahaṇādau meṣyādipratigrahe caivam | sarvatrānuktau dātṛdeśakāladravyeṣv aduṣṭeṣu dvādaśaniṣkapramāṇadravyapratigrahe kāyam | evaṃ sarvatra dravyānusārāt prāyaścittavṛddhihrāsau | dātṛdeśakāladravyeṣv anyataradoṣe prāyaścittasyaikaikavṛddhiḥ | maṇivāsogavādīnām alpadravyāṇāṃ pratigrahe ‘ṣṭasahasrajapaḥ | tato ‘lpe ‘lpaṃ bhikṣāmātrapratigrahe puṇyamantram uccaret | prāyaścittaṃ sarvaṃ dravyaṃ tyaktvaiva kāryam | dravye vidyamāne prāyaścittādhikāro nāsti |
athābhakṣyabhakṣaṇe prāyaścittam —
