tatra svabhāvaduṣṭalaśunapalāṇḍugṛñjanachatrākaviḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭānām amatyā sakṛd bhakṣaṇe sāntapanam | abhyāse [131] yaticāndrāyaṇam | matyā sakṛc cec cāndrāyaṇam | abhyāse tu surāpānasamavat | palāṇḍvādisame pūlibhede dīrghapatrapicchagandhamahauṣadhipaṇya-vṛntākaparārikayavaneṣṭeṣu chatrākabhedakabukādau caivam | abhyāse cāndrāvṛttiḥ | atyantābhyāse tu surāpānasamavat | eteṣu balātkāreṇa bhakṣaṇe tadekavedye ca sāvitryaṣṭasahasreṇa mūrdhni saṃtatajalabindupātenopavāsena ca śuddhiḥ | sarvatra charditeṣūktārdham | gandhavarṇarasair laśunādisameṣu sāntapanam | yativratibrahmacāriṇāṃ taptakṛcchrādi | khaṭvākhyaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ kusumbhaṃ vārtākam kumbhītakaṃ śigruṃ bhūtṛṇaṃ khukhaṇḍakavakaṃ kṛṣṇasarṣapaṃ taṇḍulīyakaṃ nālikānālikerīkhaṭvākhyaśākāni kālindabābakakumbhīkeṣu vartulālābvārakaṇṭakīkusumbharaktaśigrukovidāraśleṣmātakanālika-kṣudraśvetakañcukivṛntākāni potikāketukakhaṭvākhyakavakaśaṇapuṣpaśālmalīṣu cānyeṣu kandudurgandhaviṭkandamūlādiṣu vraścanodbhaveṣu lauhitavṛkṣaniryāsādiṣu retoviṇmūtreṣu karanirmathite dadhni ca bahirvedaṛtvijaś ca puroḍāśeṣu bhakṣaṇe tv amatyā sakṛc ced upavāsaḥ | āvṛttir abhyāse matyā trirātram | abhyāse kāyam | atyantābhyāse ‘tikṛcchro ‘tyantānavacchinnābhyāse taptakṛcchraḥ punar upanayanaṃ ca | anyathā patet | nīlyās tv amatyā sakṛdbhakṣaṇe cāndram | viprasyābhyāse cāvṛttir atyabhyāse patanam | saivasauranirmālya-naivedyabhakṣaṇe cāndram | abhyāse dviguṇam | matyābhyāse sāntapanam | anyanirmālye tv anāpady evam |
atha jātiduṣṭeṣu —
saṃdhinīyamasūsyandinyanirdaśāmedhyasevimṛtavatsāgavām anirdaśājāmahiṣyoś ca kṣīreṣv ekaśapheṣu mahiṣīvarjitāraṇyamṛgāṇām ajāvarjitadvistanīnāṃ ca pane dadhitatsaṃbhavavarjita-śukteṣu cāmatyopavāso matyā trirātram | avikharoṣṭravānaraviḍvarāhaśvāpadakṣīreṣv amatyā sāntapanam | matyā taptakṛcchraḥ | abhyāse cāndraṃ punar upanayanaṃ ca | vapanamekhalā-daṇḍabhaikṣyavratāni sarvatra punaḥsaṃskāre nivartante | atyabhyāse patanam | brahmacaryādeḥ kṛcchras taptakṛcchro vā | śukte dadhi savikāraṃ bhojyam | dvistaneṣv ājam āraṇyeṣu māhiṣaṃ ca | kapilākṣīrādiṣu kṣatriyādes tathaiva sarvatra niṣiddhakṣīravikāreṣu cāmatyā dvirātraṃ yāvakam | matyā ṣaḍrātram abhyāse dviguṇam | viḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭasamānajātīyavyatirikta-pakṣimāṃsādiṣv ajāmahiṣamṛgāṇāṃ mṛlloṣṭakeśanakhakīṭapataṅgakṛmijalacarāsthirakta[132] matsyakaṇṭakamatsyāsthibhakṣaṇe raktavamane cāmatyopavāso matyā trirātram | māṃsabhāṇḍapakvānnāśane kuśapakvaṃ payas tryaham | vratinaḥ keśādau mukhamātrapraviṣṭe taptaghṛtaṃ vā brāhmīrasaṃ vā pibet | anne bhojanakāle makṣikākeśādidūṣite ‘nantaram apaḥ spṛśet | tac cānnaṃ bhasmanā vā mṛdā vāmbunā vā sparśayet | asthnā dūṣite snānaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca raktādidṛṣṭau tūpavāsao mukhe dṛṣṭau trirātram | bhuktau brāhmīraso jale kuśodakam | sarpasarīsṛpamūṣakamārjārakṛkalāsakukkuṭanakulamaṇḍūkaśṛṅgimayūrakravyāda-viṣabhidamudgarakulīraśiśumāramakaravaktrasarpamukhamatsyavikṛtamukhaśṛṅgapakṣiṇāṃ medhyatve ‘py āmamāṃse ca vṛthāmāṃsabhakṣaṇe ca niyuktamāṃsavarjane cāmatyā trirātram | matyātikṛcchraḥ | pramāde tūpavāso vamane sadyaḥ snānaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca | viḍvarāhagrāmakukkuṭasamānakravyādanaravānarakapiśvagomāyuvṛkadaṃṣṭripañcanakha-siṃhavyāghraśvāpadagajāśvoṣṭrakharaikaśaphānām ubhayatodantānāṃ kākabalākābhāsagṛdhra-jālapadacakraviṣkirapuṇḍarīkakapiñjalacakrapaśupādakukkuṭasārikākāmacakrahaṃsaplava-cakravākakāraṇḍavacaṭakakapotapārāvatapāṇḍuśukasārikāsārasaṭiṭṭibholūkakaṅkaraktapāda-tittiracāṣakokilasalahikuṭacāramadgukalaviṅkakrauñcaśyenakhañjarīṭadārvāghāṭamūliṅgavā-gulādīnāṃ matsyānāṃ ca māṃsānāṃ saunasthānaṃ gatānāṃ ca śuṣkamāṃsānāṃ ca teṣāṃ viṇmūtraśukraraktavasāmajjānāṃ ca taducchiṣṭānāṃ ca bhakṣaṇe vāmatyā kāyam abhyāse mahāsāntapanam | matyā taptakṛcchro ‘bhyāse cāndram | atyantābhyāse patanam | teṣāṃ karṇaviṭprabhṛtimalaṣaṭke ‘rdhaṃ kalpyam | brahmacāryāder madhumāṃsādibhakṣaṇe laukikaṃ trirātraṃ kāyaṃ punarupanayanaṃ vā tato vratasamāpanam | tad ekavaidyārthe gurūcchiṣṭaṃ bhakṣyam | gavāṃ mūtrādi medhyam ajānām ucchiṣṭaṃ ca |
athāśucisaṃspṛṣṭabhakṣaṇe —
tatrocchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe tāvac chavakākādipakṣiśvāpadākhubiḍālanakulocchiṣṭe bhūyasy annarase jagdhe keśādyavapanne ca devadroṇyādau dravyasaṃskārarahite vāmatyā ced brāhmīṃ suvarcalām ekarātraṃ pibet | matyā cet trirātram | alpadravye ced amatyābhyāse ‘tikṛcchraḥ | matyābhyāse pakṣaṃ yāvakaṃ vratam | brahmakṣatriyavaiśyaśudrocchiṣṭe tu brāhmaṇa ekatripañcasaptarātrāṇi pañcagavyaṃ pibet | abhyāse ‘py eṣām āvṛttiḥ | matyā bhojane tu tāny eva śuddhopavāsādīni kuryāt | matyābhyāse kāyātikṛchrataptakṛcchracāndrāṇi | kṣatrādau pādaṃ hrāsayet | bhāṇḍasthe ‘nne keśapipīlikāmedhyasevikīṭair upahate tāvanmatram uddhṛtya bhasmatāmravaiḍūryahiraṇyarajatādibhir govālair darbhayuktena vāriṇā śeṣaṃ prokṣayet | hastastham evaṃbhūtaṃ tyajet | mukhasthaṃ [133] niṣṭhīvya ghṛtaṃ prāśnīyāt | manuṣyareto-viṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe varṇās taptakṛcchrātikṛcchrakāyatrirātrāṇi kuryuḥ | matyā cāndrataptakṛcchra-kāyān pūnaḥsaṃskāraṃ ca | alehyāpeyacāṇḍālādyantyānne caivam | caṇḍālaḥ śvapākaḥ kṣattā sūtro vaidehako māgadha ityādyā antyāḥ | tadannabhojanae caivam | antyānāṃ bhaktaśeṣaṃ bhuktvā varṇā yaticāndraṃ kāyaṃ tadardhaṃ pādaṃ ca kuryuḥ | antyocchiṣṭabhakṣaṇe tu cāndraṃ mahāsāntapanaṃ ṣaḍrātraṃ trirātraṃ ca kuryuḥ | abhyāse ‘tyabhyāse vā dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ nirantare patanam | āmagrahe tv ardham | taducchiṣṭabhojane dviguṇam | sahabhojane ccicuṇam (triguṇam?) | matyābhyāse patanam | dīpocchiṣṭatailaṃ rātrau rathyādūṣitaṃ cābhyaṅgaśiṣṭaṃ ca bhuktvā naktaṃ caret | pītāvaśiṣṭamukhanirgatapāne matyābhyāse cāndraṃ parākaṃ vā kuryāt | pīvāvaśiṣṭamātrapāne vāmahastena ca pāne tadardham |
athāśucidravyasaṃspṛṣṭabhakṣaṇe —
tatra keśakīṭādyavapanne nīlīlākṣāsnāyvasthicarmaraktamāṃsavasāmajjāsurāśukraviṇmūtra-saṃspṛṣṭe mahāpāpāvekṣite duṣṭapakṣyucchiṣṭe viḍvarāhādyucchiṣṭe gavāghrāte śuṣke paryuṣite vṛthāpakve devapitrarthānne homārtheṣu cāmatyā bhakṣaṇe pāne copavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca sarvatra | matyā ca pādārdhakṛcchraḥ | avijñātajātyasthyādidūṣite tadardham | karṇaviḍādimalaṣaṭke tv ardham etadalpasaṃsarge | mahāsaṃsarge tu dviguṇam | tatrāpi dūṣitasaṃjñāne taptakṛcchraḥ sarvatra punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | saṃsargaduṣṭe kriyāduṣṭe svabhāvaduṣṭe ca caṇḍālādyantyasūtikārajasvalāpatitaśavadāhakaṣaṇḍhadevalakavisṛṣṭāgny-ārūḍhapatitābhiśastādyāś caṇḍālādyāḥ śvaviḍvarāhakharoṣṭrādyāḥ śvāpadādyāḥ pañcanakhā yūthapaśvādyāḥ | caṇḍālādīn bhuñjāno dṛṣṭvā grāsam utsṛjyāntarbāhyamukhalepān prakṣālya snāyāt | kiṃcinmātranigaraṇe ‘ṣṭaśataṃ japaḥ | tadgrāsabhojane pakṣiṇyupavāsaḥ | samastabhojane trirātraṃ jalapāne tadardham | śvādiṣv ācamanaṃ kāryam | bhuktvā pītvā tadardham | caṇḍālādibhir evaṃ bhuñjānaḥ śrutvā bhojanād viramyaiva śudhyet | bhuktau snātvāṣṭaśataṃ japa upavāso vā | caṇḍālādyucchiṣṭaspṛṣṭe kāṃsye bhojane kṛcchraḥ | mṛnmaye ‘tikṛcchraḥ | rajakādyucchiṣṭaspṛṣṭe tadardham | caṇḍālādispṛṣṭānnabhojane trirātram | caṇḍālādidṛṣṭānne tūpavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | caṇḍālādihastabhājane cāndraṃ kāryam | cāṇḍā[134]lādibhojane dviguṇam | cāṇḍālādyucchiṣṭānnabhojane caturguṇam | caṇḍālādihastanirmuktāmbupāne tūpavāsaḥ | tatspṛṣṭaviṣaye tadardham | caṇḍālādīn ucchiṣṭo dṛṣṭvopavaset | spṛṣṭyā trirātram | rajasvalāṃ hīnavarṇāṃ rajasvalā spṛṣṭvā nāśnīyād ā śuddheḥ | savarṇām adhikavarṇāṃ vā spṛṣṭvā tasminn evāhani snātvāśnīyāt | rajasvalā kākādispṛṣṭānyayā rajasvalayā ca spṛṣṭā ced upavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | matyā ced ā śuddher upoṣya snātvā ghṛtaprāśanāc chuddhiḥ | rajasvalā caṇḍālādyantyaiḥ saṃspṛṣṭā nāśnīyād ā śuddheḥ | bhuktā cet tāvanty ahāny atikramya śuddhau prāyaścittaṃ kuryat | prathame ‘hni trirātraṃ dvitīye dvyahaṃ tṛtīye ‘horātraṃ parato naktam | aprāyatye rajodṛṣṭau bālāpatyāyā abhiṣekād bhuktiḥ | mṛtasūtakasaṃparka ṛtuṃ spṛṣṭvā snānakālān nāśnīyāt | matyā bhuktvā cāndram | caṇḍālādisaṃkare bhuktvā pakṣaṃ gomūtrayāvakaṃ kṛcchraṃ vā | cāṇḍālādir yasya gṛhe tv ajñātas tiṣṭhati tasyāmatyānnaṃ bhuktvā kāyam | matyā parākaḥ | cāṇḍālādisvīkṛta-tīrthataḍāganadīṣv amatyā pītvā pañcagavyaṃ pibet | caṇḍālādyambupāne kāyaṃ tadardhaṃ vā | caṇḍālādisaṃspṛṣṭāmbupāne gomūtrayāvakaṃ trirātram | bhuktvocchiṣṭasya caṇḍālādisparśane kāyam | yad vā gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ sahasram aṣṭaśataṃ vā japitvā trirātrānte pañcagavyaṃ pibet | āmabhakṣocchiṣṭe pādaḥ | adhaḥsthānocchiṣṭaś cāṇḍālādispṛṣṭaḥ pādam | cāṇḍālādispṛṣṭau mūtrotsarge trirātram upoṣya jātavedasaṃ vā japet | amatyāśaktasya tūpavāsaḥ | bhojane ṣaḍrātram | āmabhakṣaṇe trirātraṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ vā | rajakādiviṣaye cāṇḍālādyuktārthaṃ jñeyam | ucchiṣṭaḥ śvādīñ chūdramadyāmedhyādyaspṛśyān spṛṣṭvopoṣya pañcagavyaṃ pibet | rajasvalāsūtikāmedhyapatitacāṇḍālapulkasāvadhūtakuṇṭhahastakṛmikuṣṭhikunakhyaspṛśyāśauci-saṃspṛṣṭānnaṃ matyā bhuktvā kāyam amatyārdham | asaddravye keśādyavapanne ca kuśaśaṅkhapuṣpādikaṣāyaṃ brāhmīṃ vā pibet | śūdreṇa pakvaṃ vāmedhyasevikīṭopahataṃ ca bhuñjāne śūdrasparśe cānarhasahapaṅktau vā bhuktāñjāneṣu vā yatrocchiṣṭaṃ prayacched ācāmed vā kutsitvā vā yaṣṭānnaṃ dadyād ucchiṣṭapaṅktau vā bhuktāv upavāsaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca kuryāt | matyā sāntapanam | vāmahastanirmuktapātrānnabhuktau caikapaṅktāv ekasminn utthite paścādbhuktau ca sāntapanam | mṛtapañcanakhakharaviḍvarāhādiśavayuktakūpādau ca viṇmūtrādibhir atyantopahate ca pāne ca varṇās tridvyekopavāsanaktāni kuryuḥ pañcagavyāśanaṃ ca | tatraiva klinnabhinnaśavayukte ‘mbupāne tu matyātikṛcchraḥ | tādṛṅmānuṣaśavayukte [135] tu matyā cāndrāyaṇam | cāṇḍālādisaṃbaddhakūpādyalpajalāśayeṣv ambu pītvā snātvā tadbhāṇḍasthaṃ vā matyā varṇāḥ sāntapanaṃ kāyaṃ tadardhaṃ pādaṃ kramāc careyur amatyā tadardham | aśaktaḥ pañcagavyaṃ pibet | mahājalāśaye na doṣaḥ | hradapuṣkariṇyādiṣu jānudadhne na doṣaḥ | tato ‘dhastane tv amatyā naktaṃ matyopavāsaṃ vipraḥ kuryāt | rajakādyantyajakūpabhāṇḍasthāmbupayodadhipāne tv amatyā dvijā brahmakūrcena tridvyekopavāsān kuryuḥ | śūdras tūpavāsaṃ śaktyā dānaṃ ca | matyā taddviguṇam | antyajaiḥ khānitavāpīkūpataḍāgādiṣu snāne pāne copavāso matyābhyāse kāyam | madhyameṣu pañcagavyam | mahatsu na doṣaḥ | prapāsv araṇye maṭhake ca saireyadroṇyāṃ jalaṃ kośanisṛtaṃ ca pītvā pañcagavyenāśaktaḥ śuddhaḥ | śakta upavāsena |
atha bhāvaduṣṭabhakṣaṇe —
tatra nijavarṇādirahitaṃ bhāvaduṣṭam | bhāvaduṣṭe ‘nne bhāvaduṣṭe ca bhojane matyā trirātram amatyārdham | chardane ghṛtaprāśanam | bhakṣyābhakṣyaśaṅkitabhakṣaṇe tv akṣāralavaṇe rūkṣāṃ brāhmīṃ suvarcalāṃ pibet trirātram | śaṅkhapuṣpīṃ vā payasā saha kuśapadmodumbarapalāśabilvapatrāmbupakvaṃ vā pibet | saṃvatsarasya trīn dvāv ekaṃ kṛcchraṃ caret | śaṅkāyāṃ vijñāne taddviguṇaṃ dvijaś caret |
atha kāladuṣṭabhakṣaṇe —
kāladuṣṭaṃ paryuṣitaṃ śuktam anirdaśāgokṣīrādi | tatrāmatyopavāso matyā trirātraṃ kāyavratam | yavagodhūmapayodadhipiṣṭavikārasnehapakvānnasnehāktānnamāsādau na doṣaḥ | śṛṅgāsthidantajaiḥ pātraiḥ kandaśālūkaśaṅkhaśuktikapardakaiś ca pītvā navodakaṃ ca pītvā svakāle tryahād arvāg akāle daśāhād arvāk pītvā pañcagavyaṃ pibet | matyopavāsaḥ | saṃgrahe bhojane navaśrāddhe ca grāmayājakānne ca cāndram | grahaṇādinimittaniṣiddhakāle ca saṃdhikāle cātiprātaratisāyam ityādau dhānādadhisaktūnāṃ rātrau tilasaṃbaddhānnabhojane caivamādiṣv anādiṣṭaprāyaścitteṣu prāṇāyāmaśataṃ matyopavāsaḥ |
atha guṇaduṣṭaśuktādibhakṣaṇe —
tatra guṇaduṣṭaśuktāni kaṣāyāṃś cānyāny amedhyāny amatyā pītvā bhuktvopavaset | matyā trirātraṃ yāvakam | āmalakādiphalayuktakāñjikāyāṃ na doṣo ‘bhighāraṇe ca | uddhṛtasāravilāpitapiṇyākamathitādiṣv annavikāre ca matyā trirātraṃ yāvakam amatyārdhaṃ charditvā [136] ghṛtaṃ pibet | prāk pañcanakhebhyaḥ prāṇiṣu caivam | vaiśvadevāgnyādi-rahitānnabhojane vṛthā kṛśarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīmadhumāṃsānāṃ cāmatyopavāso matyā trirātram | āhitāgnis tu kāyaṃ trirātraṃ vā kuryāt | śūdrabhājanabhinnabhājanabhāṇḍeṣu bhuktvopavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | matyā trirātram | bhinnakāṃsye kāyam | bhinnabhāṇḍādibhojane pañcagavyaṃ ghṛtaṃ vā brāhmīṃ vā pibet | vaṭārkāśvatthakumbhī-tindukakovidārakadambapatreṣu bhuktvā cāndram | latāpalāśapadmavṛkṣapatre gṛhī bhuktvā cāndraṃ caret | vānaprastho yatiś ca bhuktvā cāndraphalaṃ labheta | anyaḥ padmapalāśeṣu |
atha hastadānādikriyāduṣṭābhojyabhojane —
tatra hastadattabhojane ‘brāhmaṇasamīpabhojane duṣṭapaṅktau balād atyapāṅkteyśūdrahastena ca bhojane pānīye vāmatyā naktam | matyopavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | mākṣikaphāṇitaśākagorasalavaṇaghṛteṣu hastadāne tathaiva |
āsanārūḍhapādo vā vastrārdhaprāvṛto ‘pi vā |
mukhena dhamitaṃ bhuktvā kṛcchraṃ sāntapanaṃ caret ||
atha śrāddhabhojane —
pitrādyuddeśena tyaktānne tu tatra śrāddhāni trividhāni | tatrāntardaśāhe caikādaśāhe ca nava śrāddhāni | ekādaśāhān nyūnābdāntāni ṣauḍaśaikoddiṣṭāni navamiśrāṇi | sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ miśram | tataḥ purāṇāni | ekādaśāhaśrāddhaṃ navamiśram | tatra prāyaścittagauravād doṣagauravaṃ cāsti | ādyaikoddiṣṭaṃ svatantram ity eke | tathāpy ubhayadharmaprāyaścittadoṣadaṇḍāḥ syuḥ | tatrāpi sūtakasaṃbaddheṣu navaśrāddheṣu bhojane kāyam | ekādaśāhaikoddiṣṭe kāyaṃ pādonakāyaṃ vā | dvādaśāhe prathamamāse vā pādonaṃ kāyam eva kuryāt | dvimāse tripakṣe conaṣāṇmāsike conābdike cārdhaṃ kṛcchram | trimāsādyabdamokṣānteṣu sapiṇḍane cādyābdike copavāsaḥ pādakṛcchro vā | pratyabdaṃ purāṇe ca naktam | guruviṣaye dravyārthe navaśrāddhe trirātram ādye trirātraṃ dvirātraṃ ca | dvādaśāhādau dvirātram | dvimāsādāv upavāsas trimāsādau naktaṃ pañcagavyaṃ vā | pratyabdaṃ vānyapurāṇe ca ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | vṛddhau trayaḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | tatraiva niḥspṛhabhoktur japaśīlino vā tadardham | kṣatriyādiśrāddheṣu dvitricaturguṇāni kramāt kāryāṇi | anāpadi tu navaśrāddhe cāndraṃ kāyaṃ ca | dvādaśāhādau kāyam eva | dvimāsādau pādonam | trimā[137]sādyabdānte sāpiṇḍye ca trirātram ardhaṃ kāyaṃ vā | ābdike pādaṃ kāyam | pratyābdike tūpavāsaḥ | kṣatriyasya tu navaśrāddhe cāndram | ādyamāsike cāndraṃ parākaś ca | dvādaśāhādau parākaḥ | ekadvimāsatripakṣādau mahāsāntapanam | trimāsādau kāyam | ābdike pādonaṃ pratyabde tv ardhaṃ purāṇe pādam | vaiśyasya tv etāny eva vratāni sārdhāni | śūdrasya dviguṇāni | yad vā navaśrāddhe cāndradvayaṃ sārdhaṃ cāndraṃ ca | dvādaśāhādau sārdhacāndram eva | dvimāsatripakṣādau cāndraṃ trimāsādau parākaḥ | abde mahāsāntapanaṃ pratyabde sāntapanam | caṇḍālasarpabrāhmaṇapaśudaṃṣṭrivaidyutaprapatana-viṣodbandhanānāśakair mṛtānāṃ pāpināṃ ca stenapatitādyabhojyānnānāṃ ca śrāddheṣu navaśrāddhe cāndram | ādimāsike cāndraṃ parākaś ca | dvādaśāhādau parāka eva | dvimāsatripakṣādāv atikṛcchraḥ | trimāsādau kāyo ‘bde pādaḥ | pratyabde tūpavāsaḥ | samyagviṣaye ‘pi dravyārthabhojinaś caivam | apāṅkteyānām ekādaśāhaśrāddhe śiśucāndram | yatir vratī brahmacārī sūtakyannanavaśrāddhamāsikādau bhuktvoktaṃ kṛtvādhikaṃ trirātram ekopavāsaṃ triprāṇāyāmān ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kṛtvā vrataśeṣaṃ samāpayet | anāpadi tu kāyaṃ trirātram ekarātropavāsaṃ ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ pañcagavyaprāśanam adhikam | abhyāse kṛcchrādi dviguṇam | āmahemasaṃkalpitaśrāddheṣūktārdhatattadardhāni | yad vānāpadi cāmaśrāddhe kāyaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vāśaktaś caret | hemaśrāddhe trirātraṃ kāyaṃ ca | saṃkalpitaśrāddhe tūpavāsas trirātram ca | daivāt sūtakavyavahiteṣu tu brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdraśrāddheṣv ekoddiṣṭeṣu kṛcchrātikṛcchrataptakṛcchracāndrāṇi | dvādaśāhādiṣu pādaṃ pādaṃ hrāsayet | ativyavahiteṣu tu trirātrādikam | guruviṣaye tūpavāsanaktaṣaṭtriprāṇāyāmāḥ | anāpadi sarvaṃ dviguṇam | atiśrotriyapurāṇeṣv anuktaniṣkṛtiṣu ca gāyatryā daśakṛtvo ‘paḥ pītvā śudhyati | tataḥ saṃdhyopāsanaṃ homaṃ ca yathocitaṃ kuryāt | nivṛtte cūḍāhome ca prāṅnamakaraṇe ca jātakarmaṇi cāṅgaśrāddhe sūtake ‘py asūtake ca nāmaśrāddhe cūḍāhomānte caulaśrāddhe ca sīmante some cādhāne brāhmaudane cāmatyā cāpadi ca bhuktvā sāntapanam | matyānāpadi ca cāndram | anyeṣu saṃskāreṣu tūpavāso nityaṃ trirātraṃ cānāpadi | anyadattā kanyānyasmai [138] punar dattā sā punarbhūḥ | asaṃskṛte pūrve garbhe prasūte dvitīye garbhe saṃskāre yā sā punāretāḥ | ṛtuṣoḍaśāhāt paścād eva yā garbhiṇī sā retodhāḥ | bhartṛśāsanollaṅghinī kāmacāriṇī | āsāṃ garbhe parvavat [pūrvavat?] sāntapanaṃ cāndraṃ vā sarvastrīprathamagarbhe ca |
atha parigrahācāśuciduṣṭabhojane —
tatrāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājakahute strīhute klībahute vānnabhojane rājarājapurohita-rājabhṛtyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ klībājāvikamāhiṣikasthānikabhūmipālānāṃ stenagāyakatakṣa-vārdhuṣikadīkṣāvasthitānāṃ kadaryabaddhanigaḍābhiśastapuṃścalīdāmbhikavaidyakulima-sāṃvatsarikāṇāṃ mattakruddhāturagaṇikānāṃ śvacakramadyastrīmṛgajīvināṃ krūrocchiṣṭa-bhojyugrapatitapiśunānāṃ śaulūṣatantuvāyatunnavāyakirāṭipravrajitānāṃ vrātyakarmāra-ghoṣataruvājijīvikānāṃ ghṛtakṣīratailalavaṇaguḍavikrayiṇām agnidanityarogighāṇṭikaviṣa-śastrakāravipragodevavṛttighnānāṃ vaiṇavātipāpiveśyāpākhaṇḍijīvisūtādipratilomajānāṃ karmārakṛtaghnānāṃ puṇyasomakarmātmavikrayiṇāṃ vivādadveṣaraṅgāvatārasuvarṇakāra-vṛttīnāṃ śauṇḍikavastranirṇejakavenaśvavatāṃ kūṭanaṭanartakavṛṣalītatpatīnāṃ nṛśaṃsopapatikopapatimarṣakastrījitakitavānāṃ śūdrādhyāpakayājakadharmapatistrīṇāṃ grāmayājivṛṣalījavaṇikkāṇḍapṛṣṭhasevakānāṃ paryāhitaparīṣṭaparivittiparivividānāṃ punarbhū-jāgredidhiṣūpaticakratailavadhajīvināṃ bhagavṛttipakṣirakṣisamudratārakahīnātiriktāṅgānāṃ mātṛpitṛsutagurvagnityāgināṃ kūṭamānakūṭasutakanyāstrīsatyavikrayiṇāṃ sīsakāragodhā-lohakārasūcikāputrabrahmacāriṇāṃ kuṇḍāśivīrahaguruguptikagaradasūpajīvināṃ sūtakasaunika-pauṇikulālacitrakarmaṇāṃ yonisaṃkarikāśramabhedakataskaravṛthāśramivṛthāhotṝṇām ityādipāpināṃ mārge tiṣṭhatāṃ bhikṣānne cāndram | eṣām akṛtaniṣkṛtīnāṃ annabhojane yājane dāne pratigrahe ca tathā kunakhikṛṣṇadantābrāhmaṇānnadaśūdraśūdrānnadabrāhmaṇānām abhojyānnānāṃ ca prapāsatre soma ekādaśāhaśrāddhe duṣṭānnaduṣṭavidvajjugupsitamahāpāpy- avekṣitaśvaspṛṣṭodakyāduṣṭagavāghrātapakṣyālīḍhāsadbahuyācitasaroṣasavismayānnaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭam avajñātam anarcitaparyāyānnastrīśudrocciṣṭavṛthāmāṃsajātamṛtasūtaka-sūtakikāpretānnaṃ cātuṣṭikaraṃ cāto ‘nyatama[139]syānnaṃ bhuktvā vā retoviṇmūtrabhakṣaṇe vā matyā trirātram | abhyāse kṛcchraḥ | matyātikṛcchro ‘bhyāse taptakṛcchraḥ | brahmacaryādeḥ kāryāṇi taptakṛcchrāṇi cāndrāṇi punaḥsaṃskāraś ca | dvitriṣaṇṇavadvādaśarātrāṇi payasātyabhyāse cāndram | atyantābhyāse cāndravṛddhiḥ | āpady ekadvitricaturupavāsāḥ | atyantāpadi triṣaṇṇavadvādaśa prāṇāyāmāḥ | āmeṣu snānam | viprānna ekabhaktam | anācāre durācāre naktam | brāhmaṇabruve snānam aṣṭaśatajapaḥ | gurvācāryāgnidharmaśāstrago-brāhmaṇadviḍanne śākhāraṇḍānne caivam | puṃścalyabhiśastamlecchacāṇḍāladasyubhir balād dāsīkṛtānāṃ gavādihiṃsane taducchiṣṭamārjane bhojane karoṣṭrādiviḍvarāhabhakṣaṇe tatstrībhiḥ saṃgame bhojane ca māsoṣitadvijānāṃ kāyam | āhitāgneś cāndraṃ parāko vā | saṃvatsaroṣitasya cāndraṃ parākaś ca |
atha jātamṛtāśaucānnabhojane —
tatra dvijāśauce savarṇānām upavāsas trirātraṃ vā | viprakṣatriyavaiśyāśauceṣv amatyā vipro bhuktvā naktaikadvitryupavāsān kuryāt | abhyāse tv ekatripañcasaptopavāsān | yad vābhyāse dvātriṃśadaṣṭaśataprāṇāyāmayuktān | matyā cet sāntapanakāyamahāsāntapanacāndrāṇi sarvatra pañcagavyaṃ vā | ekadvitricatuḥkāyāni vā | matyābhyāse kāyāni kṛcchrataptakṛcchracāndrāṇi | yad vābhyāsa ekadvitriṣaṇmāsāni yāvakavratāni | atyantāpadi dvādaśatriṃśatṣaṣṭiśata-prāṇāyāmāḥ | kṣatriyasya vaiśyāśudrāśaucayor vaiśyasya śūdrāśauce caivam | viprāśauce kṣatravaiśyayor aṣṭaśatajapo naktaṃ vā | kṣatrāśauce vaiśyasya caivam | dvijāśauce śūdrasya snānam | śūdrāśauce śūdrasya snānaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca | śūdraṃ saṃspṛśyācamane snānaṃ pāne pañcagavyam | brahmacāryāder upavāsaḥ śatajapaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | trirātraṃ sahasrajapaḥ | pañcagavyaṃ cādhikam | jātāśaucaṃ laghu yojyam ity eke | āhitāgnyāśauce caivam | āśauce bhuktavantaṃ na spṛśed ā purīṣotsargāt | tatprāyaścittaṃ tadāśauce gate kāryam | sakṛdbhojanamātreṇa bhoktṝṇāṃ ca tāvad aśucitvād anadhikārāt | tadgṛhabhojane naktam upavāso vā | kāpālike kāpālikānne pāne ca kramāt kāyaṃ tadardhaṃ ca | abhyāse dviguṇam | matyā triguṇam | matyābhyāse caturguṇam | atyantābhyāse cāndraṃ tadardhaṃ ca | tatstrīgamane caivam | [140] atyantābhyāse ‘bdakṛcchram | patitānne dravye sati saṃbhave ca tatsamutsṛjyātikṛcchraṃ kuryāt | annasatrapravṛttasyāgnihotriṇaś cāme na doṣaḥ | pakvānne tv ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā yāvakaṃ dvādaśa caturviṃśatir vā prāṇāyāmāḥ | gṛhītvāgniṃ samāropya pañca yajñān akurvan parapākanivṛttaḥ pañca yajñān kṛtvā nityaparānnopajīvī parapākarato dānavarjitaḥ parvapakṣe māse vā devapitṝn bhojayet | aśrāddhadas teṣām anabhyāse cāndram | pūrvaparagaṇitavyatiriktaniṣiddhācaraṇaśīlānnabhojane tūpavāso dinam | saṃvatsarābhyāse parākaḥ| atrādiṣṭaṃ viprasyaiva | tatra kṣatrasya pādonaṃ vaiśyasyārdhaṃ śūdrasya pādaḥ kalpyaḥ | tattatstrīṇām ardhaṃ rajasvalānāṃ tatra tatroktam eva kāryam |
ity abhakṣaṇaprāyaścittam |
atha jātibhraṃśakarādiprāyaścittam —
jātibhraṃśakareṣv icchayā mahāsāntapanam anicchayā kāyam | saṃkarīkaraṇeṣu māsaṃ yāvakaṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ vā | apātrīkaraṇeṣu kṛcchram atikṛcchraṃ vā | malinīkaraṇeṣu mahāsāntapanaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ vā | brāhmaṇajaḥ kṛtvā rāsabhādiprayāṇaṃ ninditād dhanādānaṃ ca kṛtvā kṛcchrārdhaṃ caret | atra strīṇām ardhaṃ rajasvalānāṃ tatra tatroktaṃ kāryam eva |
ity upapātakaprāyaścittāni |
atha prakīrṇaprāyaścittam —
tatra kharayuktam uṣṭrayuktaṃ vā yānam āruhyādhvānaṃ gatvā nagnaḥ snātvā bhuktvā ca svastriyaṃ divā gatvā matyā savastro ‘po ‘vagāhya prāṇāyāmena śudhyet | amatyā snānamātram | sākṣāt khararohaṇe tu dviguṇam | guruṃ huṃkṛtya tvaṃkṛtya vipraṃ chalavādena nirjitya vāsasā baddhvā vā śīghraṃ prasādyopavaset | abhyāse trirātram | vipraṃ hantuṃ daṇḍodyamane kṛcchraḥ | tāḍane ‘tikṛcchraḥ | raktasrāvaṇe kṛcchātikṛcchrau | ābhyantararakte tvagbhede kṛcchraḥ | athibhede ‘tikṛcchro ‘ṅgakartane parākaḥ | pādena prahāra upoṣya snātvā prasādayet | pādena sparśe tu prasādanam | asaṃnidhāv apsu vāgnau cārto viṇmūtraniṣevaṇe sacailo jalam āplutya gāṃ spṛṣṭvā śudhyet | matyopoṣya sacailaṃ snāyāt | abhyāse cānārtasya ca taptakṛcchraḥ | śrautasmārtalope tūpavāsaḥ | paścāt pratipadokteṣṭyādiprāyaścittam api sūryodaye svastho matyā supto ‘hni tiṣṭhan sāvitrīṃ japann upavaset | sūryāstamaye supto rātrau tiṣṭhan sāvitrīṃ japan nādyāt | snānādikarmalope tūpavāso ‘ṣṭa[141]śatajapo vā | jīrṇamalavadvastradhāraṇādi-snātakavratalope tūpavādo ‘ṣṭaśatajapo vā | pañcamahāyajñeṣv anyatamalope tv anāturasyopavāso dhaninaḥ kṛcchrārdham | āhitāgneḥ parvaṇy upasthānalope caivam | ṛtau bhāryāgamanalope caivam | prathamāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ jīvantyāṃ dvitīyabhāryāyā vaitānikair dahane surāpānasamavat | svabhāryāṃ krodhād agamyeti vadec ced varṇānāṃ kṛcchranavaṣaṭ-trirātrāṇi | asnātvā bhojanādau tūpavāso dinajapyaṃ ca | ekapaṅktyopaviṣṭānāṃ snehādinā viṣamadāne dāpane yācane kāyam | udakāvaraṇamārgahantuḥ kanyāvighnakasya ca sameṣu viṣamapūjādikartuś ca bhaikṣānne cāndram | surāpasya gandham āghrāya somapo brāhmaṇo ‘psu nimagnas trīn prāṇāyāmāṃs trir aghamarṣaṇaṃ vā kṛtvā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca kuryāt | asomapo ‘napsv itarasyāmantrakaprāṇāyāma eva | madyagandhe tadardham | sākṣāt surāghrāṇe surāpānasamavat | amatyā ghrāṇe triprāṇāyāmāḥ | madyaghrāṇe viṇmūtrakravyādapūtigandha-ghrāṇe triḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | darśane prāṇāyāmaḥ | sparśane tu snānaṃ ghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca | surāsparśe snānaṃ pañcagavyaṃ ca | śavaghrāṇe ‘spu triprāṇāyāmāḥ | asomapasyānapsu | itarasyāmantraprāṇāyāmāḥ | śuṣkasurābhāṇḍasthāpaḥ pītvā pakṣayāvakam | surābhājanasthāpsu śaṅkhapuṣpīpakvaṃ payasas trirātram | paryuṣitāpsu gandhapuṣpīpayaḥ ṣaḍaham | śuṣkamadyabhāṇḍasthāpsu saptarātraṃ yāvakam | anyatra pūrvoktārdham | surāmadyarasopalabdhau tāratamyaṃ kalpyam | vamane cāndraṃ kāyaṃ vā | purīṣādidūṣitā apaḥ pītvā sāntapanaṃ dvyaham | rasopalabdhau pādakṛcchraḥ | madyaviṇmūtravipruḍbhiḥ (?) saṃspṛṣṭe mukhamaṇḍale mṛttikāgomayaiḥ prakṣālya pañcagavyaṃ pibet | madirāṃ datvā spṛṣṭvā pratigṛhya ca snātvā kuśāmbu tryaham | saṃkrāntyādinimittasnānārho jagdhvā pītvāṣṭasahasraṃ japet | śūdrādisaṃsparśanimitte tūpavāsaḥ cāṇḍālādisparśanimitte trirātraṃ kāyaṃ vā | rajakādisparśe tadardhaṃ vā | tatspṛṣṭasparśe tūpavāsas trirātraṃ vā | nityasnānam akṛtvā bhūktau copavāsas trirātraṃ vā | naimittike snāne sarvatrāṣṭasahasrajapo dvādaśa prāṇāyāmā vādhikāḥ | amedhyaśvādyaspṛśyasparśane snātvā bhuktau gṛhasthādes trirātraṃ matyā ṣaḍrātram | vratinaḥ kāyaṃ cāndraṃ vā | caṇḍālādisparśane tv asnātvā bhuktau cāndram | rajakā[142]dyantasparśane snātvā bhuktau matyā kāyam | śvakākādisparśane ‘snātvā bhuktau matyā trirātram | utpanne snāne bhuktvā pītvā trirātraṃ sāntapanam | anācamya bhakṣaṇe vāṣṭaśatajapaḥ | bhojane tūpavāsaḥ | bhoktṛkāmasyāpośanāt paścād abhyavaharaṇāt pūrvaṃ purīṣanirgame sacailo bahir āplavaḥ ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | bhuñjānasya gudanisrāve śaucaṃ kṛtvopoṣya pañcagavyam | bhuñjānasyāśucitve tad grāsaṃ bhūmau kṣiptvā snāyāt | tadgrāsāśane tūpavāsaḥ | sarvānnāśane trirātram | bhuñjānasya mastake viṣṭhādipatane 'nnaṃ tyaktvā nadyāṃ snātvā triḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | bhuñjānasya sagotrasparśane ‘nnaṃ tyajet | udakaṃ spṛṣṭvā śuddhiḥ | bhuñjānasyāsagotrasparśane bhojanaṃ viramyaiva śuddhiḥ | (* bhuñjānayoḥ savarṇayoḥ sparśane ca viramyaiva śuddhiḥ | *) bhuñjānasyocchiṣṭakṣatriyasparśane snānaṃ japaṃ ca kṛtvā dināntare ghṛtaprāśanam | bhuñjānasyocchiṣṭavaiśyasparśane caivam | triṣavaṇasnānādikam | bhuktocchiṣṭaspṛṭau savarṇe snānaṃ japo vā | asavarṇe tūpavāsaḥ sahasrajapo vā | bhuktocchiṣṭasya śūdraśvādisparśe tūpoṣya pañcagavyam | rajakādisparśe trirātram | ūrdhvocchiṣṭasya cāṇḍālādisparśe kāyam | adharocchiṣṭe tūcchiṣṭo dvija ucchiṣṭaṃ dvijaṃ spṛṣṭvā śūdraśvādīn vopoṣya pañcagavyaṃ pibet | ucchiṣṭo dvijaś cāṇḍālādispṛṣṭas trirātraṃ kuryāt | rajakādisparśe tadardham | anucchiṣṭaspṛṣṭau yatra snānaṃ vihitaṃ tatrocchiṣṭaspṛṣṭau trirātraṃ kāyaṃ vā | ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭaspṛṣṭāv eka eva duṣyati | tatspṛṣṭino na duṣyanti | yena kenacid ucchiṣṭaḥ paraṃ spṛṣṭaḥ san sadya āplutya prāyaścittaṃ sarvatra kuryāt | viṇmūtrādyutsarge bhuktvā pītvā trirātram upavāsas trirātraṃ vā gomūtrayāvakena | aśaktau yāvakam | jalapāne tūpavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | kāñjikādinā śaucaṃ kṛtvā bhuktvā punar jalena śuddhiṃ kṛtvopoṣya pañcagavyaṃ pibet | nīlavastrādidhāraṇe keśanirmitacailadhāraṇe copavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ hiraṇyodakaṃ cādhikam | nīlīmadhyagamane snātvā triḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | nīlīṃ vikrīṇayan pālayan nīlīvṛttyā jīvaṃś ca patati | viraktaś cet tribhiḥ kṛcchraiḥ śudhyet | nīlīlavadhāraṇe ‘pi snānadānajapahomasvādhyāyadevatārcanādi sarvaṃ niṣphalam | nīlīkṣetrotpannabhuktau cāndram | nīlīkṣetre nīlīm utpādya dvādaśābdāt paraṃ śudhyet | nīlī[143]jagdho cāndram | nīlīṃ dhṛtvā maithune garbhaś cāṇḍālo jāyate | nīlīṃ dhṛtvā mṛtaḥ ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ kṛmiḥ syāt | mṛte bhartari patnī nīlīṃ dhṛtvāste cet tāvan narake bhartā vaset | nīlīṃ dhṛtvā yad annāni dīyante tatra dātur bhoktuś ca sāntapanam | nīlīdārubhede raktadarśane [ca] cāndram | kambalapaṭṭasūtre nīlīdoṣo nāsti | aindraṃ cāpaṃ palāśāgniṃ cānyasya pradarśyopoṣya kramād dhanur daṇḍaṃ ca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | patitamlecchāśuddhādhārmikasaṃbhāṣaṇe puṇyakṛto dhyāyed brāhmaṇena vā saha saṃbhāṣeta | vinā yajñopavītenocchiṣṭatve ca matyā bhakṣaṇe ‘mbupāne vā triḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | bhojane naktam | viṇmūtre ṣaṭ prāṇāyāmāḥ | matyā ced bhakṣyapānaviṇmūteṣv aṣṭaśatajapaḥ | bhojane tūpavāsaḥ | bhuktvānācamyotthāne sadyaḥ snānam | daṇḍyacaurādyutsarge rājña upavāsaḥ | purohitasya trirātram | adaṇḍyadaṇḍane rājñas trirātram | purohitasya kṛcchraḥ | stenapatitādyapāṅkteya-paṅktibhojane tūpavāsaḥ pañcagavyaṃ ca | palāśakhaṭvāśayanāsanapādukārohaṇe trirātraṃ yāvakam | phalapradavṛkṣacchede ‘bdavratam | palāyamānasya nṛpasyaivam | viprayor agniviprayor goviprayor daṃpatyoś ca madhye gantuḥ sāntapanam | homakāle gavāṃ dohe sāṃnāyādyaṅge svādhyāye vivāhe ca madhyagamane caivam | abhyāse cāndram | duḥsvapna-duriṣṭadarśanādau ghṛtaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca dadyāt | atīrthārthinaḥ sindhusauvīrasaurāṣṭrābdhi-pratyantāṅgavaṅgakaliṅgāndhradeśagamane punaḥ saṃskāraḥ | tīrthārthe na doṣaḥ | pratyarkādimehane cātmaśakṛddarśane ca sūryaṃ vipraṃ gāṃ vā paśyet | vahner adhaḥkaraṇe pādapratāpane ca kuśaiḥ pādamārjane copavāso yāvakaṃ vā | kṣatravaiśyayor abhivādane viprasyopavāsaḥ | śūdrābhivādane trirātram | śayyāpādukopānadāsanārūḍhāndhakārastha-śrāddhahomajapakṛddevapūjābhiratānāṃ samitpuṣpakuśāmbugandhākṣatāmbupāṇīnām eteṣāṃ cābhivādane caite ‘py anyān abhivādya sarve trirātraṃ kuryuḥ | anyatra nimantritasyānyatra bhojane ‘py evam | ityādi deśakālavayo’rthaśaktyapekṣaṃ sarvatra prāyaścittaṃ deyam | anupadiṣṭeṣu daṇḍārthavādastutinindābhiḥ kalpyam | strīvṛddhabālāturāṇāṃ ca yogyam deyam |
iti prakīrṇaprāyaścittam |
atha yas tūddhato mahāpāpaprāyaścittam na kuryāt tasya patitasya sarve sapiṇḍā bāndhavāś ca riktātithau sāyāhne grāmād bahir apāṃ pūrṇaṃ dāsyāhṛtaṃ kumbhaṃ prācīnāvītino muktaśikhā gurusaṃnidhāv asmākaṃ tvaṃ mṛta iti ninayeyuḥ | [144] tato ‘nirīkṣamāṇā apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśeyuḥ | tatas taṃ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaṃ saṃbhāṣaṇādiṣu varjayet | yadi pramādāt saṃbhāṣaṇādi kṛtam amatyā sāvitrīṃ japann ekarātraṃ tiṣṭhet |  matyā trirātram | abhyāse ‘bde pātityam | atha kṛtaprāyaścitta āgate sapiṇḍās tena saha puṇye jale snātvāpāṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ puṇye deśe kale ca puṇyadiṅmukhās tvam asmākam iti ninayeyuḥ | tataḥ sa gobhyo yavasaṃ datvā bhakṣite śuddhaḥ | na cet punaḥ prāyaścittaṃ caret sa vyavahāryaś ca | strīṇāṃ patitānāṃ caivaṃ tyāgaḥ prigrahaś ca kiṃ tu pradhānagṛhasamīpe kuṭīṃ kṛtvā prāṇadhāraṇārtham annaṃ malinaṃ vāsaś ca datvā bhogarahitāṃ rakṣet | strīṇām api mahāpāpādipāpānupāpāny abhyastāni copapāpāni patananimittāni | viśeṣato hīnavarṇagamanaṃ garbhapātanam abrāhmaṇyā api bhartur hiṃsanaṃ tāni patanahetūni | śiṣyagāgurugājuṅgitopagatānām apy evaṃ prāyaścittam | kṣatriyādeḥ prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇam antare kṛtvā brūyāt | strīśūdrāṇāṃ japahomavarjaṃ vācyam | amantrakāḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ sarvatra vācyā eva | yāgādyanuṣṭhānaśīlānuśīlānāṃ japādikam | itareṣāṃ tu japādi | nāmadhārakamūrkhanirdhanānāṃ tapa evānadhīyānāṃ ca | kartṛvyatiriktair vijñātadoṣo vikhyātadoṣaḥ | sa vidvān api śīghraṃ parṣadanumataṃ vrataṃ prāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | tatra vedadharmajñā viprāḥ sahasrādyekāntā yāvanto yathāyogyaṃ parṣady ativarjam | parṣaddakṣiṇā ca lakṣagavādi kiṃcidantaṃ yathāyogyaṃ kṛcchrasaṃkhyā vā | parṣattuṣṭir vā | tatra sakṛd āplutya sacailo vāgyataḥ snātvārdravāsāḥ parṣaddakṣiṇāṃ datvā pāpaṃ vyākhyāya prāyaścittaṃ tadvratam ādāya punaḥ snātvā vrataṃ caret | parṣac ca brūyāt | na cet tatsamā syāt | ajñātvā vadec cet tat pāpaṃ sarvam āpatet | parṣanmataṃ vinā kurvan dviguṇaṃ kuryāt | keśarakṣārthaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ caret | vidvadviprastrīṇāṃ vapanaṃ neṣyate |
iti smṛtyarthasāre prakāśaprāyaścittam |
atha rahasyaprāyaścittam —
tatra kartṛvyatiriktair avijñātadoṣo rahasyaprāyaścittaṃ caret | avidvāṃs tu mukhāntareṇa rahasyaprāyaścittaṃ jñātvā rahaś caret | strīśūdrāṃś caivaṃ japahomavarjam | tatrāhāraviśeṣānuktau payaḥprabhṛtayaḥ | kālaviśeṣānuktau saṃvatsarādayaḥ | deśaviśeṣānuktau śiloccayādayo gautamoktāḥ | tatra japeṣu ṛṣidaivatacchandoviniyogā vijñeyāḥ | amatyā brāhmaṇavadhe ‘naśnaṃs trirātram antarjale magna ṛtaṃ ca satyaṃ cety aghamarṣaṇasūktaṃ trir āvartya trirātrānte payasvinīṃ gāṃ datvā śudhyet | godānāśaktau [145] savyāhṛtikān ṣoḍaśa prāṇāyāmān pratidinaṃ samāvartya śudhyet | abhyāse tu triṃśadrātravratasthaḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ śrānto ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ japet | mahāpāpaiḥ śudhyet | matyā cet prāg vodag vā niṣkramya snātaḥ śucivastro jalānte sthaṇḍilam upalipya sakṛt klinnavāsāḥ sakṛt pavitrapāṇinārkābhimukho ‘ghamarṣaṇaṃ prātaḥ śataṃ madhyāhne śatam aparāhṇe śatam uditanakṣatradarśane cāparimitaṃ japet | prasṛtimātraṃ yāvakaṃ pibet | saptarātrād upapāpair dvādaśarātrān mahāpāpasamair viṃśatirātrān mahāpāpaiḥ śudhyet | anyamahāpāpe  matyābhyāse caivam | nirguṇasya brāhmaṇasya vadhe tu prāg vodag vā niṣkramya pravṛddhāgnāv aṣṭasahasrāhutīr juhuyāt | anugrāhakakartuś caivam | nirguṇavadhe guṇavato hantus trirātropoṣaṇaṃ triṣavaṇasnānaṃ trir aghamarṣaṇajapaś ca | prayojakānumatikartuś caivam | nimittakartur aghamarṣaṇasyaivaṃ trir japaḥ | iti smṛtyuktaṃ jñeyam | idaṃ prāyaścittajātaṃ yāgasthastrīkṣatravaiśyeṣv ātreyyām āhitāgnipatnyāṃ garbhiṇyām avijñāte ca garbhe caturthāṃśanyūnaṃ kāryam | yad vāhorātropoṣito rātrāv upavāsaṃ kṛtvottīrya lomabhyaḥ svāhetyādyair aṣṭabhir mantraiḥ pratyekaṃ pañca pañcājyāhutīr evaṃ catvāriṃśaj juhuyāt |
iti brahmahatyādiprāyaścittam |
atha surāpāne prāyaścittam —
tatra matyā paiṣṭyāḥ sakṛt pane gauḍīmādhvyoḥ pānāvṛttau ca trirātropoṣitaḥ kūṣmāṇḍībhir ṛgbhiś catvāriṃśadghṛtāhutīr juhuyāt | trirātropavāsāśaktau māsaṃ pratyahaṃ ṣoḍaśakṛtvo ‘pa naḥ śośucad agham … | prati stomebhir uṣasaṃ vasiṣṭhāḥ … | mahitrīṇām avostu … | etonvindraṃ stavāmety eṣam anyatamaṃ japet | matyā cen mahāpāpe ‘bdaṃ pratyaham | devakṛtasyainasa ityādibhir mantrair homaḥ | nama id ugraṃ nama āvivāsety asya vā japaṃ kuryāt | mahāpāpābhyāse tu samāhito gām anugacchec cābdam | bhikṣāśī pāvamānīṃ japet | mahāpāpasamuccaye caivam |
iti surāpānaprāyaścittam |
atha suvarṇasteye prāyaścittam —
tatra brāhmaṇasuvarṇahārī jale sthita ekādaśarudrajāpī trirātropoṣitaḥ śudhyet | yad vā bhasmacchanno bhasmaśayyāśano brahmavadhāt surāpānāt suvarṇasteyād gurutalpagamanāc ca śudhyet | atyantanirguṇabrāhmaṇasuvarṇaharaṇe tv asya vāmasya palitasya hotur iti sūktaṃ sakṛj japet | yaj jāgrato dūram udaiti daivam ityādi daivaṣaḍṛcaṃ śivasaṃkalpa[146]suktaṃ japet | suvarṇanyūnaparimāṇasteye caivam | anugrāhakaprayojakānāṃ caivam |
iti suvarṇasteye prāyaścittam |
atha gurutalpe prāyaścittam —
tatrāmatyā gurupatnīṃ gatas trirātropoṣitaḥ sahasraśīrṣā puruṣa iti ṣoḍaśarcaṃ sūktaṃ catvāriṃśatsaṃkhyayā japaṃ homaṃ cāvartyānte payasvinīṃ gāṃ datvā śudhyet | surāpānasuvarṇasteye caivaṃ vā | yad vā gurutalpo haviṣyāntam ajaraṃ svarvidīti sūktaṃ na tam aṃho na duritam iti sūktaṃ vā, iti vā iti me mana iti sūktaṃ vā sahasraśīṣā puruṣa iti sūktaṃ vā pratyahaṃ vā ṣoḍaśakṛtvo japitvā māsena śudhyet | matyā ced devakṛtasyainasa ityādyair mantraiḥ śākalahomaḥ | mahāpātakāvṛttau vyāhṛtibhis tilair lakṣahomaḥ | vyabhicāriṇīgamane tu haviṣpāntaṃ vā pāvamānīr vā janāpaṃ vā vālakhilyān vā nivitpraiṣān vā vṛṣākapiṃ vā hotṝn rudrān vā sakṛj japitvā śudhyet | gurutalpātideśeṣv atipātakeṣu pādonaṃ yojyam | gurutalpasameṣu pātakeṣv ardhonaṃ sarvtraivam | mahāpātakisaṃsarge ca yasya yena saha saṃsargas tasyaiva sa prāyaścittaṃ kuryād iti gurutalpe |
iti mahāpātakaprāyaścittam |
upapātakānāṃ rahaḥ prāṇāyāmaśatam | yad vā mahāpātake prāṇāyāmacatuḥśatam | atipātake triśatam | anupātake dviśatam | upapātake śatam | sarvatrābhyāse dviguṇam | matyā triguṇam | matyābhyāse caturguṇam | prakīrṇakeṣūpapāpeṣu caturthāṃśaprāṇāyāmāḥ | yad vā prakāśa upapāpe yatra cāndratrayaṃ tatra tatsahasraprāṇāyāmāḥ | yad vā gāyatryā lakṣajapo mahāpāpe | pādonam atipāpe | ardham anupāpe | pādam upapāpe | taccaturthāṃśaṃ prakīrṇakeṣu caret | yad vā gāyatryā vyāhṛtibhir vā tilalakṣahomo mahāpāpe | atipātakādiṣu pādonādikam |
iti smṛtyarthasāre rahasyaprāyaścittam |
atha kṛcchrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇam —
tatra vratāṅgabhūtā dharmā yamā niyamāś ca | brahmacaryaṃ dayā kṣāntir dānaṃ satyam akauṭilyam ahiṃsāsteyaṃ mādhuryaṃ damaś ceti daśa yamāḥ | snānamaunopavāsejyā-svādhyāyopasthanigrahaguruśuśrūṣāśaucākrodhāpramādā iti daśa niyamāḥ | sāntapane kṛcchre pūrvedyur anāhāro gomūtragomayakṣīradadhisarpiḥkuśodakāny ekīkṛtya pītvā paredyur upavaset | eṣa dvirātraḥ sāntapanaḥ kṛcchraḥ | pūrvedyur upoṣyāparedyur imāni samantrakaṃ svayaṃ [147] saṃpūjya hutvā samantrakaṃ prāśanaṃ yadā tadā brahmakūrcasaṃjñakam | gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhiḥ sarpiḥ kuśodakaṃ pañcagavyasaṃjñakam | tatra tāmravarṇāyā gor mūtram | śvetāyā gomayam | pītāyāḥ kṣīram | nīlāyā dadhi | kṛṣṇāyāḥ sarpir grāhyam | yad vā sarvaṃ kāpilam eva | alābhe sarvavarṇānāṃ sarvaṃ grāhyam | gomūtre ‘ṣṭa māṣāḥ pramāṇam | gomaye ṣoḍaśa māṣāḥ | kṣīre dvādaśa māṣāḥ | dadhi daśa māṣāḥ | sarpiṣy aṣṭa māṣāḥ | kuśodake tadardham | gāyatryā gomūtram | gandhadvārām iti gomayam | āpyāyasveti kṣīram | dadhikrāvṇa iti dadhi | tejo ‘si śukram asīty ājyam | devasya tveti kuśodakam | etat pañcagavyaṃ saptapatrair acchinnāgraiḥ śukavarṇaiḥ kuśair irāvatīdaṃ viṣṇur mānastoke śaṃvatītyantābhir agnau hutvā hutaśeṣaṃ praṇavenāloḍya tenābhimantrya tenoddhṛtya tenaiva pibet | madhyamena palāśapatreṇa padmapatreṇa svarṇapātreṇa tāmrapātreṇa vā brahmatīrthena vā pibet | etad brahmakūrcasaṃjñakaṃ homasahitaṃ vijñeyam | strīśūdrau tu vipraiḥ kārayitvā tūṣṇīṃ pibetām | brahmakūrcatrirātrābhyāse yatisāntapanam | gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi sarpiḥ kuśodakam | ekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ pītvaikarātropavāse sāntapanakṛcchraḥ sāptāhikaḥ | eṣu śaktyādyapekṣayā yojyam | evam uttaratrāpi yojyam | mahāsāntapanaṃ tu gomūtragomayakṣīradadhisarpiṣām ekaikaṃ tryaham āvartya pañcadaśāhikaṃ syāt | yad vā gomūtragomayakṣīradadhisarpiḥkuśodakānāṃ ṣaṇṇām ekaikāhaṃ tryaham āvartya tryaham upavāsa ekaviṃśatirātrakaṃ mahāsāntapanam | yatimahāsāntapanaṃ tu gomūtrādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇām ekaikasya dvyahadvyahābhyāse syāt | taptakṛcchraś ca taptakṣīraghṛtodakanānām ekaikaṃ pratyahaṃ pītvaikarātropavāse syāt | yad voṣṇajalakṣīraghṛtodakabāṣpamārutānāṃ caturṇāṃ trir abhyāse dvādaśarātras taptakṛcchraḥ syāt | tatra jalaṃ tripalaṃ kṣīraṃ dvipalaṃ sarpiḥ palamānaṃ pibet | uṣṇodakabāṣpamānaṃ trirātrasya pūraṇamātram | pādakṛcchra ekabhaktena naktenāyācitenopavāsena caivaṃ syāt | tatra grāsasaṃkhyaikabhakte pañcadaśa ṣaḍviṃśatir vā grāsāḥ | nakte dvādaśa vā dvāviṃśatir vā | ayācite tu caturviṃśatir eva | sarve grāsāḥ kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇā āsyapraveśapramāṇā vā grāhyāḥ | anayoḥ pakṣayoḥ śaktyā vikalpaḥ | yad vaivaṃ pādakṛcchraḥ | ekabhaktatrayaṃ pādakṛcchraḥ śūdrasya deyaḥ | naktatrayaṃ pādakṛcchro vaiśyasya | ayācitatrayaṃ pādakṛcchro rājñaḥ | upavāsatrayaṃ pādakṛcchro [148] viprasyeti vyavasthā ca | ardhakṛcchras tv ayācitopavāsapādābhyāṃ syāt | pādonakṛcchra ekabhaktāyācitopavāsapādaiḥ syāt | prājāpatyakṛcchra ekabhaktanaktāyācitopavāsais trirāvṛttaiḥ prātilomyena trir āvṛttair vā bālāturāya syāt | ekabhaktatrayanaktatrayāyācitatrayopavāsatrayaiḥ prājāpatyah syāc chaktānām upavāsaḥ prātilomyena vā | atikṛcchras tu pāṇipūraṇamātrānna-bhojanayukto na grāsasaṃkhyākaḥ | yad vaikaikagrāsāśī bhojanadine syāc chaktasyaiṣo ‘tikṛcchraḥ | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | kṛcchrātikṛcchraḥ payasaikaviṃsatirātraiḥ syāt | yad vā dvādaśatrātram ambubhakṣaṇe kṛcchrātikṛcchraṃ syāc chaktasya | parāke dvādaśāhopavāso syāt | saumyakṛcchras tu piṇyākamodanāgranisrāvaṃ takram udakaṃ saktūṃś ca pañcadineṣu kramāt prāśya ṣaṣṭhe hy upavāsaḥ syāt | piṇyākādīnāṃ parimāṇaṃ prāṇayātrāmātram eva syāt | piṇyākasaktutakrair tryahaṃ caturthe ‘hany upavāso vā saumyakṛcchraḥ syāt | cāndrāyaṇaṃ tu śuklapratipadādidineṣu mayūrāṇḍapramāṇagrāsānām ekaikaṃ vṛddhyā paurṇamāsyāṃ pañcadaśa grāsāḥ syuḥ | tataḥ kṛṣṇapratipadādiṣv ekaikagrāsahrāso ‘rthād amāvāsyāyām upavāsaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ yavamadhyaṃ syāt | pipīlikāmadhyaṃ tu kṛṣṇpratipadādiprakrame paurṇamāsyantaṃ syāt | sarvatra tithivṛddhihrāse grāsavṛddhihrāsaḥ | yad vā catvāriṃśadadhikaśatadvayagrāsānāṃ yathākathaṃcid āvṛttyā māsena cāndrāyaṇaṃ syāt | yaticāndrāyaṇaṃ prayahaṃ divāṣṭāṣṭagrāsair māsena syāt | śiśucāndrāyaṇaṃ tu pratyahaṃ divā caturgrāsai rātrau caturgrāsair māsena syāt | ṛṣicāndrāyaṇaṃ tu pratyahaṃ tribhis tribhir grāsair māsenaiva syāt | yaticāndrāyaṇādiṣu yathākathaṃcit triṃśaddinamāso na cāndragativat | somāyanaṃ tu gokṣīraṃ stanacatuṣṭayajaṃ saptarātraṃ pibet | stanatrayāt saptarātram | stanadvayāt saptarātram | stanaikena ṣaḍrātram | trirātraṃ vāyubhakṣitā cāndrāyaṇadharmatā ca |
iti kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādilakṣaṇam |
atha cāndrāyaṇasādhāraṇadharmāḥ —
trikālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā snānam | taptakṛcchre sakṛt snānam | sarvatra snāne mārjanādy aghamarṣaṇādi kṛtvottīrya sauramantreṇa sāvitryā vā sūryopasthānam | tataḥ puṇyamantraṃ gāyatrīṃ cāṣṭasahasraṃ śataṃ [149] vā śaktyā japet | caturbhikṣāyāvakapayo-dadhighṛtamūlaphalakandādihaviṣyānnam ucyate | taddhaviṣyam āpyāyasvety abhimantrya grāsān gāyatryābhimantrayet |
atha pratyāmnāyo vakṣyate —
prājāpatye dhenupradānaṃ tanmūlyadānaṃ godānaṃ vā tanmūlyadānaṃ vā | tac ca gomūlyaṃ niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhaṃ niṣkapādaṃ vā dadyāt | atyaśaktau tadardhaṃ tatsamabhūdhanadhānya-vastrādīn vā dadyād dvādaśabrāhmaṇabhojanaṃ vā | tāvadbhūdhanadhānyavastrādidānaṃ vā saṃhitāpārāyaṇaṃ vā gāyatryayutajapo vā | tilahomasahasraṃ vā | prāṇāyāmaśatadvayaṃ vā śaktyā kuryāt | dhenubhojanādyaśaktyapekṣayā dvitricatuḥpañcaṣaḍguṇaṃ vardhate | upavāsasthāna ekaviprabhojanaṃ tāvaddhanadhānyavastrādidānaṃ vā | śaktyā dviguṇādivṛddhiś ca | gāyatrīsahasrajapo vā dvādaśaprāṇāyāmā vā śaktyā kāryāḥ | cāndrāyaṇasthāne 'ṣṭau prājāpatyāḥ | taptakṛcchre ṣaṭ | atikṛcchre trayaḥ kāryāḥ | sukhasādhyeṣu tu cāndrāyaṇe trayaḥ kāryāḥ | taptakṛcchre sārdhakāyadvayam | atikṛcchre mahāsāntapane ca kāyadvayam | parāke pañca trayo vā kāyāḥ kāryāḥ |
atha kṛcchrasthāne tīrthapratyāmnāyo vakṣyate —
vede tīrthe ca deve ca yajñe caivauṣadhe gurau |
yādṛśīṃ bhāvanāṃ kuryāt siddhir bhavati tādṛśī ||
kṣetrāgnyāpaḥ surā viprā guar gurus tīrtham ucyate |
nivāsahomasnānārcāroṣapoṣaiḥ punāti tat ||
atha purāṇebhyaḥ sarvatīrthapratyāmnāye kiṃcin mūlam ucyate |
brahmaviṣṇuśivā devā indro ‘gnir yamanirṛtī |
varuṇaḥ śvasano yakṣaḥ somaḥ guho gaṇāḥ ||
ete svayaṃ tīrthabhūtās tīrthaiḥ siddhim avāpnuvan |
manavo vasavo rudrā ādityā maruto ‘nilāḥ ||
viśve devas tuṣāḥ sādhyā nāgā yakṣāś ca rākṣasāḥ |
ityādyanekadevāś ca tīrthaiḥ siddhim avāpnuvan ||
bhṛgavo ‘ṅgiraso ‘gastyaviśvāmitrātrikaśyapāḥ |
vasiṣṭhādyā mahātmānas tīrthaiḥ sāmarthyam āpnuvan ||
[150]        hariścandro nalo vainyaḥ purukutsaḥ purūravāḥ |
sagaraḥ kārtavīryādyās tīrthai rājyam avāpnuvan ||
indrabhairavarāmādyā gautamādyā maharṣayaḥ |
brahmahatyādipāpāni tīrthair eva vyapohayan ||
viśvāmitraḥ saputras tu mahāpāpaṃ vyapohayat |
sutīrthatapasā brāhmyaṃ prāpya vāsiṣṭhaśāpataḥ ||
triśaṅkum api caṇḍālaṃ vipraṃ kṛtvāpy ayājayat |
taṃ tu tenaiva dehena svargalokam aveśayat ||
anupoṣya trirātrādi tīrthāny anupagamya ca |
adatvā kāñcanaṃ gāś ca daridro nāma jāyate ||
vratopavāsakṛcchrādīn akṛtvā tīrthasevanam |
adatvānnāśvavastrādi daridro jāyate dhruvam ||
tathā devīpurāṇe |
pāṇḍavā rājyalobhāya duritopaśamāya ca |
śrīkṛṣṇanāradavyāsaśrīkaṇṭhendrājalomaśaiḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapulastyājasaptarṣipramukhais tathā |
dvādaśadvādaśābdāni kṛcchrāṇy ādāya bhaktitaḥ ||
tīrthair akurvann ityādi purāṇe śrūyate kathā |
viśvanāthena yatinā nirbījena mahātmanā ||
viṃśatyabdāc chatābdāntaṃ sarvadā tīrthasevanāt |
bahusmṛtipurāṇajñair bahubhiḥ sahacāriṇā ||
kṛtsnaṃ tirthaphalaṃ proktaṃ yan na sādhyam apīryate |
mārkaṇḍeyājahanumajjāmbabadromaśādibhiḥ ||
kṛtsnaṃ tīrthaphalaṃ proktaṃ godānakratusāmyataḥ |
dvāparānte purāṇāni vyāsaḥ svalpāyuṣāṃ nṛṇām ||
himāntād dakṣiṇe deśe saṃkocya vyavahārayet |
bhūtapretapiśācāś ca pitaro brahmarākṣaśāḥ ||
adyāpi pravadanty evaṃ kṛcchrārthe tīrtham arthavat |
sūtaḥ śūdraḥ purāṇajñas tīrthair vipro ‘bhavat tataḥ ||
śiṣyān ūce ‘kṣarāṅkāni tīrthāni kuruteti vai ||
te viśvakarmaṇe procur viśvakarmā ca sarvavit |
śiṣyaiḥ kṛcchrākṣarāṅgāni sarvatīrtheṣv akārayat ||
[151]        adyāpi kṛcchravarṇāṅkā dṛśyante tatra tatra ca |
lipyaṅkānām avijñāne prakārāntam ucyate ||
purāṇasmṛtivid viṣnuproktatīrthaphalāptaye |
uktaṃ lokopakārārthaṃ yatrāsādyam apīryate ||
purāṇasmṛtiṣu tīrthakalpeṣu ca tīrthaphalaṃ godānasamaṃ daśadhenusamaṃ śatadhenausamaṃ gosahasraphalam agnihotraphalaṃ puṇyeṣṭiphalaṃ yajñaphalam aśvamedhaphalam upapātakanāśanaṃ pātakanāśanaṃ mahāpātakanāśanam upavāsaphalaṃ trirātraphalaṃ kṛcchraphalaṃ cāndrāyaṇaphalaṃ pakṣopavāsaphalaṃ māsopavāsaphalaṃ ṣanmāsopavāsaphalaṃ saṃvatsaropavāsaphalaṃ labed ityādivacaneṣv arthavādapraśaṃsāṃ visarjya sarvatra yogyatayā kṛcchrāṇi parikalpya tīrthaphalāny uktāni | tīrthasaṃgrahakārair api tathaivoktāni | kāyakṛcchrācaraṇāśaktānāṃ pātityaparihārārtham asmābhis tathaivocyante | tatra bhāgīrathyāṃ snānaṃ ṣaṣṭiyojanagatānāṃ ṣaḍabdakṛcchrasamam | tatra yātrā yojanavṛddhau | yojanasyārdhakṛcchrā vṛddhiḥ | pūrvaṃ saptamātṛkā rākṣasabhayānnadyo ‘bhūvan | bhaye gate punar devyo abhūvan | tato nadīśuṣkakhāteṣu bhāgīrathī saptavarṇā saptadhāvahat | tāsu viṣṇugaṅgādisaptamātṛkāṇāṃ sakṛt saptagaṅgāsu snānaṃ pādakṛcchrasamam | prayāge dviguṇam | gaṅgādvāre gaṅgāsāgarasaṃgame caivam | vārāṇasyāṃ gaṅgātīva durlabhā | samudrānte puṇyaṃ na gaṇyate | vārāṇasyāṃ mahāpātakaṃ na praviśati | tatra mṛto mukta eva | sarvayātrā deśāntarabhāṣābhedaviṣaye mahāparvatavyavadhāne mahānadīvyavadhāne ṣaṭcaturdviyojananyūnā bhavati | yamunāyāṃ snāne tu dvādaśakṛcchrasamaṃ viṃśatiyojanagatasya | mathurāyāṃ dviguṇam | sarasvatyāṃ caturabdakṛcchrasamaṃ catvāriṃśadyojanagatasya | prabhāse dvāravatyāṃ ca dviguṇam | etayor nadyor yojanavṛddhau pādakṛcchravṛddhiḥ | dṛṣadvatīśatadruvipāśāvitastāśarāvatīmaruvṛddhāsiknīmadhamatī-payasvatīghṛatavatyādidevanadīṣu snānaṃ triṃśatkṛcchrasamaṃ pañcadaśayojanagatasya | candrabhāgāvetravatīśarayūgomatīdevikākauśikīnityajalāmandākinīsahasravaktrāpaunaḥpunyā-pūrṇapuṇyābāhudāvāruṇīgaṇḍakyādidevanadīṣu snānaṃ ṣoḍaśakṛcchrasamaṃ dvādaśayojanagatasya | etāsu parasparasaṃgame trinadīphalam | anyāsu samudragāsu mahānadīṣu ṣaṭkṛcchraphalam | mahānadeṣu mahānadyardhaphalam | śoṇamahā[152]nade gaṅgārdhaphalam | nadeṣu nadyardhaphalam | vairocananadeṣu mahānadyardhaphalam | puṣkaratīrtheṣu prayāgasamam | saṃnihatyāṃ tathaiva | māhiṣmatyādau nityapratyakṣāgnau homo daśaguṇaḥ | gayāṃ mahānadīṃ seturāmeśvaraṃ someśvaraṃ bhīmeśvaraṃ śriraṅgaṃ padmanābhaṃ puruṣottamaṃ naimiṣaṃ badaryāśramaṃ puṇyāraṇyaṃ dharmāraṇyaṃ kurukṣetraṃ śrīśailaṃ mahālayaṃ kedāraṃ puṣkaraṃ rudrakoṭiṃ narmadām āmrātakeśvaraṃ kubjāmraṃ kokāmukhaṃ prabhāsaṃ vijayeśaṃ purīndraṃ pañcanadaṃ gokarṇaṃ śaṅkukarṇaṃ bhadrakarṇam ayodhyāṃ mathurāṃ dvāravatīṃ māyām avantīṃ gayāṃ kāñcīṃ śālagrāmaṃ śaṃbhalagrāmaṃ kambalagrāmam evamādimuktikṣetrāṇi saṃsevya labhate gayāsnānam | sarvebhyo vārāṇasī viśiṣṭaiva | mahāprayāge mṛtasyāpi muktir eva | anyaprayāge maraṇaṃ muktibījam | narmadāyāṃ caturviṃśatiyojanagatasya caturviṃśatikṛcchrasamam | kubjikāsaṃgame dviguṇam | muktitīrthe caturguṇam | tāpyāṃ daśakṛcchrasamaṃ daśayojanagatasya | payoṣṇyām aṣṭayojanagatasyāṣṭakṛcchrasamam | tatra saṃgame dviguṇam | godāvaryāṃ ṣaṣṭiyojanagatasya tryabdaṃ kṛcchrasamam | tatra triṃśadyojana-gatasyaikādaśatīrtheṣu pratilomānulomasnānaṃ ṣaṣṭikṛcchrasamam | vañjarāsaṃgamaprayāge taddviguṇam | saptagodāvaryāṃ bhīmeśvare triguṇam | kuśatarpaṇe gayāsamam | vañjarāyāṃ dvādaśayojanagatasya dvādaśakṛcchrasamam | godāvaryāṃ viśleṣe samudrāntaṃ ṣaḍguṇam | praṇītāyāṃ catuḥkṛcchrasamaṃ caturyojane | pūrṇāyāṃ tadardhaṃ tadardhayojane | kṛṣṇāveṇyāyāṃ pañcadaśayojane pañcadaśakṛcchrasamam | tuṅgabhadrāyāṃ viṃśatiyojanagatasya viṃśatikṛcchrasamam | paṃpāyāṃ taddviguṇam | hārihare triguṇam | bhīmarathyāṃ daśakṛcchrasamaṃ daśayojanagatasya | kakudmatīsaṃge pañcadaśakṛcchrasamam | tuṅgabhadrāvaradāsaṃgame pañcaviṃśatikṛcchrasamam | malāpahāriṇyām aṣṭakṛcchrasamam aṣṭayojanagatasya | nivṛtyāṃ ṣaṭkṛcchrasamaṃ ṣaḍyojanagatasya | godāvaryāṃ yātrāvṛddhau yojane pādakṛcchraḥ | siṃhasthe gurau sarvatra jāhnavīsamam | kanyāsthe gurau kṛṣṇāveṇyāṃ sarvatra jāhnavyardhaṃ ca grāhyam | tuṅgabhadrāyāṃ tulāsthe gurau jāhnavyardham | karkaṭe gurau ca kṛṣṇāveṇyāyāṃ malahārisaṃgame prayāge triṃśadyojanagatasya triṃśatkṛcchrasamam | bhāgīrathīsaṃgame prayāge dviguṇam | tuṅgabhadrāsaṃgame prayāge triguṇam | nivṛttisaṃgame prayāge caturguṇam | brahmeśvare pañcaguṇam | pātālagaṅgāyāṃ mallikārjunadarśane ṣaḍguṇam | tataḥ pūrvaṃ ṣaṣṭikṛcchrasamam | liṅgālaye dviguṇam | samudrasaṃgame [153] caivam | kāveryāṃ pratīcīmahānadyāṃ pañcadaśakṛcchraphalaṃ pañcadaśayojanagatasya | tāmraparṇīkṛtamālāpayasvinīṣu dvādaśayojanair dvādaśakṛcchrasamam | sahyapādodbhavā vedādripādobhavā nadyaḥ svadairdhyānusāreṇaika-dvitrikṛcchraphaladāḥ | vindhyaśrīśailodbhavā dviguṇāḥ | himodbhavās triguṇāḥ | smṛtau purāṇe ca yathākathaṃcidanuktau kulyās trirātraphaladāḥ | alpanadyaḥ kṛcchraphaladāḥ | sarvatra yātrānuktau kṛcchrasaṃkhyayā yojanasaṃkhyā syāt | ekayojanagādiṣaḍyojanagatāḥ saritaḥ kulyāḥ | tato dvādaśayojanagatā alpanadyaḥ | tataś caturviṃśatiyojanagatā nadyaḥ | samudragāś ca mahānadyaḥ | mahānadīsamākhyāśritāś ca mahānadyas tatropavāsasahitaṃ nadīsnānaṃ kṛcchrasamam | yojanād arvāg api sūnīgardabhīśūnīcāṇḍālīkaṣṭagādinadyaḥ pāpanadyaś ca varjyāḥ | sarvatra samudrasnānaṃ darśe kāryam | daivatasamīpe saraḥsarinnadīsaṃgameṣu sadā ca kāryam | samudrasnānaṃ pañcadaśakṛcchrasamaṃ pañcadaśayojanagatasya | prakhyātadevatāsamīpe taddviguṇam | tatra snātvā taddevadarśane sthāṇudarśane ca triguṇam | setau triṃśatkṛcchrasamaṃ triṃśadyojanagatasya | snātvā rāmeśvaradarśane ṣaṣṭikṛcchrasamaṃ vindhyadeśīyānām | seturāmeśvare jāhnavyāṃ ca triguṇaṃ phalam | jāhnavīdedārayos tathaiva | dakṣiṇābdhideśīyānāṃ jāhnavyāṃ ṣaḍguṇam | gaṅgādeśīyānāṃ tu seturāmeśvare ṣaḍguṇam | skandadarśane triṃśatkṛcchrasamaṃ triṃśadyojanagatasya ca | yatra gaṅgāsaṃjñāsti tatra caivam | sarvatra bhāṣābhedaparvatādinā yātrāhrāso bhavaty eva | śrīraṅgapadmanābha-puruṣottamacakrakoṭamahālakṣmīdarśane lavaṇārṇavasnāne triṃśadyojanagatasya triṃśatkṛcchrasamam | kedāre triguṇam | sarvavaiṣṇavamāheśvarasauraśākteyajyeṣṭhādipīṭha-darśane pañcadaśakṛcchrasamam | prakhyāte dviguṇam | ahobale’pi tathaiva | śrīśailapradakṣiṇaṃ ṣaṣṭikṛcchrasamam | śrīśaila ekaikadvāradarśane dvādaśakṛcchrasamam | anyeṣu prakhyātatīrthadevatādarśaneṣu ṣaṭkṛcchraphalam | siddhakṣetre svayaṃbhudarśanam anyakṣetre ca trikṛcchrasamaṃ triyojanagatasya | sarvatra kṛcchrasaṃkhyayā trayodaśayojana-saṃkhyā vijñeyā | yatra yatra yadviśeṣatayā dṛṣṭaṃ tac ca grāhyam | sarvatra deśakālaviśeṣeṇa phalaviśeṣo ‘sty eva | sarvatroktadaśāṃśaphalaṃ tīrasthasya yojanād arvāg bhavati tatrāpi krośasaṃkhyayā tāratamyam asti | sarvatra yātrāvṛddhau yātrāphalam | [154] yojane yojana upavāsaḥ | tathā sati ṣaḍ upavāsāḥ prājāpatya iti kalpanā bhavati |
tiryag yavodarāṇy aṣṭāv ūrdhvā vā vrīhayas traya |
pramāṇamaṅgalasyoktaṃ vitastir dvādaśāṅgulā ||
vitastidviguṇāratnis tataḥ kiṣkus tato dhanuḥ |
dhanuḥsahasre dve krośaiṃś catuṣkrośaṃ tu yojanam ||
sārdhagavyūtideśaṃ ca yojanaṃ paricakṣate |
gavyūtiṃ pañcasāhasradhanurbhiḥ pramite viduḥ ||
yātrāsu niyamāḥ | ekabhaktādhaḥśayane anṛtau brahmacaryam ityādyāḥ | tīrthe snānajapa-tarpaṇādi kāryam | daivate ‘rghyapādyākṣatagandhārcanādi kāryam ityādi tīrthapratyāmnāyo yojyaḥ | evaṃ sthite yathā manaḥ pūtaṃ bhavati tathā kāryam | parārthe tu gantā ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ labhate | prasaṅgena gantārdhaphalam | anuṣaṅgeṇa tīrthaṃ prāpya snāne snānaphalam eva na yātrāphalam | pitṛpitṛvyapitāmahaprapitāmahamātulaśvaśurapoṣakārthadagurv-ācāryopādhyāyārthaṃ teṣāṃ patnyarthaṃ mātṛṣvasṛpitṛṣvasrarthaṃ ca snātvā svayam aṣṭamāṃśaṃ labhate | sākṣāt pitroḥ kurvan putraś caturthāṃśaṃ labhate |
dampatī ca sapatnyaś ca labhante 'rdhaṃ mithaḥ phalam |
arthināṃ tu phalahrāsaḥ śuśrūṣāphalam īdṛśam ||
karkaṭādau māsadvaye nirantaraṃ rajasvalā mahānadyaḥ samudragāḥ | tāsv api bhāgīrathi gomatī candrabhāgā sarasvatī sindhumahānadī śarayūś ca trirātraṃ rajasvalāḥ |
śuṣyanti yāḥ kusarito grīṣmakāle na codakaiḥ |
pūryante caiva varṣāsu daśāhānte rajasvalāḥ ||
kulyā alpanadyo nadyaś ca saptāhaṃ pakṣaṃ māsaṃ tā rajasvalāḥ | vāpīkūpataḍāgādiṣu svalpodakeṣu purāṇodakavarjiteṣu trirātraṃ rajasvalātvam | atas tatra snānatarpaṇādikam apraśastatvān na kāryam | tīravāsināṃ tu na niṣedhaḥ | sarasvatyanyagaṅgā yamunā ca sarve ca nadāḥ kadācid api na rajavalāḥ | smṛtipurāṇeṣu yatra gaṅgāyamunāsarasvatīśabdo ‘sti tatrāpi rajodoṣo [155] nāstīty āhuḥ | atīravāsinām api upākarmaṇi cotsarjane vratasnāne candrasūryagrahe ca rajodoṣo nāsti | kṣudranadyaḥ sarvā na rajasvalāḥ |
iti tīrthavidhiḥ |
atha mahāpātakeṣu dvādaśavārṣikādivratasthāneṣu prājāpatyādayas tatpratyāmnāyāś cocyante —
tatra dvādaśavārṣike dvādaśadineṣv ekaikaṃ prājāpatyaṃ parikalpyaivaṃ kalpyamāne ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatriṃśatprājāpatyā dvādaśavārṣikeṣu bhavanti | tasmān mahāpātake tv amatyā sakṛtkaraṇe ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayaṃ kāyāṃś caret | tadaśaktau ṣaṣṭyadhikatriṃśaddhenūr dadyāt tanmūlyaṃ vā | gomūlyatvena ṣaṣṭyadhikatriṃśanniṣkān vā niṣkārdhāni vā niṣkapādān vā śaktyā dadyāt | mūlyadāne ‘py aśaktau tāvanta udavāsāḥ kāryāḥ | tatrāpy aśaktāv ekaikasya prājāpatyasyāyutagāyatrījapasaṃkhyayā ṣaṭtriṃśallakṣasaṃkhyāko gāyatrījapaḥ kāryaḥ | ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayavedapārāyaṇāni vā kāryāṇi | tāvanti tilahomasahasrāṇi vā | tāvantaḥ śatadvayaprāṇāyāmā vā | ityādikṛcchrapratyāmnāyatvena ṣaṣṭyadhita[ka?]śatatrayasaṃkhyayā mahāpātakeṣu yojyam | atipātakeṣu pādanyūnadvādaśābdanavavārṣikavratasthāne saptatyadhikaśatadvaye kāyāḥ kāryāḥ | tadaśaktau dhenudānādi kāryam | anupātakeṣu ṣaḍvārṣikavratasthāne sāśītiśatakāyāḥ | dhenvādayaś ca tatsaṃkhyākāḥ | upapātakabahutve traivārṣikaṃ vrataṃ kāryam | tatsthāne trivārṣike navatisaṃkhyākāḥ kāyāḥ kāryāḥ | dhenvādayaś ca tatsaṃkhyākāḥ | ekaikopapātakeṣu traimāsikavratam uktam | tatsthāne sārdhasapta kāyāḥ pratyāmnāyaś ca | dhenvādayas tatsaṃkhyākāḥ | vṛṣabhaikādaśagodānasahitatrirātrātmakā govadhavrate tu sārdhaikādaśa kāyā upavāsādayaś ca tatsaṃkhyākāḥ | māsapayovrate sārdhakāyadvayaṃ pratyāmnāyaś ca tadvat | pañcagavyamāse caivam | parāke tu kāyatrayaṃ pratyāmnāyaś ca tadvat | saptāhaṃ sāntapane tv ardhakāyā ityādi | prakīrṇakeṣu prājāpatyaṃ pādādikḷptyā kalpyam | prājāpatyaḥ ṣaḍ upavāsā iti vā śaktyā kalpyam |
atha sarvaprāyaścittāni vakṣyante —
tatra mahāpātakād arvācīnapāpeṣu bahuṣu vividheṣv ajñātādiṣv atyantaguṇavān atyantaviraktaḥ pratinimittaṃ kartum aśaktau prāyaścittaṃ ṣaḍabdakṛcchraṃ sāśītiśatasaṃkhyākaṃ prājāpatyaṃ caret | dhenvādipratyāmnāyaṃ vā tatsaṃkhyayā kuryāt | abhyāse dviguṇaṃ matyā triguṇaṃ matyā [156] abhyāse caturguṇam | atyantābhyāse nirantarābhyāse vā pañcaguṇam | bahukālābhyāse ṣaḍguṇam | prakīrṇakeṣu kṣudrapāpeṣu cātibahuṣv amatyā kṛteṣu pratinimittaṃ kartuam aśaktau prāyaścittam abdakṛcchraṃ triṃśatsaṃkhyākaprājāpatyaṃ caret | dhenvādipratyāmnāyaṃ vā tatsaṃkhyayā kuryāt | abhyase dviguṇaṃ matyā triguṇam | matyābhyāse caturguṇam | atyantābhyāse nirantarābhyāse vā pañcaguṇam | tathā bahukālābhyāse ṣaḍguṇam | kṣudrapāpeṣv ajñātakṛteṣu pratinimittaṃ prāyaścittāśaktau kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇāni kuryāt | tatsthāne dvādaśa prājāpatyān vā caret | dhenvādipratyāmnāyaṃ vā kṛcchrasthāne tatsaṃkhyayā kuryāt | abhyāse dviguṇaṃ matyā triguṇaṃ matyābhyāse caturguṇam | atyantābhyāse nirantarābhyāse ca pañcaguṇam | tathā bahukālābhyāse śaḍguṇam | idaṃ prāyaścittacatuṣṭayam uttamasya | madhyamasya dviguṇam | jaghanyasya triguṇam | iṣṭāpūrtaśubhāśubhamahākarmasv anupahatānām api jñartvigācāryayajamānādīnāṃ kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇākhyaṃ sarvaprāyaścittaṃ bhavet | tatsthāne dvādaśa prājāpatyān vā tadardhaṃ vā vadanti |
iti smṛtyarthasāre sarvaprāyaścittavidhiḥ |
sarvatrānuktaniṣkṛtau kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyaṇāni samastavyastarūpeṇa yogyatayā yojyāni | kṣudeṣūpapāpeṣūpavāsas trirātrakāyā vā yogyatayā yojyāḥ | atikṣudreṣu dvādaśa ṣaḍ vā prāṇāyāmā yogyatayā yojyāḥ | strīśūdrāṇām amantrakāḥ prāṇāyāmāḥ | puruṣāhārahantakārāgradānāni vā yojyāni tāvad dhanadhānyādidānaṃ vā kāryam | home bhūr bhuvaḥ svaḥ svāhety āhutiḥ kāryā | karmaṇy aṅgalope bhūr bhuvaḥ svar iti japaḥ 108 | vāgyamalope viṣṇusmaraṇaṃ śivasmaraṇaṃ vā | ajñātanyūnasaṃpūrṇatārtham acyutasmaraṇaṃ śaṃbhusmaraṇaṃ vā kāryam |
yasya smṛtyā ca nāmoktyā tapoyajñakriyādiṣu |
nyūnaṃ saṃpūrṇatāṃ yāti sadyo vande tam acyutam ||
ajñānād atha vā lobhāt pracyavetādhvareṣu yat |
smaraṇād eva tad viṣṇoḥ saṃpūrṇaṃ syād iti śrutiḥ ||
iti padmapurāṇavacanam |
iti smṛtyarthasāre sarvaprāyaścittavidhiḥ samāptaḥ |
niṣkaparimāṇam | niṣkaś ca lokaśāstrayor ekavākyatayā māṣāḥ, catvāriṃśat 40 | tadardhaṃ 20 tadardhaṃ 10 suvarṇaṃ tadaśaktau rajataṃ vā | aṣṭaprājāptyair ekaṃ cāndrāyaṇaṃ ṣadbhis tapta(bhir vā) kṛcchraḥ | tribhir atikṛcchraḥ | evam abdavrate triṃśatsaṃkhyākāḥ prājāpatyāḥ |
iti viśvāmitramahāmaheśvaranāgabhartṛviṣṇubhaṭṭopādhyāyasūnuna
yajvanā śrīdharācāryeṇa śrutismṛtividā kṛte
smṛtyarthasāre prāyāścittaprakaraṇaṃ samāptam |
[1] The section within brackets is omitted in the edition. This must be an error, because words in this section are commented on later by the author.
